================================================================================
FILE: AdvancedKeyboardMode.py
================================================================================

import curses
import threading
import time
import logging
import sys
from typing import Optional

# Core-Projekt-Importe
import Driving
import InfraredSensor
import UltrasonicSensor
import logging_setup
from Driving import DrivingMode, SensorCarMode, StopReason
from SensorCar import SensorCar
from ConfigReader import ConfigReader

class KeyboardMode(SensorCarMode):
    SPEED_FORWARD = 50
    SPEED_BACKWARD = -50
    ANGLE_STRAIGHT = 90
    ANGLE_LEFT = 45
    ANGLE_RIGHT = 135

    def __init__(self, car):
        super().__init__("AdvancedKeyboardMode")
        self._car = car
        self._stop_event = threading.Event()
        self.__log = logging.getLogger(self.__class__.__name__)
        
        self._autonomous_instance = None
        
        # Interne Variablen für die Stillstands-PID-Prognose
        self._last_error = 0.0
        self._integral = 0.0
        self._last_time = time.time()

    def _run(self) -> bool:
        return False
    
    def _run_condition(self) -> bool:
        return True

    def start(self):
        self._stop_event.clear()
        curses.wrapper(self._main_loop)

    def stop(self, reason:StopReason|int=0) -> bool:
        self._stoppe_autonomen_modus()
        return super().stop(reason)

    def _stoppe_autonomen_modus(self):
        if self._autonomous_instance is not None:
            self._autonomous_instance.stop()
            self._autonomous_instance = None
            self._car.drive(0, self.ANGLE_STRAIGHT)

    def _berechne_prognose_lw(self, ir_display) -> tuple[float, float, float, float, int]:
        """
        Berechnet den virtuellen PID-Stellwert u und den daraus resultierenden
        Lenkwinkel, ohne den echten Servo des Autos anzusteuern.
        """
        # 1. Config laden (Exakt wie im sensorcar_controller / FollowLine)
        self._car._cfg._load_config_file()
        if self._autonomous_instance is not None:
            self._autonomous_instance._cfg._load_config_file() 
        
        sc_cfg = ConfigReader("sensorcar_controller")
        kp = sc_cfg.get_float("korrektur_proportional", 25.0)
        ki = sc_cfg.get_float("korrektur_integral", 0.2)
        kd = sc_cfg.get_float("korrektur_differential", 10.0)
        i_bound = sc_cfg.get_float("korrektur_integral_boundary", 30.0)

        # 2. Gewichtete Abweichung (Error) berechnen (Beispiel für 5 Sensoren)
        # Wenn deine FollowLine-Klasse eine andere Formel nutzt, hier anpassen!
        # Typischer Ansatz: S1=-2, S2=-1, S3=0, S4=1, S5=2 gewichtet mit Helligkeit
        if len(ir_display) == 5:
            # Gewichtung: Links negativ, Rechts positiv
            weights = sc_cfg.get_list("ir_sensor_weights", [2.0, 1.0, 0.0, -1.0, -2.0])

            # Wir invertieren den Wert (1.0 = Weiß = Keine Linie, 0.0 = Schwarz = Linie)
            # Damit zieht die Linie den Fehler an
            inverted_sensors = [1.0 - v for v in ir_display]
            total_brightness = sum(inverted_sensors)
            
            if total_brightness > 0.1:
                error = sum(s * w for s, w in zip(inverted_sensors, weights)) / total_brightness
            else:
                error = 0.0
        else:
            error = 0.0

        # 3. Zeit-Differenz (dt) ermitteln
        now = time.time()
        dt = now - self._last_time
        if dt <= 0: dt = 0.01
        self._last_time = now

        # 4. PID-Glieder berechnen
        p_term = kp * error
        
        self._integral += error * dt
        # Begrenzung des I-Glieds (Anti-Windup)
        self._integral = max(-i_bound, min(i_bound, self._integral))
        i_term = ki * self._integral
        
        derivative = (error - self._last_error) / dt
        d_term = kd * derivative
        self._last_error = error

        # Stellgröße u berechnen
        u = p_term + i_term + d_term

        # 5. Prognostizierten Lenkwinkel berechnen (Basis 90° geradeaus)
        # u positiv -> schlägt nach rechts aus, u negativ -> nach links
        vorausgesagter_lw = int(90 + u)
        vorausgesagter_lw = max(self.ANGLE_LEFT, min(self.ANGLE_RIGHT, vorausgesagter_lw))

        return p_term, i_term, d_term, u, vorausgesagter_lw

    def _draw_stats(self, stdscr):
        """Zeigt die Echtzeitdaten und die PID-Stillstands-Prognose an."""
        speed = getattr(self._car, "speed", 0)
        angle = getattr(self._car, "steering_angle", 90)
        distance = getattr(self._car, "distance", 300)
        
        try:
            is_on_line = "JA 🛣️" if self._car.is_on_line() else "NEIN"
        except Exception:
            is_on_line = "UNKNOWN"

        ir_values = getattr(self._car, "ir_sensor_values", [[1.,1.,1.,1.,1.]])
        if isinstance(ir_values, list) and len(ir_values) > 0 and isinstance(ir_values[0], list):
            ir_display = ir_values[-1]
        else:
            ir_display = ir_values

        s_min = min(ir_display) if ir_display else 0.0
        s_max = max(ir_display) if ir_display else 0.0
        s_delta = s_max - s_min
        ir_str = " ".join([f"S{i+1}:[{v:.2f}]" for i, v in enumerate(ir_display)])

        # UNSE RE NEUE PROGNOSE IM STILLSTAND BERECHNEN:
        p_p, p_i, p_d, p_u, p_lw = self._berechne_prognose_lw(ir_display)

        cfg_pid = ConfigReader("sensorcar_controller")
        car_pid = ConfigReader("car_controller")

        # Curses Render-Bereich ab Zeile 7
        stdscr.move(7, 0)
        stdscr.clrtoeol()
        stdscr.addstr(7, 0, "─" * 76)
        
        stdscr.move(8, 0)
        stdscr.clrtoeol()
        stdscr.addstr(8, 0, f"📊 REAL-STATS -> Tempo: {speed:3d}% | Lenkung: {angle:3d}° | Auf Linie: {is_on_line}")
        
        stdscr.move(9, 0)
        stdscr.clrtoeol()
        stdscr.addstr(9, 0, f"📏 US-Distanz: {distance:3d} cm")
        
        stdscr.move(10, 0)
        stdscr.clrtoeol()
        stdscr.addstr(10, 0, f"📷 IR-Sensoren: {ir_str}")
        
        stdscr.move(11, 0)
        stdscr.clrtoeol()
        stdscr.addstr(11, 0, f"📈 IR-Stats:    Min: [{s_min:.2f}] | Max: [{s_max:.2f}] | Delta: [{s_delta:.2f}]")
        
        # HIER WIRD DIE PROGNOSE ANGEZEIGT (Zeile 13 & 14)
        stdscr.move(13, 0)
        stdscr.clrtoeol()
        stdscr.addstr(13, 0, f"🔮 PID-PROGNOSE -> P:{p_p:5.1f} | I:{p_i:5.1f} | D:{p_d:5.1f} | u (Gesamt): {p_u:6.1f}", curses.A_BOLD)
        stdscr.move(14, 0)
        stdscr.clrtoeol()
        stdscr.addstr(14, 0, f"🔮 Virtueller Lenkwinkel (lw): {p_lw:3d}°  (Soll-Wert bei Autopilot)", curses.A_BOLD)

        stdscr.move(16, 0)
        stdscr.clrtoeol()
        stdscr.addstr(16, 0, f"⚙️  Active Config -> Target-P:{cfg_pid.get_int('korrektur_proportional', 25)} D:{cfg_pid.get_int('korrektur_differential', 10)} | Sonic-V_max: {car_pid.get_int('v_max', 60)}%")

        stdscr.move(17, 0)
        stdscr.clrtoeol()
        stdscr.addstr(17, 0, "─" * 76)
        stdscr.refresh()

    def _main_loop(self, stdscr):
        stdscr.nodelay(True)  
        stdscr.keypad(True)   
        curses.curs_set(0)    

        last_speed = 0
        last_angle = self.ANGLE_STRAIGHT
        step_time = 0.05

        while not self._stop_event.is_set():
            try:
                auto_aktiv = self._autonomous_instance is not None
                status_text = "🤖 AUTOPILOT (FOLLOW LINE) AKTIV" if auto_aktiv else "✋ MANUELLE STEUERUNG"
                status_attr = curses.A_REVERSE if auto_aktiv else curses.A_NORMAL

                stdscr.move(0, 0)
                stdscr.addstr(0, 0, f"⌨️  KOMMANDOZENTRALE (KEYBOARD MODE)  |  [{status_text}]", curses.A_UNDERLINE | curses.A_BOLD | status_attr)
                stdscr.addstr(1, 0, "Steuerung:  [Pfeiltasten] Manuell fahren  |  [LEERTASTE] Not-Stopp")
                stdscr.addstr(2, 0, "Autopilot:  [A] Autopiloten starten       |  [M] Autopiloten beenden")
                stdscr.addstr(3, 0, "Programm:   Beenden mit [ESC]")
                
                self._draw_stats(stdscr)
                key = stdscr.getch()

                if key != -1:
                    speed = last_speed
                    angle = last_angle
                    action_text = "Keine Aktion"
                    valid_key = False

                    if key == ord('a') or key == ord('A'):
                        if not auto_aktiv:
                            self._stoppe_autonomen_modus()
                            self._autonomous_instance = Driving.FollowLine(car=self._car)
                            self._autonomous_instance.start()
                            action_text = "🤖 AUTOPILOT GESTARTET"
                            stdscr.move(5, 0)
                            stdscr.clrtoeol()
                            stdscr.addstr(5, 0, f"Letzte Aktion: {action_text}")
                            stdscr.refresh()
                        continue

                    elif key == ord('m') or key == ord('M'):
                        if auto_aktiv:
                            self._stoppe_autonomen_modus()
                            action_text = "✋ AUTOPILOT GESTOPPT"
                            last_speed, last_angle = 0, self.ANGLE_STRAIGHT
                            stdscr.move(5, 0)
                            stdscr.clrtoeol()
                            stdscr.addstr(5, 0, f"Letzte Aktion: {action_text}")
                            stdscr.refresh()
                        continue

                    elif key == 27: 
                        break

                    elif key == curses.KEY_UP:
                        if auto_aktiv: self._stoppe_autonomen_modus()
                        speed = self.SPEED_FORWARD
                        angle = self.ANGLE_STRAIGHT
                        action_text = "MANUELL VORWÄRTS"
                        valid_key = True
                    elif key == curses.KEY_DOWN:
                        if auto_aktiv: self._stoppe_autonomen_modus()
                        speed = self.SPEED_BACKWARD
                        angle = self.ANGLE_STRAIGHT
                        action_text = "MANUELL RÜCKWÄRTS"
                        valid_key = True
                    elif key == curses.KEY_LEFT:
                        if auto_aktiv: self._stoppe_autonomen_modus()
                        angle = self.ANGLE_LEFT
                        action_text = "MANUELL LINKS"
                        valid_key = True
                    elif key == curses.KEY_RIGHT:
                        if auto_aktiv: self._stoppe_autonomen_modus()
                        angle = self.ANGLE_RIGHT
                        action_text = "MANUELL RECHTS"
                        valid_key = True
                    elif key == ord(' '): 
                        if auto_aktiv: self._stoppe_autonomen_modus()
                        speed = 0
                        angle = self.ANGLE_STRAIGHT
                        action_text = "🛑 NOT-STOPP"
                        valid_key = True

                    if valid_key and (speed != last_speed or angle != last_angle):
                        self._car.drive(speed, angle)
                        
                        stdscr.move(5, 0)
                        stdscr.clrtoeol()
                        stdscr.addstr(5, 0, f"Letzte Aktion: {action_text} -> drive({speed}, {angle})")
                        stdscr.refresh()
                        
                        last_speed = speed
                        last_angle = angle

                time.sleep(step_time)

            except KeyboardInterrupt:
                break

        self._stoppe_autonomen_modus()
        self._car.drive(0, self.ANGLE_STRAIGHT)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    logging_setup.setup_project_logging(logging.CRITICAL)

    print("\n🏎️  Initialisiere SensorCar Hardware und Sensor-Threads...")
    sc = SensorCar()
    sc.stop()

    us = UltrasonicSensor.UltrasonicSensor(sc)
    ir = InfraredSensor.InfraredSensor(sc)
    
    stop_event = threading.Event()
    us_sensor_thread = threading.Thread(target=us.read_loop, args=[stop_event], daemon=True)
    ir_sensor_thread = threading.Thread(target=ir.read_loop, args=[stop_event], daemon=True)
    
    ir_sensor_thread.start()
    us_sensor_thread.start()
    
    time.sleep(0.5) 

    key_mode = KeyboardMode(car=sc)
    try:
        key_mode.start()
    finally:
        print("\n🛑 Räume Hardware-Ressourcen auf... Bitte warten.")
        stop_event.set()
        sc.stop()
        us_sensor_thread.join()
        ir_sensor_thread.join()
        print("✅ Hardware sicher gestoppt. Bis zur nächsten Fahrt!")

================================================================================
FILE: BaseCar.py
================================================================================

from threading import RLock
from typing import Any, Optional
import logging

from CarLogger import Loggable
from ConfigReader import ConfigReader
from basisklassen import BackWheels, FrontWheels
import time

class BaseCar(Loggable):

    FORWARD_MODE = 1
    BACKWARD_MODE = -1

    _json_config = {}

    def __init__(self, config:Optional[ConfigReader]=None):
        self._steering_angle = 90
        self._speed = 0
        self._mode = self.FORWARD_MODE
        self._cfg = config if config is not None else ConfigReader("car")
        self._fw = FrontWheels(turning_offset=self.turning_offset)
        self._bw = BackWheels(forward_A=self.forward_a, forward_B=self.forward_b)
        self.__log = logging.getLogger(BaseCar.__name__)

        self._lock = RLock()

    def get_logging_payload(self, log_level:int=logging.INFO) -> dict:
        with self._lock:
            return {
                "timestamp": time.time(),
                "steering_angle": self.steering_angle,
                "speed": self.speed,
                "direction": self.direction
            }


    def get_car_properties(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "steering_angle": self._steering_angle,
            "speed": self._speed
        }

    @property
    def v_min(self) -> int:
        return self._cfg.get_int("v_min", 20)

    @property
    def v_max(self) -> int:
        return self._cfg.get_int("v_max", 100)


    # self._config
    def _save_config(self) -> bool:
        return self._cfg._save_config()

    # Legacy, use self._config.set_config() instead
    def set_config(self, attribut: str, values = any, save_config=False) -> bool:
        return self._cfg.set_config(attribut, values, save_config)

    # Legacy, use self._config.get_config() instead
    def get_config(self, file: str = "config.json", force_update = False) -> dict:
        return self._cfg.get_config(force_update)
    
    # Legacy, use self._config._update_config() instead
    def _update_config(self):
        self._cfg._load_config_file()

    # Value from cfg_file
    @property
    def turning_offset(self) -> int:
        return self.get_config().get("turning_offset", 0)

    # Value from cfg_file
    @property
    def forward_a(self) -> int:
        return self.get_config().get("forward_A", 0)
    
    # Value from cfg_file
    @property
    def forward_b(self) -> int:
        return self.get_config().get("forward_B", 0)

    def test_wheels(self):
        self._fw.test()
        self._bw.test()

    # Live value (with lock)
    @property
    def steering_angle(self):
        with self._lock:
            return self._steering_angle
 
    # Live value (with lock)
    @steering_angle.setter
    def steering_angle(self, angle:int):
        angle = min(135, max(45, angle))
        self._fw.turn(angle)
        with self._lock:
            if (self._steering_angle != angle):
                self.__log.info(f"Changed steering_angle: {self._steering_angle} -> {angle}")
            self.__log.debug(f"Set steering angle: {angle}")
            self._steering_angle = angle
    
    # Live value (with lock)
    @property
    def speed(self) -> int:
        with self._lock:
            return self._mode * self._speed

    # Live value (with lock)
    @speed.setter
    def speed(self, speed:int): 
          
        vmin = self._cfg.get_int("v_min", 20)
        vmax = self._cfg.get_int("v_max", 100)
        speed = max(-100, min(100, speed))
        self._mode = self.FORWARD_MODE if speed >= 0 else self.BACKWARD_MODE
        if (speed == 0):
            self._bw.speed = speed
            self._mode = self.FORWARD_MODE
            self._bw.forward()
        elif (speed > 0):
            speed = min(max(vmin, speed), vmax)
            self._bw.speed = speed
            self._mode = self.FORWARD_MODE
            self._bw.forward()
        else:
            speed = max(-vmax, min(-vmin, speed))
            self._bw.speed = abs(speed)
            self._mode = self.BACKWARD_MODE
            self._bw.backward()
        with self._lock:
            if (self._speed != speed):
                self.__log.info(f"Changed speed: {self._speed} -> {speed}")

            self.__log.debug(f"Set steering angle: {speed}")           
            self._speed = abs(speed)

    # Direction (equals mode)
    # Live value (with lock)
    @property
    def direction(self):
        with self._lock:
            return self._mode

    def drive(self, speed:Optional[int]=None, steering_angle:Optional[int]=None):
        self.speed = speed if speed is not None else self.speed
        self.steering_angle = steering_angle if steering_angle is not None else self._steering_angle

    def stop(self):
        self.drive(0,90)



================================================================================
END FILE: BaseCar.py

# Changelog Paket A+B

- Paket A: `SensorSnapshot`, Zeitstempel für IR und Ultraschall, gemeinsames `CameraCar`-Sensormodell.
- Paket B: Ringbuffer-basierter Recorder mit Frame-ID-Verknüpfung, Rohbildern, Linien, Prozessparametern und Status für fehlende Fahrentscheidungen.
- `BaseCar`: negative Geschwindigkeit als Betrag intern speichern und Richtung getrennt halten.
- `ConfigReader`: boolesche Zeichenketten explizit auswerten.
- `FollowLine`: PID-Zeit und Zustandsfortschreibung korrigiert.
- `DrivingMode`: Start-/Stop-Thread-Lifecycle verbessert.
- `CameraSensor`: leere Frames und Ressourcenfreigabe behandelt; zyklisches Debug-JPEG entfernt.
- `CameraDataProcessed` als Kompatibilitätsbrücke beibehalten.

import logging
from typing import Optional
 
from SensorCar import SensorCar
from ConfigReader import ConfigReader
 
from models import CameraData, CameraDataProcessed, DrivingState
from telemetry import FrameRingBuffer, FrequencyCounter
 
import numpy as np
import os
 
import cv2
 
 
class CameraCar(SensorCar):
    """Lässt das Auto mittels des Ultraschallsensor (Abstandmessung) fahren
 
    Args:
        BaseCar (object): Basisklasse des Autos - Kann nur Lenkung und Geschwindigkeit einstellen
    """
    def __init__(self, config:Optional[ConfigReader]=None):
        """Initiert das Object 'CameraCar' und über super-init die Methoden aus 'BaseCar'
        """        
 
        # Super init - Damit ein Object initiiert wird die auch BaseCar kennt (Methoden der BaseCar)
        super().__init__(config=config)
        #self._distance = 300
        self._camera_data = CameraData(process_image=np.empty(0), raw_image=np.empty(0))
        self._camera_data_processed = CameraDataProcessed(lanes=None, process_image=np.empty(0), raw_image=np.empty(0), process_image_lines=np.empty(0), line_data=np.empty(0))
        # Der Ringbuffer enthaelt eine begrenzte Historie, keine neue Bildkopie.
        self.frame_history = FrameRingBuffer(maxlen=60)
        self.thread_metrics = {
            "camera": FrequencyCounter(),
            "controller": FrequencyCounter(),
        }
        self._driving_state: DrivingState | None = None
        self.__log = logging.getLogger(CameraCar.__name__)
 
    @property
    def save_data(self) -> bool: # Lenkwinkel, Speed für AI imt Zeitstempel
        with self._lock:
            return self._cfg.get_bool("save_data", False)
 
    @property
    def camera_data(self) -> CameraData :
        with self._lock:
            return self._camera_data
 
    @camera_data.setter
    def camera_data(self, camera_data:CameraData):
        with self._lock:
            self._camera_data = camera_data
            self.frame_history.add_camera(camera_data)
            self.thread_metrics["camera"].tick()
        #print(self._camera_data)
 
    @property
    def camera_data_processed(self) -> CameraDataProcessed :
        with self._lock:
            return self._camera_data_processed
 
    @camera_data_processed.setter
    def camera_data_processed(self, camera_data_processed:CameraDataProcessed):
        with self._lock:
            self._camera_data_processed = camera_data_processed
        #print(self._camera_data_processed)

    @property
    def driving_state(self) -> DrivingState | None:
        """Letzte Fahrentscheidung, unabhaengig vom Dashboard-Format."""
        with self._lock:
            return self._driving_state

    @driving_state.setter
    def driving_state(self, state: DrivingState):
        with self._lock:
            self._driving_state = state
            self.frame_history.add_driving(state)
            self.thread_metrics["controller"].tick()

import logging
import threading
import time
from typing import Optional
import cv2

from models import CameraData, CameraDataProcessed

import numpy as np

import ConfigReader
from CameraCar import CameraCar

from basisklassen_cam import Camera
from pipeline_processors import LaneProcessor, CopilotLaneProcessor

def blur_img(img, blur_value = (10,10)):
    return cv2.blur(img,blur_value)

def kanten_erkennung(img, minmaxVal = (20,250)):
    return cv2.Canny(img,minmaxVal[0],minmaxVal[1])

def morph_kernel_filter(img, morph_kernel=(5, 5)):

    # Falls ein BGR-Bild übergeben wird, in Graustufen umwandeln
    if img.ndim == 3:
        mask_gray = cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    else:
        mask_gray = img

    mask_bin = (mask_gray > 0).astype(np.uint8) * 255

    kernel = np.ones(morph_kernel, dtype=np.uint8)

    img_morph_kernel = cv2.morphologyEx(
        mask_bin,
        cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,
        kernel
    )

    return img_morph_kernel


class CameraSensor(Camera):

    def __init__(self, car:CameraCar, sensor_config:Optional[ConfigReader.ConfigReader]=None, logger:Optional[logging.Logger]=None) -> None:

        self._log = logger if logger is not None else logging.getLogger(self.__class__.__name__)
        self._cfg = sensor_config if sensor_config is not None else ConfigReader.ConfigReader('cam_sensor', logger=self._log)
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._debug_mode = False
        self._car = car
        # Der Prozessor ist austauschbar; die restliche Architektur bleibt gleich.
        self._processor = CopilotLaneProcessor()
        # Laufende Nummer fuer die Zuordnung von Bild und Fahrentscheidung.
        self._frame_id = 0
        # Gespeicherte Studio-Konfiguration auch ohne offenen Browser nutzen.
        from pipeline_config import load as load_pipeline_config
        settings = load_pipeline_config()
        self._pack_processing_parameters = (
            tuple(settings['hue']), tuple(settings['sat']), tuple(settings['val']),
            tuple(settings['crop']), int(settings['blur']), int(settings['morph']),
            tuple(settings['canny']), dict(settings['enabled']),
        )
        print(self._cfg.get('colormode'))
        super().__init__ (colorspace=str(self._cfg.get_str('colormode','gray')),
                          height=self._cfg.get_int('height',480),
                          width=self._cfg.get_int('width',640))
        
        self.set_camera_controls({
            "AeEnable": True,
            "ExposureValue": 1.0,
        })

    @property
    def scan_frequency(self) -> int:
        with self._lock:
            return self._cfg.get_int('scan_frequency', 25)

    @property
    def save_image(self) -> bool: # Mit Zeitstempel. -- In Dateiname: Zeitstempel; Lenkwinkel, Speed
        # Hier einstellen (Auflösung, Pixel etc.)
        with self._lock:
            return self._cfg.get_bool('save_image', False)

    @property
    def processing_parameters(self):
        with self._lock:
            return self._pack_processing_parameters

    @processing_parameters.setter
    def processing_parameters(self,pack_processing_parameters):
        with self._lock:
            self._pack_processing_parameters = pack_processing_parameters

    def set_processor(self, name: str):
        """Wechselt zwischen Legacy und Diagnose-Prozessor.

        Der Wechsel wird unter dem bestehenden Sensor-Lock vorgenommen.
        """
        if name not in ('legacy', 'copilot'):
            raise ValueError('Unbekannter Prozessor')
        with self._lock:
            self._processor = LaneProcessor() if name == 'legacy' else CopilotLaneProcessor()

    @property
    def processor_name(self) -> str:
        with self._lock:
            return 'copilot' if isinstance(self._processor, CopilotLaneProcessor) else 'legacy'

    def read_loop(self, stop_event:threading.Event):
        try:
            self._log.debug('Entering Camera-Sensor loop')
            while (not stop_event.is_set()):
                if (self._debug_mode):
                    self._cfg._load_config_file()
                #v = self.sensor_values
                self._log.debug(f'Read from camera-sensor')

                image = self.get_frame()
                # Leere Frames werden nicht an die Verarbeitung weitergereicht.
                if image is None or image.size == 0:
                    self._log.warning('Leeres Kamerabild verworfen')
                    stop_event.wait(0.1)
                    continue
                # Parameter fuer diesen Frame einmalig lesen.
                parameters = self.processing_parameters
                # Genau eine Verarbeitung je Frame, mit optionalen Diagnosebildern.
                with self._lock:
                    processor = self._processor
                result = processor.process(image, parameters)
                self._frame_id += 1
                cameradata = CameraData(
                    raw_image=image,
                    timestamp=time.time_ns(),
                    frame_id=self._frame_id,
                    monotonic_ns=time.monotonic_ns(),
                    crop=(tuple(parameters[3]) if len(parameters) < 8 or
                          parameters[7].get("roi", True) else (0.0, 1.0)),
                    lanes=result.lanes,
                    process_image=result.processed_image,
                    stages=result.stages,
                    processing_parameters=parameters,
                    processor_name=type(processor).__name__,
                )
                self._car.camera_data = cameradata

                stop_event.wait(1/max(1, self.scan_frequency))
        except Exception:
            self._log.exception("Kameraschleife fehlgeschlagen")
            raise
        finally:
            self._log.debug('Exit Camera loop')
            self.release()

if __name__ == '__main__':

    print('This is the main.')

STAGES = [('raw', 'Rohbild'), ('roi', 'ROI'), ('hsv', 'HSV'), ('mask', 'Farbmaske'),
          ('blur', 'Blur'), ('morph', 'Morphologie'), ('edges', 'Canny'), ('overlay', 'Linien')]

def uri(image):
    if image is None or getattr(image, 'size', 0) == 0:
        return None
    ok, encoded = cv2.imencode('.jpg', image, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 70])
    return 'data:image/jpeg;base64,' + base64.b64encode(encoded).decode('ascii') if ok else None

def slider(label, key, low, high, value, step=1, pair=False):
    component = dcc.RangeSlider if pair else dcc.Slider
    return html.Div([html.Label(label), component(id=f'cfg-{key}', min=low, max=high,
        step=step, value=value)], className='mb-3')

def create_app(application):

    pipeline = html.Div([
        dbc.Row([dbc.Col(dbc.Alert('LIVE: Bilder folgen der Kamera. FREEZE: Ein Frame bleibt für die Analyse stehen.', color='secondary'), md=8),

        dbc.Button('Sessions aktualisieren', id='session-refresh', color='secondary', className='my-2'),

        ])), id='recovery-panel', is_open=False),

    ], fluid=True)

    @app.callback(Output('global-action-message', 'children'),
                  Input('global-start', 'n_clicks'), Input('global-stop', 'n_clicks'),
                  State('mode', 'value'), prevent_initial_call=True)
    def global_action(_start, _stop, mode):
        try:
            if ctx.triggered_id == 'global-stop':
                application.recovery.disable()
                application.stop_modes()
                return 'Fahrzeug gestoppt'
            application.start_mode(mode)
            return 'Fahrmodus gestartet'
        except Exception as exc:
            return f'Aktion abgewiesen: {exc}'

    @app.callback(Output('recovery-panel', 'is_open'),
                  Input('recovery-toggle', 'n_clicks'),
                  State('recovery-panel', 'is_open'), prevent_initial_call=True)
    def recovery_toggle(_, opened):
        try:
            if opened:
                application.recovery.disable()
                return False
            application.recovery.enable()
            return True
        except RuntimeError:
            return False

    @app.callback(Output('recovery-status', 'children'),
                  Input('recovery-pulse', 'n_intervals'), State('recovery-keys', 'data'))
    def recovery_pulse(_, keys):
        if not application.recovery.enabled:
            return 'Bergung inaktiv'
        keys = keys or {}
        try:
            application.recovery.command(
                forward=bool(keys.get('w')), reverse=bool(keys.get('s')),
                left=bool(keys.get('a')), right=bool(keys.get('d')),
                deadman=bool(keys.get('deadman')))
            return 'Bergung aktiv · Steuerung nur mit Leertaste'
        except RuntimeError as exc:
            return str(exc)

    @app.callback(Output('session-stats', 'children'), Input('refresh', 'n_intervals'))
    def session_stats(_):
        return f"{'REC ●' if sessions.is_running() else 'REC AUS'} · {sessions.count} Frames · {sessions.path or 'keine Session'} · {sessions.error or 'kein Fehler'}"

    @app.callback(Output('global-camera-state', 'children'),
                  Output('global-mode-state', 'children'), Output('global-frame', 'children'),
                  Output('global-start', 'disabled'), Output('global-start', 'outline'),
                  Output('global-stop', 'outline'), Output('recovery-toggle', 'outline'),
                  Output('telemetry-preview', 'src'), Output('record-preview', 'src'),
                  Input('refresh', 'n_intervals'), State('main-tabs', 'active_tab'))
    def global_status(_, tab):
        camera = application.car.camera_data
        active = application.running()
        frame = camera.frame_id if camera is not None else None
        preview = None
        if tab in ('telemetry', 'record') and camera is not None:
            # Niedrig aufgelöstes Rohbild, keine zweite Linienerkennung.
            raw = camera.raw_image
            if raw is not None and getattr(raw, 'size', 0):
                h, w = raw.shape[:2]
                preview_image = render_calibration_overlay(camera)
                if preview_image is not None:
                    ph, pw = preview_image.shape[:2]
                    preview = uri(cv2.resize(preview_image, (min(320, pw), max(1, int(ph * min(320,pw)/pw)))))
        return ('KAMERA LIVE' if camera is not None else 'KAMERA WARTET',
                'FAHRT AKTIV' if active else 'FAHRMODUS AUS',
                f'Frame #{frame}' if frame is not None else '',
                active or application.recovery.enabled, not active,
                active, not application.recovery.enabled,
                preview if tab == 'telemetry' else no_update,
                preview if tab == 'record' else no_update)

    @app.callback(Output('drive-message', 'children'), Input('drive-start', 'n_clicks'),
                  Input('drive-stop', 'n_clicks'), State('mode', 'value'), prevent_initial_call=True)
    def drive_action(_start, _stop, mode):
        try:
            if ctx.triggered_id == 'drive-start':
                application.start_mode(mode)
                return 'Fahrmodus gestartet'
            application.recovery.disable()
            application.stop_modes()
            return 'Fahrzeug gestoppt'
        except Exception as exc:
            log.exception('Fahraktion fehlgeschlagen')
            return f'Fehler: {exc}'

    @app.callback(Output('cfg-message', 'children'), Input('cfg-apply', 'n_clicks'),
                  Input('cfg-save', 'n_clicks'),
                  Input('cfg-processor', 'value'), Input('cfg-crop', 'value'),
                  Input('cfg-hue', 'value'), Input('cfg-sat', 'value'),
                  Input('cfg-val', 'value'), Input('cfg-blur', 'value'),
                  Input('cfg-morph', 'value'), Input('cfg-canny', 'value'),
                  *[Input(f'cfg-enable-{name}', 'value') for name in ('roi','mask','blur','morph','canny','lanes')],
                  prevent_initial_call=True)
    def configure(_apply, _save, processor, crop, hue, sat, val, blur, morph, canny, *switches):
        names = ('roi','mask','blur','morph','canny','lanes')
        settings = dict(processor=processor, crop=crop, hue=hue, sat=sat, val=val,
                        blur=blur, morph=morph, canny=canny,
                        enabled={name: 'on' in (v or []) for name,v in zip(names,switches)})
        try:
            if ctx.triggered_id == 'cfg-save':
                application.save_pipeline(settings)
                return 'Einstellungen gespeichert'
            application.set_pipeline(settings)
            return 'Live-Vorschau aktualisiert · zum dauerhaften Speichern auf Speichern klicken'
        except Exception as exc:
            return f'Nicht übernommen: {exc}'

    @app.callback(Output('ab-status', 'children'), Output('ab-image-a', 'src'),
                  Output('ab-image-b', 'src'), Output('ab-lanes-a', 'children'),
                  Output('ab-lanes-b', 'children'),
                  Input('ab-freeze', 'n_clicks'), Input('ab-run', 'n_clicks'),
                  Input('ab-reset', 'n_clicks'),
                  State('cfg-processor', 'value'), State('cfg-crop', 'value'),
                  State('cfg-hue', 'value'), State('cfg-sat', 'value'),
                  State('cfg-val', 'value'), State('cfg-blur', 'value'),
                  State('cfg-morph', 'value'), State('cfg-canny', 'value'),
                  *[State(f'cfg-enable-{name}', 'value') for name in ('roi','mask','blur','morph','canny','lanes')],
                  prevent_initial_call=True)
    def ab_calibrate(_freeze, _run, _reset, processor, crop, hue, sat, val, blur, morph, canny, *switches):
        trigger = ctx.triggered_id
        if trigger == 'ab-reset':
            ab.clear()
            return 'A/B zurückgesetzt', None, None, '', ''
        enabled = {name: 'on' in (v or []) for name, v in
                   zip(('roi','mask','blur','morph','canny','lanes'), switches)}
        params = (tuple(hue), tuple(sat), tuple(val), tuple(crop),
                  int(blur), int(morph), tuple(canny), enabled)
        try:
            if trigger == 'ab-freeze':
                frame = ab.freeze(application.car.camera_data, params, processor)
                return f'Referenz A: Frame {frame} eingefroren', None, None, '', ''
            comparison = ab.compare(params, processor)
            return (f'A/B auf Frame {comparison.frame_id} berechnet',
                    uri(comparison.image_a), uri(comparison.image_b),
                    f'Linien A: {len(comparison.lanes_a)}',
                    f'Linien B: {len(comparison.lanes_b)}')
        except (ValueError, TypeError, cv2.error) as exc:
            return f'A/B nicht möglich: {exc}', no_update, no_update, no_update, no_update

    @app.callback(Output('record-status', 'children'), Input('record-toggle', 'n_clicks'),
                  prevent_initial_call=True)
    def toggle(_):
        try:
            if sessions.is_running(): sessions.stop()
            else: sessions.start()
            return ('REC AKTIV' if sessions.is_running() else 'Aufzeichnung gestoppt')
        except Exception as exc:
            return f'Aufzeichnungsfehler: {exc}'

    @app.callback(Output('session-choice', 'options'), Input('session-refresh', 'n_clicks'))
    def refresh_sessions(_):
        return [{'label':'Live-Ringbuffer','value':'__live__'}] + [
            {'label':name,'value':name} for name in list_sessions()]

    @app.callback(Output('pipeline-frozen', 'data'), Input('pipeline-freeze', 'n_clicks'),
                  State('pipeline-frozen', 'data'), prevent_initial_call=True)
    def freeze(_, frozen):
        if frozen is not None:
            return None
        camera = application.car.camera_data
        return camera.frame_id if camera is not None else None

    @app.callback(Output('inspected-stage', 'data'),
                  *[Input(f'inspect-{key}', 'n_clicks') for key, _ in STAGES],
                  prevent_initial_call=True)
    def inspect(*_):
        button = ctx.triggered_id
        return button.removeprefix('inspect-') if button else 'overlay'

    @app.callback(Output('drive-overlay', 'src'),
                  *[Output(f'stage-{key}', 'src') for key,_ in STAGES],
                  Output('drive-status', 'children'), Output('drive-angle', 'children'),
                  Output('drive-speed', 'children'), Output('drive-camera-badge', 'children'),
                  Output('drive-frame-badge', 'children'), Output('drive-mode-status', 'children'),
                  Output('record-status', 'title'),
                  Output('pipeline-frame-label', 'children'),
                  Output('inspect-image', 'src'), Output('inspect-title', 'children'),
                  *[Output(f'stage-info-{key}', 'children') for key,_ in STAGES],
                  Input('refresh', 'n_intervals'), State('pipeline-frozen', 'data'),
                  State('inspected-stage', 'data'), State('main-tabs', 'active_tab'))
    def images(_, frozen, inspected, active_tab):
        camera = application.car.camera_data
        if camera is None or camera.frame_id < 0:
            return (None, *([None] * len(STAGES)), 'Noch kein Kameraframe', '–', '–', 'WARTET', '', 'Fahrmodus aus', '', 'Warte auf Kamera', None, '', *([''] * len(STAGES)))
        if frozen is not None:
            match = next((r.camera for r in application.car.frame_history.snapshot()
                          if r.camera.frame_id == frozen), None)
            if match is not None:
                pipeline_camera = match
            else:
                pipeline_camera = camera
                frozen = None
        else:
            pipeline_camera = camera
        stages = getattr(pipeline_camera, 'stages', {}) or {}
        state = application.car.driving_state
        record = next((r for r in reversed(application.car.frame_history.snapshot())
                       if state is not None and r.camera.frame_id == state.frame_id and r.driving is not None), None)
        overlay = None
        if active_tab == 'drive':
            overlay = (render_overlay(record.camera, record.driving)
                       if record and record.camera.frame_id == camera.frame_id and application.running()
                       else render_calibration_overlay(camera))
            overlay = draw_steering_overlay(overlay, application.car.steering_angle)
        stage_images = ({**stages, 'raw': pipeline_camera.raw_image,
                         'overlay': render_calibration_overlay(pipeline_camera)}
                        if active_tab == 'pipeline' else {})
        speed = state.speed if state and application.running() else application.car.speed
        angle = state.steering_angle_car if state and application.running() else application.car.steering_angle
        text = f'Frame {camera.frame_id} · Speed-Steuerwert {speed} · Lenkwinkel {angle} · Modus {application.active_mode or "aus"}'
        return (uri(overlay) if active_tab == 'drive' else no_update,
                *[(uri(stage_images.get(key)) if active_tab == 'pipeline' else no_update) for key,_ in STAGES], text,
                f'{angle}°', str(speed), 'LIVE', f'Frame #{camera.frame_id}',
                f'Modus: {application.active_mode or "aus"}',
                f'Frame {camera.frame_id} · Recorder {"aktiv" if application.recorder.is_running() else "inaktiv"}',
                f'{"FREEZE" if frozen is not None else "LIVE"} · Frame {pipeline_camera.frame_id}',
                (uri(stage_images.get(inspected)) if active_tab == 'pipeline' else no_update), inspected,
                *[('Verfügbar' if stage_images.get(key) is not None else 'Nicht bereitgestellt')
                  for key, _ in STAGES])

    @app.callback(Output('drive-diagnostic', 'children'), Input('refresh', 'n_intervals'))
    def driving_diagnostic(_):
        mode = application.modes.get('camera')
        diagnostic = getattr(mode, 'last_diagnostic', None)
        if not diagnostic:
            return 'Noch keine Controllerdiagnose'
        return (f"Frame {diagnostic.get('frame_id')} · "
                f"Linien: {len([x for x in diagnostic.get('lanes', ()) if x is not None])} · "
                f"Rohwinkel: {diagnostic.get('raw_angle')} · "
                f"Vorheriger Winkel gehalten: {diagnostic.get('held_previous_angle')} · "
                f"Soll: {diagnostic.get('requested_angle')} · "
                f"Stellwert: {diagnostic.get('actual_angle')} · "
                f"Stoppursache: {getattr(mode, 'last_stop_reason', None) or 'keine'}")

    @app.callback(Output('freq-bars', 'figure'), Output('freq-history', 'figure'),
                  Output('drive-history', 'figure'), Output('data-age', 'children'),
                  Output('event-list', 'children'), Input('refresh', 'n_intervals'))
    def charts(_):
        rows, freqs, events = application.sample()
        camera = application.car.camera_data
        state = application.car.driving_state
        metrics = application.car.thread_metrics
        targets = [float(application.sensor.scan_frequency),
                   float(getattr(application.modes.get('camera'), '_frequency', 0))]
        actual = [metrics[key].hz if metrics[key].age_seconds is not None and metrics[key].age_seconds < 2 else 0
                  for key in ('camera', 'controller')]
        bars = go.Figure()
        bars.add_bar(name='Soll', x=['Kamera','Controller'], y=targets)
        bars.add_bar(name='Ist', x=['Kamera','Controller'], y=actual)
        bars.update_layout(barmode='group')
        fplot = go.Figure()
        if freqs:
            start = freqs[-1][0]
            for i, name in ((1, 'Kamera'), (2, 'Controller')):
                fplot.add_scatter(x=[r[0]-start for r in freqs], y=[r[i] for r in freqs], name=name)
        dplot = go.Figure()
        if rows:
            start = rows[-1][0]
            for i, name in ((2, 'Lenkwinkel'), (3, 'Speed'), (4, 'Linie fehlt')):
                dplot.add_scatter(x=[r[0]-start for r in rows], y=[r[i] for r in rows], name=name)
        age = 'Keine Daten verfügbar'
        if camera is not None and camera.monotonic_ns:
            camera_ms = max(0, time.monotonic_ns() - camera.monotonic_ns) / 1e6
            age = f'Kameradatenalter: {camera_ms:.1f} ms'
            if state and state.frame_id == camera.frame_id:
                age += f' · Entscheidungsverzögerung: {(state.timestamp_ns-camera.timestamp)/1e6:.1f} ms (Systemuhr)'
        return bars, fplot, dplot, age, (
            [html.Div(f'Frame {event.frame_id}: {event.code} · {event.detail}')
             for event in application.health.events()[-12:]] +
            [html.Div(f'Frame {fid}: {text}') for fid,text in list(events)[-5:]])
    def replay_rows(name):
        if not name or name == '__live__':
            return application.car.frame_history.snapshot()
        try:
            return load_session(name)[1]
        except (ValueError, OSError):
            return []

    def record_id(record):
        return int(record['frame_id']) if isinstance(record, dict) else record.camera.frame_id

    @app.callback(Output('replay-index', 'max'), Output('replay-range', 'children'),
                  Input('refresh', 'n_intervals'), Input('session-choice', 'value'))
    def replay_limits(_, session_name):
        records = replay_rows(session_name)
        return max(0, len(records)-1), f'{len(records)} Frames · '+('Live-Ringbuffer' if session_name == '__live__' else 'gespeicherte Session')

    @app.callback(Output('replay-playing', 'data'), Input('replay-play', 'n_clicks'),
                  State('replay-playing', 'data'), prevent_initial_call=True)
    def replay_toggle(_, playing):
        return not bool(playing)

    @app.callback(Output('replay-index', 'value'),
                  Input('replay-prev', 'n_clicks'), Input('replay-next', 'n_clicks'),
                  Input('replay-chart', 'clickData'), Input('refresh', 'n_intervals'),
                  State('replay-index', 'value'), State('replay-playing', 'data'), State('session-choice', 'value'))
    def seek(_prev, _next, click, _tick, index, playing, session_name):
        records = replay_rows(session_name)
        if not records:
            return 0
        index = max(0, min(int(index or 0), len(records)-1))
        trigger = ctx.triggered_id
        if trigger == 'replay-prev':
            return max(0, index-1)
        if trigger == 'replay-next':
            return min(len(records)-1, index+1)
        if trigger == 'replay-chart' and click and click.get('points'):
            fid = click['points'][0].get('customdata')
            return next((i for i,r in enumerate(records) if record_id(r) == fid), index)
        if trigger == 'refresh' and playing:
            return min(len(records)-1, index+1)
        return no_update

    @app.callback(Output('replay-image', 'src'), Output('replay-frame-label', 'children'),
                  Output('replay-details', 'children'), Output('replay-chart', 'figure'),
                  Input('replay-index', 'value'), Input('refresh', 'n_intervals'), Input('session-choice', 'value'))
    def replay_render(index, _, session_name):
        records = replay_rows(session_name)
        chart = go.Figure()
        if not records:
            return None, 'Keine Frames', 'Kamera starten', chart
        ids = [record_id(r) for r in records]
        angles = [float(r['steering_angle']) if r.get('steering_angle') else None for r in records] if isinstance(records[0], dict) else [r.driving.steering_angle if r.driving else None for r in records]
        chart.add_scatter(x=ids, y=angles, customdata=ids, mode='lines+markers', name='Lenkwinkel')
        chart.update_layout(xaxis_title='Frame-ID', yaxis_title='Lenkwinkel',
                            margin=dict(l=35,r=15,t=20,b=35), height=220)
        record = records[max(0, min(int(index or 0),len(records)-1))]
        if isinstance(record, dict):
            path, _rows = load_session(session_name)
            filename = (path/record['image_filename']).resolve()
            if not filename.is_relative_to(path):
                return None, 'Ungültiger Bildpfad', '', chart
            image = draw_hud(cv2.imread(str(filename)), record)
            return uri(image), f"Frame #{record_id(record)}", f"Status: {record['data_status']} · Winkel: {record['steering_angle'] or 'fehlt'}", chart
        image = render_overlay(record.camera, record.driving) if record.driving else render_calibration_overlay(record.camera)
        info = (f'Frame {record.camera.frame_id} · '
                + (f'Lenkwinkel {record.driving.steering_angle} · Speed {record.driving.speed}'
                   if record.driving else 'Keine zugeordnete Fahrentscheidung'))
        return uri(image), f'Frame #{record.camera.frame_id}', info, chart

    @app.callback(Output('replay-download', 'data'), Input('replay-export', 'n_clicks'),
                  prevent_initial_call=True)
    def export_buffer(_):
        records = application.car.frame_history.snapshot()
        if not records:
            return no_update
        buffer = io.BytesIO()
        with zipfile.ZipFile(buffer, 'w', compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:
            rows = []
            for record in records:
                camera, driving = record.camera, record.driving
                image = camera.raw_image
                if image is None or image.size == 0:
                    continue
                success, encoded = cv2.imencode('.jpg', image)
                if not success:
                    continue
                name = f'frames/frame_{camera.frame_id}.jpg'
                archive.writestr(name, encoded.tobytes())
                rows.append(dict(frame_id=camera.frame_id, image=name,
                                 timestamp_ns=camera.timestamp,
                                 steering_angle=driving.steering_angle if driving else '',
                                 speed=driving.speed if driving else '',
                                 status='complete' if driving else 'no_driving_state'))
            text = io.StringIO()
            writer = csv.DictWriter(text, fieldnames=['frame_id','image','timestamp_ns',
                'steering_angle','speed','status'])
            writer.writeheader()
            writer.writerows(rows)
            archive.writestr('frames.csv', text.getvalue())
            archive.writestr('README.txt', 'Kurzexport des flüchtigen Ringbuffers. Keine vollständige Session.\n')
        return dcc.send_bytes(buffer.getvalue(), 'camp2code_ringbuffer_export.zip')

    return app


def main():
    application = CarApplication()
    atexit.register(application.shutdown)
    try:
        create_app(application).run(host='0.0.0.0', port=8050, debug=False, use_reloader=False)
    finally:
        application.shutdown()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

FILE: CarLogger.py

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
import logging
import threading
import time
  
class Loggable(ABC):

    @abstractmethod
    def get_logging_payload(self, log_level:int=logging.INFO) -> dict:
        pass
        


class CarLogger():

    def __init__(self, log_object:Loggable, logfile="car_log.json", log_name="CarLogger", log_level=logging.INFO):
        self._lastTime = 0
        self._car = log_object

        # 1. Logger erstellen
        self._logger = logging.getLogger(log_name)
        self._logger.setLevel(log_level)

        # 2. Datei-Handler hinzufügen (Schreibt in die JSON-Datei)
        file_handler = logging.FileHandler(logfile)
        file_handler.setLevel(log_level)
        
        # Einfaches Format für die Datei (Zeit - Nachricht)
        formatter = logging.Formatter('%(asctime)s - %(message)s')
        #formatter = jsonlogger.JsonFormatter(
        #    fmt="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s %(message)s",
        #    rename_fields={"asctime": "timestamp", "levelname": "level"}
        #)
        file_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
        
        # Handler an den Logger hängen
        self._logger.addHandler(file_handler)

        self._log_level = log_level

    @property
    def logger(self):
        return self._logger

    def debug(self, msg):
        return self.logger.debug(msg)
    
    def error(self, msg):
        return self.logger.error(msg)
    
    def run(self, stop_event:threading.Event):
        while not stop_event.is_set():
            self.log_car_state()
            time.sleep(0.1)
       
    def log_car_state(self):
        self._logger.log(self._log_level, self._car.get_logging_payload())


================================================================================
END FILE: CarLogger.py

================================================================================

import json
import threading
import logging
from typing import Any, Optional

class ConfigReader:
    """
    Thread-sicherer JSON-Konfigurationsleser für Namespaced-Konfigurationen.

    Diese Klasse lädt eine JSON-Datei, liest Werte aus einem angegebenen
    Namespace und stellt Hilfsmethoden für verschiedene Datentypen bereit.
    """

    def __init__(self, cfg_namespace: str, config_file: str = "config.json", logger:Optional[logging.Logger]=None) -> None:
        """
        Initialisiert den ConfigReader und lädt die Konfigurationsdatei.

        Args:
            cfg_namespace (str): Der Namespace innerhalb der JSON-Datei,
                aus dem Werte gelesen werden sollen.
            config_file (str, optional): Pfad zur JSON-Konfigurationsdatei.
                Defaults to "new_config.json".

        Raises:
            AttributeError: Wenn die Datei nicht geladen werden kann.
        """
        self._config_file = config_file
        self._cfg_namespace = cfg_namespace
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._log = logger if logger is not None else logging.getLogger(self.__class__.__name__)
        self._load_config_file()

    def _load_config_file(self):
        """
        Lädt die JSON-Konfigurationsdatei in den Speicher.

        Raises:
            AttributeError: Wenn die Datei nicht existiert oder nicht lesbar ist.
        """
        self._log.debug(f"Lese Config-File {self._config_file}")
        try:
            with open(self._config_file, "r") as f:
                self._json_config = json.load(f)
                self._log.debug(f"Config-File gelesen {self._config_file}")
        except OSError as e:
            error = AttributeError(name=f"Datei {self._config_file} nicht verfügbar")
            self._log.error(error)
            raise error
        except Exception as e:
            self._log.error(e)
            raise e

    def get_config(self, force_update: bool = False) -> dict:
        """
        Gibt den Namespace-Abschnitt der Konfiguration zurück.

        Args:
            force_update (bool, optional): Wenn True, wird die Datei neu geladen.
                Defaults to False.

        Returns:
            dict: Der Konfigurationsabschnitt des angegebenen Namespaces.
        """
        with self._lock:
            if (force_update or self._json_config is None):
                self._load_config_file()
            return self._json_config.get(self._cfg_namespace, {})
    
    def set_config(self, attribut:str, values=any, save_config=False) -> bool:
        """
        Setzt einen Wert im Namespace und speichert optional die Datei.

        Args:
            attribut (str): Name des Konfigurationsparameters.
            values (Any): Neuer Wert, der gesetzt werden soll.
            save_config (bool, optional): Wenn True, wird die Datei gespeichert.
                Defaults to False.

        Returns:
            bool: True bei Erfolg, False bei Fehlern beim Speichern.
        """
        with self._lock:
            if (self._json_config is None):
                self._load_config_file()
            self._json_config.get(self._cfg_namespace, {})[attribut] = values
            return True if (not save_config) else self._save_config()
    
    def get(self, attribut:str, dv=None) -> None|Any:
        """
        Gibt einen beliebigen Wert aus der Konfiguration zurück.

        Args:
            attribut (str): Name des Parameters.
            dv (Any, optional): Default-Wert, falls der Parameter nicht existiert.
                Defaults to None.

        Returns:
            Any: Der gelesene Wert oder der Default-Wert.
        """      
        return self.get_config().get(attribut, dv)

    def get_list(self, attribut: str, default: Optional[list] = None) -> list:
        """
        Gibt eine Liste aus der Konfiguration zurück.

        Args:
            attribut (str): Name des Parameters.
            default (list, optional): Default-Liste, falls der Parameter nicht existiert.
                Defaults to None.

        Returns:
            list: Die gelesene Liste oder der Default-Wert.
        """
        dv = default if default is not None else []
        return self.get_config().get(attribut, dv)

    def get_int(self, attribut: str, dv: int) -> int:
        """
        Gibt einen Integer-Wert aus der Konfiguration zurück.

        Args:
            attribut (str): Name des Parameters.
            dv (int): Default-Wert, falls der Parameter nicht existiert.

        Returns:
            int: Der gelesene Integer-Wert.
        """
        return int(self.get_config().get(attribut, dv))

    def get_str(self, attribut: str, dv: str) -> str:
            """
            Gibt einen String-Wert aus der Konfiguration zurück.
    
            Args:
                attribut (str): Name des Parameters.
                dv (str): Default-Wert, falls der Parameter nicht existiert.
    
            Returns:
                str: Der gelesene String-Wert.
            """
            return str(self.get_config().get(attribut, dv))

    def get_bool(self, attribut: str, dv: bool) -> bool:
        """Liest boolesche Werte und bekannte Textrepräsentationen eindeutig."""
        value = self.get_config().get(attribut, dv)
        if isinstance(value, bool):
            return value
        if isinstance(value, str):
            normalized = value.strip().lower()
            if normalized in ("true", "1", "yes", "on"):
                return True
            if normalized in ("false", "0", "no", "off", ""):
                return False
            raise ValueError(f"Ungültiger boolescher Konfigurationswert: {value!r}")
        if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and value in (0, 1):
            return bool(value)
        raise ValueError(f"Ungültiger boolescher Konfigurationswert: {value!r}")

    def get_float(self, attribut: str, dv: float) -> float:
        """
        Gibt einen Float-Wert aus der Konfiguration zurück.

        Args:
            attribut (str): Name des Parameters.
            dv (float): Default-Wert, falls der Parameter nicht existiert.

        Returns:
            float: Der gelesene Float-Wert.
        """
        return float(self.get_config().get(attribut, dv))

    def _save_config(self) -> bool:
        """
        Speichert die aktuelle Konfiguration zurück in die JSON-Datei.

        Returns:
            bool: True bei Erfolg, False bei Fehlern beim Schreiben.
        """
        try:
            with open(self._config_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as jf:
                json.dump(self._json_config, jf, indent=4, ensure_ascii=True, sort_keys=True)
            return True
        except OSError as e:
            error = AttributeError(name=f"Datei {self._config_file} nicht verfügbar")
            self._log.error(error)
            raise error
        except Exception as e:
            self._log.error(e)
            raise e        


class Configurable():
    
    def __init__(self, config_reader:Optional[ConfigReader]=None):
        self._cfg = config_reader if config_reader is not None else ConfigReader("ir_sensors")

import logging
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime

import dash_bootstrap_components as dbc
import plotly.graph_objects as go
from dash import Dash, Input, Output, State, dcc, html

import Driving
import InfraredSensor
import UltrasonicSensor
import BaseCar
import SensorCar
import logging_setup
app = Dash(external_stylesheets=[dbc.themes.BOOTSTRAP])

logging_setup.setup_project_logging(logging.WARN)

bc = BaseCar.BaseCar()
sc = SensorCar.SensorCar()

ir = InfraredSensor.InfraredSensor(sc)
us = UltrasonicSensor.UltrasonicSensor(sc)

sensor_stop_event = threading.Event()
drive_stop_event = threading.Event()

mode_one = Driving.ModeOne(car=sc)
currentMode = mode_one
mode_two = Driving.ModeTwo(car=sc)
re = Driving.RoomExplorer(car=sc)
ao = Driving.ApproachObstacle(car=sc)
fl = Driving.FollowLine(car=sc)

us_sensor_thread = threading.Thread(
    target=us.read_loop,
    args=(sensor_stop_event,),
    daemon=True
)
ir_sensor_thread = threading.Thread(
    target=ir.read_loop,
    args=(sensor_stop_event,),
    daemon=True
)

us_sensor_thread.start()
ir_sensor_thread.start()

# Listen für Liveplot
plot_time = []
plot_distance = []
plot_speed = []
plot_steering_angle = []
plot_p_glied = []
plot_i_glied = []
plot_d_glied = []

measurement_active = False
measurement_start_time = None
measurement_max_time = 0
controller_thread = None
show_distance_plot = False
show_pid_plot = False
active_car = sc


def add_min_max_annotations(figure, values, unit=''):
    if len(values) > 0:
        max_value = max(values)
        min_value = min(values)
        max_text = f'Max: {max_value:.1f}{unit}'
        min_text = f'Min: {min_value:.1f}{unit}'
    else:
        max_text = 'Max: -'
        min_text = 'Min: -'

    figure.add_annotation(
        x=0.99,
        y=0.98,
        xref='paper',
        yref='paper',
        text=max_text,
        showarrow=False,
        xanchor='right',
        yanchor='top'
    )

    figure.add_annotation(
        x=0.99,
        y=0.02,
        xref='paper',
        yref='paper',
        text=min_text,
        showarrow=False,
        xanchor='right',
        yanchor='bottom'
    )


def create_figure(x_data, y_data, title, y_title, unit=''):
    figure = go.Figure()
    figure.add_trace(
        go.Scatter(
            x=x_data,
            y=y_data,
            mode='lines+markers'
        )
    )
    figure.update_layout(
        title=title,
        xaxis_title='Zeit [s]',
        yaxis_title=y_title,
    )
    add_min_max_annotations(figure, y_data, unit)
    return figure

@app.callback(
    Output('Time', 'children'),
    Output('Speed', 'children'),
    Output('Steer', 'children'),
    Output('g_distance', 'figure'),
    Output('g_speed', 'figure'),
    Output('g_angle', 'figure'),
    Output('g_p_glied', 'figure'),
    Output('g_i_glied', 'figure'),
    Output('g_d_glied', 'figure'),


    Output('g_distance', 'style'),

def update_values(n):
    global measurement_active, measurement_start_time, measurement_max_time
    global controller_thread, show_distance_plot, show_pid_plot, active_car

    current_time = datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S')

    speed = active_car.speed * active_car.direction
    steering_angle = active_car.steering_angle

    if show_pid_plot:
        p_glied = sc.p_wert
        i_glied = sc.i_wert
        d_glied = sc.d_wert
    else:
        p_glied = '-'
        i_glied = '-'
        d_glied = '-'

    # Fülle Liste während des Durchlaufs
    if measurement_active and measurement_start_time is not None:
        elapsed = time.time() - measurement_start_time

        plot_time.append(elapsed)
        plot_speed.append(speed)
        plot_steering_angle.append(steering_angle)

        if show_distance_plot:
            plot_distance.append(sc.distance)

        if show_pid_plot:
            plot_p_glied.append(sc.p_wert)
            plot_i_glied.append(sc.i_wert)
            plot_d_glied.append(sc.d_wert)

        # Stoppe wenn Zeit überschritten
        if elapsed >= measurement_max_time:
            drive_stop_event.set()
            measurement_active = False
            active_car.stop()

    # Wenn vorbei, ändere Status
    if controller_thread is not None and not controller_thread.is_alive():
        measurement_active = False

    figure_speed = create_figure(
        plot_time,
        plot_speed,
        'Geschwindigkeit über Zeit',
        'Geschwindigkeit'
    )

    figure_angle = create_figure(
        plot_time,
        plot_steering_angle,
        'Lenkwinkel über Zeit',
        'Lenkwinkel [°]',
        ' °'
    )

    figure_distance = create_figure(
        plot_time[:len(plot_distance)],
        plot_distance,
        'Distanz über Zeit',
        'Distanz [cm]',
        ' cm'
    )
    ### PID Plots
    figure_p_glied = create_figure(
        plot_time[:len(plot_p_glied)],
        plot_p_glied,
        'P-Glied über Zeit',
        'P-Glied [ ]',
        '-'
    )

    figure_i_glied = create_figure(
        plot_time[:len(plot_i_glied)],
        plot_i_glied,
        'I-Glied über Zeit',
        'I-Glied [ ]',
        '-'
    )

    figure_d_glied = create_figure(
        plot_time[:len(plot_d_glied)],
        plot_d_glied,
        'D-Glied über Zeit',
        'D-Glied []',
        '-'
    )

    if show_distance_plot:
        distance_style = {'display': 'block'}
    else:
        distance_style = {'display': 'none'}

    if show_pid_plot:
        pid_style = {'display': 'block'}
    else:
        pid_style = {'display': 'none'}

    return (
        f'Time: {current_time}',
        f'Speed: {speed}',
        f'Steer: {steering_angle}',
        figure_distance,
        figure_speed,
        figure_angle,
        figure_p_glied,
        figure_i_glied,
        figure_d_glied,
        distance_style,
        pid_style,
        pid_style,
        pid_style
    )

@app.callback(
    Output('Status', 'children'),
    Output('Distance', 'children'),
    Input('button_start_fahrmodi', 'n_clicks'),
    State('dropdown_fahrmodus', 'value'),
    prevent_initial_call=True
)
def start_fahrmodus(clicks, fahrmodus):
    global measurement_active, measurement_start_time, measurement_max_time
    global controller_thread, show_distance_plot, show_pid_plot, active_car, currentMode

    plot_time.clear()
    plot_distance.clear()
    plot_speed.clear()
    plot_steering_angle.clear()

    drive_stop_event.clear()

    if fahrmodus == 'fahrmodus_1':
        print('Starte Fahrmodus 1')
        if (currentMode is not None):
            currentMode.stop()
            time.sleep(0.25)
        currentMode = mode_one

        active_car = bc

        measurement_start_time = time.time()
        measurement_max_time = currentMode._cfg.get_int('forward_backward_max_time', 10)
        measurement_active = True
        show_distance_plot = False
        show_pid_plot = False

        currentMode.start()
        return 'Fahrmodus 1 gestartet', f'Distanz: {11111}'

    elif fahrmodus == 'fahrmodus_2':
        print('Starte Fahrmodus 2')
        if (currentMode is not None):
            currentMode.stop()
            time.sleep(0.25)
        currentMode = mode_two
        active_car = bc

        measurement_max_time = currentMode._cfg.get_int('forward_backward_max_time', 10)
        measurement_active = True
        show_distance_plot = False
        show_pid_plot = False

        currentMode.start()
        return 'Fahrmodus 2 gestartet', f'Distanz: {11111}'

    elif fahrmodus == 'fahrmodus_3':
        print('Starte Fahrmodus 3')
        if (currentMode is not None):
            currentMode.stop()
            time.sleep(0.25)
        
        currentMode = ao

        active_car = sc

        measurement_max_time = currentMode._cfg.get_int('explorer_max_time', 30)
        measurement_active = True
        show_distance_plot = True
        show_pid_plot = False

        ao.start()

        return 'Fahrmodus 3 gestartet', f'Distanz: {11111}'

    elif fahrmodus == 'fahrmodus_4':
        print('Starte Fahrmodus 4')
        if (currentMode is not None):
            currentMode.stop()
            time.sleep(0.25)

        currentMode = re

        active_car = sc

        measurement_max_time = currentMode._cfg.get_int('explorer_max_time', 30)
        measurement_active = True
        show_distance_plot = True
        show_pid_plot = False

        re.start()
        return 'Fahrmodus 4 gestartet', f'Distanz: {11111}'

    elif fahrmodus == 'fahrmodus_5':
        print('Starte Fahrmodus 5')
        if (currentMode is not None):
            currentMode.stop()
            time.sleep(0.25)


        currentMode = fl

        active_car = sc

        measurement_active = True
        show_distance_plot = False
        show_pid_plot = True

        currentMode.start()

        return 'Fahrmodus 5 noch nicht umgesetzt', f'Distanz: {11111}'

    elif fahrmodus == 'fahrmodus_6':
        print('Starte Fahrmodus 6')

        measurement_active = True
        show_distance_plot = False
        show_pid_plot = True

        return 'Fahrmodus 6 noch nicht umgesetzt', f'Distanz: {11111}'

    elif fahrmodus == 'fahrmodus_7':
        print('Starte Fahrmodus 7')

        measurement_active = True
        show_distance_plot = False
        show_pid_plot = True

        return 'Fahrmodus 7 noch nicht umgesetzt', f'Distanz: {11111}'

    return 'Nix passiert', f'Distanz: {0}'


@app.callback(
    Output('button_stop_fahrmodi', 'children'),
    Input('button_stop_fahrmodi', 'n_clicks'),
    prevent_initial_call=True
)
def stop_fahrmodus(clicks):
    global measurement_active, active_car

    drive_stop_event.set()
    measurement_active = False
    active_car.stop()

    if (currentMode is not None):
        currentMode.stop()

    return 'Stop'


if __name__ == '__main__':
    app.run(host='0.0.0.0', debug=True)

================================================================================
FILE: InfraredSensor.py
