class Servo(object):
    """Servo driver class"""

    _MIN_PULSE_WIDTH = 600
    _MAX_PULSE_WIDTH = 2400
    _DEFAULT_PULSE_WIDTH = 1500
    _FREQUENCY = 60

    _DEBUG = False
    _DEBUG_INFO = 'DEBUG "Servo.py":'

    def __init__(self, channel, offset=0, lock=True, bus_number=1, address=0x40):
        """ Init a servo on specific channel, this offset """
        if channel < 0 or channel > 16:
            raise ValueError('Servo channel "{0}" is not in (0, 15).'.format(channel))
        self._debug_("Debug on")
        self.channel = channel
        self.offset = offset
        self.lock = lock

        self.pwm = PWM(bus_number=bus_number, address=address)
        self.frequency = self._FREQUENCY
        self.write(90)

    def _debug_(self, message):
        if self._DEBUG:
            print(self._DEBUG_INFO, message)

    def setup(self):
        self.pwm.setup()

    def _angle_to_analog(self, angle):
        """ Calculate 12-bit analog value from giving angle """
        pulse_wide = self.pwm.map(
            angle, 0, 180, self._MIN_PULSE_WIDTH, self._MAX_PULSE_WIDTH
        )
        analog_value = int(float(pulse_wide) / 1000000 * self.frequency * 4096)
        self._debug_("Angle %d equals Analog_value %d" % (angle, analog_value))
        return analog_value

    @property
    def frequency(self):
        return self._frequency

    @frequency.setter
    def frequency(self, value):
        self._frequency = value
        self.pwm.frequency = value

    @property
    def offset(self):
        return self._offset

    @offset.setter
    def offset(self, value):
        """ Set offset for much user-friendly """
        self._offset = value
        self._debug_("Set offset to %d" % self.offset)

    def write(self, angle):
        """ Turn the servo with giving angle. """
        if self.lock:
            if angle > 180:
                angle = 180
            if angle < 0:
                angle = 0
        else:
            if angle < 0 or angle > 180:
                raise ValueError(
                    'Servo "{0}" turn angle "{1}" is not in (0, 180).'.format(
                        self.channel, angle
                    )
                )
        val = self._angle_to_analog(angle)
        val += self.offset
        self.pwm.write(self.channel, 0, val)
        self._debug_("Turn angle = %d" % angle)

    @property
    def debug(self):
        return self._DEBUG

    @debug.setter
    def debug(self, debug):
        """ Set if debug information shows """
        if debug in (True, False):
            self._DEBUG = debug
        else:
            raise ValueError(
                'debug must be "True" (Set debug on) or "False" (Set debug off), not "{0}"'.format(
                    debug
                )
            )

        if self._DEBUG:
            print(self._DEBUG_INFO, "Set debug on")
        else:
            print(self._DEBUG_INFO, "Set debug off")


"""
taken from
**********************************************************************
* Filename    : TB6612.py
* Description : A driver module for TB6612
* Author      : Cavon
* Brand       : SunFounder
* E-mail      : service@sunfounder.com
* Website     : www.sunfounder.com
* Update      : Cavon    2016-09-23    New release

# import RPi.GPIO as GPIO


class Motor(object):
    """Motor driver class
    Set direction_channel to the GPIO channel which connect to MA,
    Set motor_B to the GPIO channel which connect to MB,
    Both GPIO channel use BCM numbering;
    Set pwm_channel to the PWM channel which connect to PWMA,
    Set pwm_B to the PWM channel which connect to PWMB;
    PWM channel using PCA9685, Set pwm_address to your address, if is not 0x40
    Set debug to True to print out debug informations.
    """

    _DEBUG = False
    _DEBUG_INFO = 'DEBUG "TB6612.py":'

    def __init__(self, direction_channel, pwm=None, offset=True):
        """Init a motor on giving dir. channel and PWM channel."""
        self._debug_("Debug on")
        self.direction_channel = direction_channel
        self._pwm = pwm
        self._offset = offset
        self.forward_offset = self._offset

        self.backward_offset = not self.forward_offset
        self._speed = 0

        GPIO.setwarnings(False)
        GPIO.setmode(GPIO.BCM)

        self._debug_("setup motor direction channel at %s" % direction_channel)
        self._debug_(
            "setup motor pwm channel"
        )  # self._debug_('setup motor pwm channel as %s ' % self._pwm.__name__)
        GPIO.setup(self.direction_channel, GPIO.OUT)

    def _debug_(self, message):
        if self._DEBUG:
            print(self._DEBUG_INFO, message)

    @property
    def speed(self):
        return self._speed

    @speed.setter
    def speed(self, speed):
        """ Set Speed with giving value """
        if speed not in range(0, 101):
            raise ValueError('speed ranges fron 0 to 100, not "{0}"'.format(speed))
        if not callable(self._pwm):
            raise ValueError(
                "pwm is not callable, please set Motor.pwm to a pwm control function with only 1 veriable speed"
            )
        self._debug_("Set speed to: %s" % speed)
        self._speed = speed
        self._pwm(self._speed)

    def forward(self):
        """ Set the motor direction to forward """
        GPIO.output(self.direction_channel, self.forward_offset)
        self.speed = self._speed
        self._debug_("Motor moving forward (%s)" % str(self.forward_offset))

    def backward(self):
        """ Set the motor direction to backward """
        GPIO.output(self.direction_channel, self.backward_offset)
        self.speed = self._speed
        self._debug_("Motor moving backward (%s)" % str(self.backward_offset))

    def stop(self):
        """ Stop the motor by giving a 0 speed """
        self._debug_("Motor stop")
        self.speed = 0

    @property
    def offset(self):
        return self._offset

    @offset.setter
    def offset(self, value):
        """ Set offset for much user-friendly """
        if value not in (True, False):
            raise ValueError('offset value must be Bool value, not"{0}"'.format(value))
        self.forward_offset = value
        self.backward_offset = not self.forward_offset
        self._debug_("Set offset to %d" % self._offset)

    @property
    def debug(self, debug):
        return self._DEBUG

    @debug.setter
    def debug(self, debug):
        """ Set if debug information shows """
        if debug in (True, False):
            self._DEBUG = debug
        else:
            raise ValueError(
                'debug must be "True" (Set debug on) or "False" (Set debug off), not "{0}"'.format(
                    debug
                )
            )

        if self._DEBUG:
            print(self._DEBUG_INFO, "Set debug on")
        else:
            print(self._DEBUG_INFO, "Set debug off")

    @property
    def pwm(self):
        return self._pwm

    @pwm.setter
    def pwm(self, pwm):
        self._debug_("pwm set")
        self._pwm = pwm


"""
taken from
**********************************************************************
* Filename    : PCA9685.py
* Description : A driver module for PCA9685
* Author      : Cavon
* Brand       : SunFounder
* E-mail      : service@sunfounder.com
* Website     : www.sunfounder.com
* Version     : v2.0.0
**********************************************************************
"""
# import smbus
# import time
# import math


class PWM(object):
    """A PWM control class for PCA9685."""

    _MODE1 = 0x00
    _MODE2 = 0x01
    _SUBADR1 = 0x02
    _SUBADR2 = 0x03
    _SUBADR3 = 0x04
    _PRESCALE = 0xFE
    _LED0_ON_L = 0x06
    _LED0_ON_H = 0x07
    _LED0_OFF_L = 0x08
    _LED0_OFF_H = 0x09
    _ALL_LED_ON_L = 0xFA
    _ALL_LED_ON_H = 0xFB
    _ALL_LED_OFF_L = 0xFC
    _ALL_LED_OFF_H = 0xFD

    _RESTART = 0x80
    _SLEEP = 0x10
    _ALLCALL = 0x01
    _INVRT = 0x10
    _OUTDRV = 0x04

    _DEBUG = False
    _DEBUG_INFO = 'DEBUG "PCA9685.py":'

    def __init__(self, bus_number=1, address=0x40):
        self.address = address
        self.bus_number = bus_number
        self.bus = smbus.SMBus(self.bus_number)

    def _debug_(self, message):
        if self._DEBUG:
            print(self._DEBUG_INFO, message)

    def setup(self):
        """Init the class with bus_number and address"""
        self._debug_("Reseting PCA9685 MODE1 (without SLEEP) and MODE2")
        self.write_all_value(0, 0)
        self._write_byte_data(self._MODE2, self._OUTDRV)
        self._write_byte_data(self._MODE1, self._ALLCALL)
        time.sleep(0.005)

        mode1 = self._read_byte_data(self._MODE1)
        mode1 = mode1 & ~self._SLEEP
        self._write_byte_data(self._MODE1, mode1)
        time.sleep(0.005)
        self._frequency = 60

    def _write_byte_data(self, reg, value):
        """Write data to I2C with self.address"""
        self._debug_("Writing value %2X to %2X" % (value, reg))
        try:
            self.bus.write_byte_data(self.address, reg, value)
        except Exception as i:
            print(i)
            self._check_i2c()

    def _read_byte_data(self, reg):
        """Read data from I2C with self.address"""
        self._debug_("Reading value from %2X" % reg)
        try:
            results = self.bus.read_byte_data(self.address, reg)
            return results
        except Exception as i:
            print(i)
            self._check_i2c()

    def _run_command(self, cmd):
        import subprocess

        p = subprocess.Popen(
            cmd, shell=True, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT
        )
        result = p.stdout.read().decode("utf-8")
        status = p.poll()
        # print(result)
        # print(status)
        return status, result

    def _check_i2c(self):
        from os import listdir

        print("I2C bus number is: %s" % self.bus_number)
        print("Checking I2C device:")
        devices = listdir("/dev/")
        if "i2c-%d" % self.bus_number in devices:
            print("I2C device exist.")
        else:
            print(
                "Seems like I2C have not been set, run 'sudo raspi-config' to enable I2C"
            )
        cmd = "i2cdetect -y %s" % self.bus_number
        _, output = self._run_command(cmd)
        print("Your PCA9685 address is set to 0x%02X" % self.address)
        print("i2cdetect output:")
        print(output)
        outputs = output.split("\n")[1:]
        addresses = []
        for tmp_addresses in outputs:
            tmp_addresses = tmp_addresses.split(":")
            if len(tmp_addresses) < 2:
                continue
            else:
                tmp_addresses = tmp_addresses[1]
            tmp_addresses = tmp_addresses.strip().split(" ")
            for address in tmp_addresses:
                if address != "--":
                    addresses.append(address)
        print("Conneceted i2c device:")
        if addresses == []:
            print("None")
        else:
            for address in addresses:
                print("  0x%s" % address)
        if "%02X" % self.address in addresses:
            print(
                "Wierd, I2C device is connected, Try to run the program again, If problem stills, email this information to support@sunfounder.com"
            )
        else:
            print("Device is missing.")
            print(
                "Check the address or wiring of PCA9685 Server driver, or email this information to support@sunfounder.com"
            )
            quit()

    @property
    def frequency(self):
        return self._frequency

    @frequency.setter
    def frequency(self, freq):
        """Set PWM frequency"""
        self._debug_("Set frequency to %d" % freq)
        self._frequency = freq
        prescale_value = 25000000.0
        prescale_value /= 4096.0
        prescale_value /= float(freq)
        prescale_value -= 1.0
        self._debug_("Setting PWM frequency to %d Hz" % freq)
        self._debug_("Estimated pre-scale: %d" % prescale_value)
        prescale = math.floor(prescale_value + 0.5)
        self._debug_("Final pre-scale: %d" % prescale)

        old_mode = self._read_byte_data(self._MODE1)
        new_mode = (old_mode & 0x7F) | 0x10
        self._write_byte_data(self._MODE1, new_mode)
        self._write_byte_data(self._PRESCALE, int(math.floor(prescale)))
        self._write_byte_data(self._MODE1, old_mode)
        time.sleep(0.005)
        self._write_byte_data(self._MODE1, old_mode | 0x80)

    def write(self, channel, on, off):
        """Set on and off value on specific channel"""
        self._debug_('Set channel "%d" to value "%d"' % (channel, off))
        self._write_byte_data(self._LED0_ON_L + 4 * channel, on & 0xFF)
        self._write_byte_data(self._LED0_ON_H + 4 * channel, on >> 8)
        self._write_byte_data(self._LED0_OFF_L + 4 * channel, off & 0xFF)
        self._write_byte_data(self._LED0_OFF_H + 4 * channel, off >> 8)

    def write_all_value(self, on, off):
        """Set on and off value on all channel"""
        self._debug_('Set all channel to value "%d"' % (off))
        self._write_byte_data(self._ALL_LED_ON_L, on & 0xFF)
        self._write_byte_data(self._ALL_LED_ON_H, on >> 8)
        self._write_byte_data(self._ALL_LED_OFF_L, off & 0xFF)
        self._write_byte_data(self._ALL_LED_OFF_H, off >> 8)

    def map(self, x, in_min, in_max, out_min, out_max):
        """To map the value from arange to another"""
        return (x - in_min) * (out_max - out_min) / (in_max - in_min) + out_min

    @property
    def debug(self):
        return self._DEBUG

    @debug.setter
    def debug(self, debug):
        """Set if debug information shows"""
        if debug in (True, False):
            self._DEBUG = debug
        else:
            raise ValueError(
                'debug must be "True" (Set debug on) or "False" (Set debug off), not "{0}"'.format(
                    debug
                )
            )

        if self._DEBUG:
            print(self._DEBUG_INFO, "Set debug on")
        else:
            print(self._DEBUG_INFO, "Set debug off")


@click.command()
@click.option(
    "--modus", "--m", type=int, default=None, help="Startet Test für Klasse direkt."
)
def main(modus):
    """
    Test der Basisklassen
    """
    print("-- DEMO BASISKLASSEN--------------------")
    modi = {
        0: "Ausrichtung der Servo der Lenkung",
        1: "Test Hinterräder - Antrieb / Klasse: Back_Wheels",
        2: "Test Vorderräder - Lenkung / Klasse: Front_Wheels",
        3: "Test Ultraschallmodul / Klasse: Ultrasonic",
        4: "Test Infrarotmodul / Klasse: Infrared",
    }

    if modus == None:
        print("--" * 20)
        print("Auswahl:")
        for m in modi.keys():
            print("{i} - {name}".format(i=m, name=modi[m]))
        print("--" * 20)

    while modus == None:
        modus = input("Wähle  (Andere Taste für Abbruch): ? ")
        if modus in ["1", "2", "3", "4"]:
            break
        else:
            modus = None
            print("Getroffene Auswahl nicht möglich.")
            quit()
    modus = int(modus)

    if modus == 0:
        print("Ausrichtung der Vorderräder")
        fw = FrontWheels()
        fw.turn(45)
        time.sleep(0.5)
        fw.turn(135)
        time.sleep(0.5)
        print(
            "Servo der Lenkung auf 90 Grad/geradeaus ausgerichtet! (CRTL-C zum beenden)"
        )
        while True:
            fw.turn(90)
            time.sleep(1)

    if modus == 1:
        x = input(
            "ACHTUNG! Das Auto wird ein Stück fahren!\n Dücken Sie ENTER zum Start."
        )
        print("Test Hinterräder")
        if x == "":
            bw = BackWheels()
            bw.test()
        else:
            print("Abruch.")

    if modus == 2:
        print("Test Vorderräder")
        fw = FrontWheels()
        fw.test()

    if modus == 3:
        print("Test Ultrasonic")
        usm = Ultrasonic()
        usm.test()

    if modus == 4:
        print("Test Infrared")
        irm = Infrared()
        irm.test()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Information: Module mit Basisklasse fuer Camera fuer Projekt RPiCar
# File name: basisklassen_cam.py
# Author (Original): Robert Heise (FIDA)
# Date created: 11/10/2021
# Date last modified: 18/10/2026 (Picamera2 / Debian 13)
# Usage: U4I/FIDA "Autonomes Fahren" Sunfounder PiCar-S

import cv2
import numpy as np


class Camera:
    def __init__(
        self,
        devicenumber: int = 0,
        buffersize: int = 1,
        skip_frame: int = 0,
        height: int = None,
        width: int = None,
        flip: bool = True,
        colorspace: str = "bgr",
        backend: str = "auto",
    ) -> None:
        """Initializes Camera.

        Args:
            devicenumber (int): Kameranummer (je nach Backend). Defaults to 0.
            buffersize (int): OpenCV-Puffergroesse bzw. mindestens zwei
                Aufnahmebuffer bei Picamera2. Defaults to 1.
            skip_frame (int): Anzahl zu verwerfender Frames. Defaults to 0.
            height (int): Bildhoehe. None verwendet die Kameravorgabe.
            width (int): Bildbreite. None verwendet die Kameravorgabe.
            flip (bool): Dreht das Bild um 180 Grad. Defaults to True.
            colorspace (str): 'bgr', 'rgb' oder 'gray'. Defaults to 'bgr'.
            backend (str): 'auto', 'picamera2' oder 'opencv'.
                'auto' bevorzugt Picamera2 fuer CSI-Kameras und verwendet
                OpenCV fuer USB-/V4L2-Kameras.
        """
        if backend not in ("auto", "picamera2", "opencv"):
            raise ValueError("backend muss 'auto', 'picamera2' oder 'opencv' sein")
        if (colorspace or "bgr").lower() not in ("bgr", "rgb", "gray"):
            raise ValueError("colorspace muss 'bgr', 'rgb' oder 'gray' sein")
        if buffersize < 1 or skip_frame < 0:
            raise ValueError("buffersize muss >= 1 und skip_frame muss >= 0 sein")
        if width is not None and width <= 0:
            raise ValueError("width muss positiv sein")
        if height is not None and height <= 0:
            raise ValueError("height muss positiv sein")
        if devicenumber < 0:
            raise ValueError("devicenumber muss >= 0 sein")

        self.__devicenumber = devicenumber
        self.__flip = flip
        self.__colorspace = (colorspace or "bgr").lower()
        self.__skip_frame = int(skip_frame)
        self.__VideoCapture = None
        self.__backend = None
        self.__is_active = False
        self.__imgsize = (0, 0)

        # Fuer CSI-Kameras auf aktuellen Raspberry-Pi-Systemen wird
        # Picamera2/libcamera statt cv2.VideoCapture verwendet.
        if backend in ("auto", "picamera2"):
            try:
                from picamera2 import Picamera2
            except ImportError as exc:
                if backend == "picamera2":
                    raise RuntimeError(
                        "Picamera2/libcamera kann nicht importiert werden. "
                        "Auf Raspberry Pi OS: sudo apt install python3-picamera2"
                    ) from exc
            else:
                use_picamera2 = backend == "picamera2"
                if backend == "auto":
                    cameras = Picamera2.global_camera_info()
                    if devicenumber < len(cameras):
                        # USB-Kameras weiterhin ueber OpenCV ansprechen.
                        camera_id = str(cameras[devicenumber].get("Id", ""))
                        use_picamera2 = "usb" not in camera_id.lower()

                if use_picamera2:
                    self.__backend = "picamera2"
                    try:
                        self.__VideoCapture = Picamera2(devicenumber)
                        # RGB888 ergibt bei Picamera2 ein BGR-NumPy-Array,
                        # also dieselbe Kanalreihenfolge wie cv2.VideoCapture.
                        main = {"format": "RGB888"}
                        buffer_count = max(2, int(buffersize))
                        config = self.__VideoCapture.create_preview_configuration(
                            main=main, buffer_count=buffer_count, queue=False
                        )
                        if width is not None or height is not None:
                            default_width, default_height = config["main"]["size"]
                            main["size"] = (
                                width if width is not None else default_width,
                                height if height is not None else default_height,
                            )
                            config = self.__VideoCapture.create_preview_configuration(
                                main=main, buffer_count=buffer_count, queue=False
                            )
                        self.__VideoCapture.configure(config)
                        self.__imgsize = tuple(self.__VideoCapture.camera_configuration()["main"]["size"])
                        self.__VideoCapture.start()
                        self.__is_active = True
                    except Exception as exc:
                        self.release()
                        raise RuntimeError(f"Picamera2-Kamera {devicenumber} konnte nicht gestartet werden") from exc

        # OpenCV bleibt fuer USB-Webcams und andere V4L2-Geraete verfuegbar.
        if self.__VideoCapture is None:
            self.__backend = "opencv"
            self.__VideoCapture = cv2.VideoCapture(devicenumber)
            if not self.__VideoCapture.isOpened():
                self.release()
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"OpenCV-Kamera {devicenumber} konnte nicht geoeffnet werden. "
                    "Fuer eine CSI-Kamera ggf. backend='picamera2' verwenden."
                )
            if width is not None:
                self.__VideoCapture.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH, width)
            if height is not None:
                self.__VideoCapture.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT, height)
            self.__VideoCapture.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_BUFFERSIZE, buffersize)
            self.__imgsize = (
                int(self.__VideoCapture.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH)),
                int(self.__VideoCapture.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT)),
            )
            self.__is_active = True

    def get_size(self) -> tuple:
        """Returns (width, height) of the configured camera image."""
        return self.__imgsize

    def get_frame(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """Returns the current frame as NumPy array with transformations applied.

        Raises:
            RuntimeError: Camera is not accessible or frame capture failed.
        """
        if not self.check():
            raise RuntimeError("Camera is not accessible or was already released")

        try:
            if self.__backend == "picamera2":
                for _ in range(self.__skip_frame):
                    self.__VideoCapture.capture_array("main")
                frame = self.__VideoCapture.capture_array("main")
            else:
                for _ in range(self.__skip_frame):
                    if not self.__VideoCapture.grab():
                        raise RuntimeError("Could not skip video frame")
                success, frame = self.__VideoCapture.read()
                if not success:
                    raise RuntimeError("Could not read video frame")
        except Exception as exc:
            raise RuntimeError("Could not capture video frame") from exc

        if frame is None or frame.size == 0:
            raise RuntimeError("Camera returned an empty frame")

        if self.__flip:
            frame = cv2.flip(frame, -1)  # bewusst beide Achsen / 180 Grad
        if self.__colorspace == "rgb":
            frame = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
        elif self.__colorspace == "gray":
            frame = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        return frame

    def check(self) -> bool:
        """Returns True if the camera is initialized and not released."""
        if self.__VideoCapture is None or not self.__is_active:
            return False
        if self.__backend == "opencv":
            return self.__VideoCapture.isOpened()
        return True

    def release(self) -> None:
        """Releases camera so that other processes can access it."""
        camera = self.__VideoCapture
        if camera is None:
            return
        was_active = self.__is_active
        self.__VideoCapture = None
        self.__is_active = False
        if self.__backend == "picamera2":
            try:
                if was_active:
                    camera.stop()
            finally:
                camera.close()
        else:
            camera.release()

    def __enter__(self):
        """Allows use of Camera as context manager."""
        return self

    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_value, traceback):
        self.release()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    # Funktioniert auch ohne Desktop/Display (z. B. bei SSH-Zugriff).
    with Camera() as cam:
        img = cam.get_frame()
        print("Image size:", cam.get_size())
        cv2.imwrite("camera_test.jpg", img)
        print("Bild gespeichert: camera_test.jpg")

"""
Alternative Klasse für die Kamera für die Verwendung auf RPIOS Bullseye 
"""


# import click
# import time
import numpy as np
from picamera2 import Picamera2
import cv2
import libcamera


class Camera(object):
    """Klasse für die Abfrage der Kamera mittels OpenCV

    Args:
        devicenumber int: Identifier for camera (OpenCV VideoCapture)
        buffersize int : Größe des Videobuffers (OpenCV VideoCapture)
        skip_frame int : Anzahl der zu verwerfenden Bilder bei Ausführung von get_frame
        height, width int: Höhe und Breite des Bildes
        flip bool: vertical flip of taken image
        colorspace str: Verwendeter Farbraum ('bgr','rgb', 'gray')
    Methoden:
        get_frame: Rückgabe eines Bildes als np.array unter Verwendung von skip_frame
        get_jpeg: Rückgabe eines Bildes als jpeg
        release: Freigabe der Kamera
        get_size (int,int): Rückgabe des Bildgröße
        get_size: Rückgabe der verwendeten Bildgröße
        check bool: Rückgabe von True wenn Kamera erreichbar

    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        buffersize: int = 1,
        skip_frame: int = 2,
        height: int = None,
        width: int = None,
        hflip: bool = True,
        vflip=True,
        colorspace: str = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.__skip_frame = skip_frame
        self.__PiCamera = Picamera2()
        self.__imgsize = self.__PiCamera.preview_configuration.size
        self.__width = width or self.__imgsize[0]
        self.__height = height or self.__imgsize[1]
        self.__hflip = hflip
        self.__vflip = vflip
        self.__colorspace = colorspace or "bgr"
        self.__config = self.__PiCamera.create_still_configuration(
            main={"size": (self.__width, self.__height), "format": "RGB888"},
            transform=libcamera.Transform(
                hflip=int(self.__hflip), vflip=int(self.__vflip)
            ),
            buffer_count=buffersize,
        )
        self.__PiCamera.configure(self.__config)
        self.__PiCamera.start()

    def get_size(self) -> tuple:
        """
        Return size of image returned be get_frame
        """
        return self.__imgsize

    def get_frame(self) -> np.array:
        """
        Reads frame from camera, applies tranformations to it and returns die resulting frame
        """
        if self.__skip_frame:
            for i in range(int(self.__skip_frame)):
                frame = self.__PiCamera.capture_array()
        frame = self.__PiCamera.capture_array()
        if self.__colorspace == "rgb":
            frame = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
        elif self.__colorspace == "gray":
            frame = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        else:
            pass
        return frame

    def get_jpeg(self) -> np.array:
        """
        Reads frame from camera via get_frame and return the frame in jpg-format
        Primarily used for video-streaming
        """
        frame = self.get_frame()
        _, jpeg = cv2.imencode(".jpeg", frame)
        return jpeg

    def check(self) -> bool:
        """
        Test for accessibility of the camera
        """
        flag = self.__PiCamera.is_open
        return flag

    def release(self) -> None:
        """
        Releases camera and allows other processes to access it
        """
        self.__PiCamera.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":

    cam = Camera()
    while True:
        img = cam.get_frame()
        cv2.imshow("image", img)
        if cv2.waitKey(1) & 0xFF == ord("q"):
            break
    cam.release()
    print(" - camera released")
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

import cv2
import dash_bootstrap_components as dbc
import numpy as np
from basisklassen_cam import Camera
from dash import Dash, Input, Output, dcc, html
from flask import Flask, Response

external_stylesheets = [dbc.themes.CERULEAN]
server = Flask(__name__)
app = Dash(external_stylesheets=external_stylesheets, server=server)


@server.route("/test")
def unterseite_test():
    return "Hier ist die Testseite"


cam = Camera()

hsv_range = {
    "lower": (90, 20, 50),
    "upper": (120, 255, 255),
}


def generate_stream(cam):
    while True:
        frame = cam.get_frame()
        resized = cv2.resize(frame, None, fx=0.5, fy=0.5)

        # 1. HSV-Farbfilter
        hsv = cv2.cvtColor(resized, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)

        lower = np.array(hsv_range["lower"], dtype=np.uint8)
        upper = np.array(hsv_range["upper"], dtype=np.uint8)
        filtered = cv2.inRange(hsv, lower, upper)

        # 2. Canny Edge Detection
        edges = cv2.Canny(filtered, 50, 150)

        # 3. Hough Lines P
        lines = cv2.HoughLinesP(
            edges,
            rho=1,
            theta=np.pi / 180,
            threshold=50,
            minLineLength=40,
            maxLineGap=10,
        )

        # 4. Erkannte Linien einzeichnen
        if lines is not None:
            for line in lines:
                x1, y1, x2, y2 = line[0]
                cv2.line(resized, (x1, y1), (x2, y2), (0, 255, 0), 2)

        # 5. Stream erzeugen
        _, frame_as_jpeg = cv2.imencode(".jpeg", resized)
        frame_in_bytes = frame_as_jpeg.tobytes()
        frame_as_string = (
            b"--frame\r\nContent-Type: image/jpeg\r\n\r\n"
            + frame_in_bytes
            + b"\r\n\r\n"
        )
        yield frame_as_string


@server.route("/stream")
def stream():
    return Response(
        generate_stream(cam),
        mimetype="multipart/x-mixed-replace; boundary=frame",
    )


app.layout = dbc.Container(
    [
        dbc.Row([html.H1("Projektphase 2", id="title")]),
        dbc.Row(
            [
                dbc.Col([html.Img(src="/stream")]),
                dbc.Col(
                    [
                        dcc.RangeSlider(
                            0, 179, value=[90, 120], id="h-slider"
                        ),
                        dcc.RangeSlider(
                            0, 255, value=[20, 255], id="s-slider"
                        ),
                        dcc.RangeSlider(
                            0, 255, value=[50, 255], id="v-slider"
                        ),
                    ]
                ),
            ]
        ),
    ]
)


@app.callback(
    Output("title", "children"),
    Input("h-slider", "value"),
    Input("s-slider", "value"),
    Input("v-slider", "value"),
)
def update_filter(h, s, v):
    hsv_range["lower"] = (h[0], s[0], v[0])
    hsv_range["upper"] = (h[1], s[1], v[1])

    return f"Projektphase 2 - H: {h}, S: {s}, V: {v}"


if __name__ == "__main__":
    app.run(host="0.0.0.0")

from dash import Dash, html, dcc, Input, Output
import dash_bootstrap_components as dbc
from flask import Flask, Response
from ShowCar.basisklassen_cam import Camera
import cv2
import numpy as np

external_stylesheets = [dbc.themes.BOOTSTRAP]
server = Flask(__name__)

app = Dash(__name__, external_stylesheets=external_stylesheets, server=server)

cam = Camera()


def generate_stream(camera_instance):
    while True:
        frame = camera_instance.get_frame()
        gray = cv2.cvtColor(frame, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        canny = cv2.Canny(gray, 100, 200)
        stacked = np.hstack([gray, canny])
        _, x = cv2.imencode(".jpeg", stacked)
        x_bytes = x.tobytes()

        x_string = (b'--frame\r\n'
                b'Content-Type: image/jpeg\r\n\r\n' + x_bytes + b'\r\n\r\n')
        
        yield x_string
        

@server.route("/video_feed")
def video_feed():
   return Response(generate_stream(cam), mimetype='multipart/x-mixed-replace; boundary=frame')


# Reine Optik
app.layout = html.Div(children=[
    html.H1("Hallo Projektphase 2"),
    dbc.Row([
        dbc.Col( html.Div([html.Img(src="/video_feed", id="videofeed", style={'height':'200px'})])), 
        dbc.Col([html.H3("Hier wird der Slider Text stehen", id="slider-text"),]),
        dbc.Col([
            dbc.Row([dcc.RangeSlider(id="h-slider", min=0, max=180, value=[20, 50])]),
            dbc.Row([dcc.RangeSlider(id="s-slider", min=0, max=180, value=[20, 50])]),
            dbc.Row([dcc.RangeSlider(id="v-slider", min=0, max=180, value=[20, 50])]),
        
        ])
    ])
    
   
])


# Verbindung Interaktiver Elemente (Slider, Dropdown, usw.) mit Optik über Callbacks
@app.callback(
    # id zum identifizieren, property um zu sagen was genau verändert werden soll (Wert, Farbe, Text)
    Output("slider-text", "children"),
    Input("h-slider", "value")
    )
def update_slider_text(h_slider):
    return f"Untere Grenze {h_slider[0]}, obere Grenze {h_slider[1]}"

if __name__ == "__main__":
    app.run_server(host="0.0.0.0", debug=False, port=8050)

from dash import Dash, html, dcc, Input, Output
import dash_bootstrap_components as dbc
from flask import Flask, Response
from basisklassen_cam import Camera
import cv2
import numpy as np

external_stylesheets = [dbc.themes.DARKLY]
server = Flask(__name__)

cam = Camera()

app = Dash(external_stylesheets=external_stylesheets, server=server)

@server.route("/test")
def unterseite_test():
    return "Hier ist die zweite Seite"


def generate_stream(cam):
    while True:
        frame = cam.get_frame()
        resized = cv2.resize(frame, None, fx=0.5, fy=0.5)
        hsv = cv2.cvtColor(resized, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
        filtered = cv2.inRange(hsv, np.array([90, 0, 0]), np.array([120, 255, 255]))
        h, w = filtered.shape
        cropped = filtered[int(0.2*h):, :]
        median_blurred = cv2.medianBlur(cropped, 13)
        edges = cv2.Canny(median_blurred, 100, 200)
        lines = cv2.HoughLinesP(edges, 1, np.pi/180, threshold=20, minLineLength=20, maxLineGap=10)
        if lines is not None:
            for line in lines:
                x1, y1, x2, y2 = line[0]
                cv2.line(resized[int(0.2*h):, :], (x1, y1), (x2, y2), (0, 255, 0), 2)
        #print(lines)
        _, frame_as_jpeg = cv2.imencode(".jpeg", resized)

        frame_in_bytes = frame_as_jpeg.tobytes()

        frame_as_string = (b'--frame\r\n'
                b'Content-Type: image/jpeg\r\n\r\n' + frame_in_bytes + b'\r\n\r\n')
        
        yield frame_as_string


@server.route("/video_stream")
def video_stream():
    return Response(generate_stream(cam), mimetype='multipart/x-mixed-replace; boundary=frame')



app.layout = dbc.Container([
    dbc.Row([
        dbc.Col([
            html.H1("Hallo Projektphase"),
            html.P("Test", id="test"),
            html.Div(html.Img(src="/video_stream"))
        ]),
        dbc.Col([
            dcc.RangeSlider(id="slider-h", min=0, max=180),
            dcc.RangeSlider(id="slider-s", min=0, max=255),
            dcc.RangeSlider(id="slider-v", min=0, max=255),
        ],
        width=6)
    ])
    

])

@app.callback(
    Output("test", "children"),
    Input("slider-h", "value"),
    Input("slider-s", "value"),
    Input("slider-v", "value"),
)
def update_p(value_h, value_s, value_v):
    return_value = f"Die Einstelungen sind: {value_h}, {value_s}, {value_v}"
    return return_value


if __name__ == "__main__":
    app.run(host="0.0.0.0", debug=False)

================================================================================
FILE: camp2code/Zusatzmaterial/OpenCV/DEMO_OpenCV_hsv_filter.py
================================================================================

import cv2
import sys
import numpy as np

def hsv_helper():
    def nothing(x):
        pass
    # Create a window
    window_id='Press q to quit'
    cv2.namedWindow(window_id)
    # create trackbars for color change
    cv2.createTrackbar('HMin',window_id,0,255,nothing) # Hue is from 0-179 for Opencv
    cv2.createTrackbar('SMin',window_id,0,255,nothing)
    cv2.createTrackbar('VMin',window_id,0,255,nothing)
    cv2.createTrackbar('HMax',window_id,0,255,nothing)
    cv2.createTrackbar('SMax',window_id,0,255,nothing)
    cv2.createTrackbar('VMax',window_id,0,255,nothing)
    # Set default value for MAX HSV trackbars.
    cv2.setTrackbarPos('HMax', window_id, 255)
    cv2.setTrackbarPos('SMax', window_id, 255)
    cv2.setTrackbarPos('VMax', window_id, 255)
    # Initialize to check if HSV min/max value changes
    hMin = sMin = vMin = hMax = sMax = vMax = 0

    cap = cv2.VideoCapture(0)
    while(1):
        ret, frame = cap.read()
        height, width, _ = frame.shape
        img = cv2.resize(frame,(int(width*1/3), int(height*1/3)), interpolation = cv2.INTER_CUBIC)
        # get current positions of all trackbars
        hMin = cv2.getTrackbarPos('HMin',window_id)
        sMin = cv2.getTrackbarPos('SMin',window_id)
        vMin = cv2.getTrackbarPos('VMin',window_id)
        hMax = cv2.getTrackbarPos('HMax',window_id)
        sMax = cv2.getTrackbarPos('SMax',window_id)
        vMax = cv2.getTrackbarPos('VMax',window_id)
        # Set minimum and max HSV values to display
        lower = np.array([hMin, sMin, vMin])
        upper = np.array([hMax, sMax, vMax])
        # Create HSV Image and threshold into a range.
        hsv = cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
        mask = cv2.inRange(hsv, lower, upper)
        output = cv2.bitwise_and(img,img, mask=mask)
        cv2.imshow(window_id,output)
        if cv2.waitKey(30) == ord('q'):
            break
    cap.release()
    cv2.destroyAllWindows()

hsv_helper()

"""Zentrale Laufzeitverwaltung für eine Kamera und alle vier Ansichten."""
from __future__ import annotations
import logging
import threading
from collections import deque
from time import monotonic, monotonic_ns
from CameraCar import CameraCar
from CameraSensor import CameraSensor
from Save_Data_AI import SaveData_4_AI
from pipeline_config import load, save
import Driving
from driving_health import DrivingHealthMonitor
from recovery_control import RecoveryControl

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class CarApplication:
    """Besitzt Hardware, Threads, Fahrmodi und Recorder genau einmal."""

    def __init__(self, car=None, sensor=None, modes=None, recorder=None, start_camera=True):
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self.car = car if car is not None else CameraCar()
        self.sensor = sensor if sensor is not None else CameraSensor(self.car)
        self.modes = modes if modes is not None else {
            'camera': Driving.CameraMode(car=self.car),
            'gamepad': Driving.PSControllerMode(car=self.car),
        }
        self.recorder = recorder if recorder is not None else SaveData_4_AI(car=self.car, frequency=5)
        self.stop_event = threading.Event()
        self._camera_thread = None
        self._telemetry_thread = None
        self._closed = False
        self.active_mode = None
        self._history = deque(maxlen=240)
        self._last_state_id = None
        self._events = deque(maxlen=60)
        self.health = DrivingHealthMonitor()
        self.recovery = RecoveryControl(self.car, self.running)
        self._requested_stop = False
        self._freq_history = deque(maxlen=240)
        if start_camera:
            self.start_camera()
        self._telemetry_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._sample_loop,
            name='telemetry-sampler', daemon=True)
        self._telemetry_thread.start()

    def start_camera(self):
        with self._lock:
            if self._closed:
                raise RuntimeError('Anwendung bereits beendet')
            if self._camera_thread and self._camera_thread.is_alive():
                return
            self._camera_thread = threading.Thread(target=self.sensor.read_loop,
                args=(self.stop_event,), name='camera-sensor', daemon=True)
            self._camera_thread.start()

    def running(self):
        return any(mode.is_running() for mode in self.modes.values())

    def start_mode(self, key):
        with self._lock:
            if self._closed:
                raise RuntimeError('Anwendung bereits beendet')
            if key not in self.modes:
                raise ValueError('Unbekannter Fahrmodus')
            if self.running():
                raise RuntimeError('Zuerst aktiven Fahrmodus stoppen')
            self.recovery.disable()
            self._requested_stop = False
            self.modes[key].start()
            self.active_mode = key

    def stop_modes(self):
        with self._lock:
            self._requested_stop = True
            # Stop sendet selbst den sicheren Stoppbefehl an das Fahrzeug.
            for mode in self.modes.values():
                if mode.is_running():
                    mode.stop()
            self.car.stop()
            self.active_mode = None

    def set_processor(self, name):
        with self._lock:
            if self.running():
                raise RuntimeError('Prozessorwechsel nur im Stillstand')
            self.sensor.set_processor(name)

    def set_pipeline(self, config):
        with self._lock:
            if self.running():
                raise RuntimeError('Kalibrierung nur im Stillstand ändern')
            if not 0 <= config['crop'][0] < config['crop'][1] <= 1:
                raise ValueError('Ungültiger Bildausschnitt')
            self.sensor.processing_parameters = (
                tuple(config['hue']), tuple(config['sat']), tuple(config['val']),
                tuple(config['crop']), int(config['blur']), int(config['morph']),
                tuple(config['canny']), dict(config['enabled']))
            self.sensor.set_processor(config['processor'])

    def save_pipeline(self, config):
        self.set_pipeline(config)
        save(config)

    def toggle_recording(self):
        with self._lock:
            if self._closed:
                raise RuntimeError('Anwendung bereits beendet')
            if self.recorder.is_running():
                self.recorder.stop()
            else:
                self.recorder.start()
            return self.recorder.is_running()

    def _sample_loop(self):
        """Erfasst die Historie unabhängig von Dash-Callbacks."""
        while not self.stop_event.is_set():
            try:
                self._sample_once()
            except Exception:
                log.exception('Telemetrieerfassung fehlgeschlagen')
            self.stop_event.wait(0.25)

    def _sample_once(self):
        """Erfasst Telemetrie unabhängig vom Browser in begrenzten Puffern."""
        with self._lock:
            state = self.car.driving_state
            mode = self.modes.get('camera')
            reason = ('Benutzer-STOP' if self._requested_stop else
                      getattr(mode, 'last_stop_reason', None))
            self.health.observe(self.car.camera_data, state, self.running(), reason,
                                getattr(mode, 'last_diagnostic', None))
            if state is not None and state.frame_id != self._last_state_id:
                self._last_state_id = state.frame_id
                missing = not state.lanes
                self._history.append((monotonic(), state.frame_id,
                                      state.steering_angle, state.speed, int(missing)))
                if missing:
                    self._events.append((state.frame_id, 'Keine Linien erkannt'))
            metrics = getattr(self.car, 'thread_metrics', {})
            self._freq_history.append((monotonic(),
                metrics['camera'].hz if 'camera' in metrics else 0,
                metrics['controller'].hz if 'controller' in metrics else 0))

    def sample(self):
        """Liefert eine unveränderliche Momentaufnahme der Historie."""
        with self._lock:
            return tuple(self._history), tuple(self._freq_history), tuple(self._events)

    def shutdown(self):
        with self._lock:
            if self._closed:
                return
            self._closed = True
            self.stop_event.set()
        try:
            self.recovery.shutdown()
            self.stop_modes()
        finally:
            try:
                self.recorder.stop()
            finally:
                telemetry_thread = self._telemetry_thread
                if telemetry_thread and telemetry_thread is not threading.current_thread():
                    telemetry_thread.join(timeout=2)
                thread = self._camera_thread
                if thread and thread is not threading.current_thread():
                    thread.join(timeout=3)
                    if thread.is_alive():
                        log.warning('Kamerathread läuft noch')
                # Das Kamerabackend darf nicht parallel zum aktiven Lesethread freigegeben werden.
                if not thread or not thread.is_alive():
                    if hasattr(self.sensor, 'release'):
                        self.sensor.release()

================================================================================
FILE: dash_live.py

import logging
import threading
import time

import cv2
import dash_bootstrap_components as dbc
import numpy as np
from dash import Dash, Input, Output, dcc, html
from flask import Flask, Response

import Driving
import logging_setup  # noqa: F401  – konfiguriert Logging beim Import
from CameraCar import CameraCar
from CameraSensor import CameraSensor

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# --- Konfiguration ----------------------------------------------------------
DEFAULT_HSV = {"h": (90, 120), "s": (20, 255), "v": (50, 255)}
SCALE = 0.5
TARGET_FPS = 15
JPEG_QUALITY = 70


class LaneProcessor:
    """Verarbeitet Kamerabilder in einem eigenen Thread und hält das letzte Ergebnis bereit."""

    def __init__(self, car):
        self.car = car
        self._hsv_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._lower = np.array([DEFAULT_HSV[k][0] for k in "hsv"], dtype=np.uint8)
        self._upper = np.array([DEFAULT_HSV[k][1] for k in "hsv"], dtype=np.uint8)
        self._frame_cond = threading.Condition()
        self._jpeg = None
        self.lines = None  # Koordinaten im skalierten Bild (Faktor SCALE)!

    def set_hsv(self, h, s, v):
        lower = np.array([h[0], s[0], v[0]], dtype=np.uint8)
        upper = np.array([h[1], s[1], v[1]], dtype=np.uint8)
        with self._hsv_lock:  # beide Grenzen zusammen tauschen
            self._lower, self._upper = lower, upper

    def run(self, stop_event):
        period = 1.0 / TARGET_FPS
        while not stop_event.is_set():
            t0 = time.monotonic()
            try:
                self._process_once()
            except Exception:
                log.exception("Fehler in der Bildverarbeitung")
                stop_event.wait(1.0)  # im Fehlerfall nicht im Vollgas loopen
            stop_event.wait(max(0.0, period - (time.monotonic() - t0)))

    def _process_once(self):
        frame = self.car.camera_data.raw_image
        if frame is None or frame.size == 0:
            return

        resized = cv2.resize(frame, None, fx=SCALE, fy=SCALE)
        hsv = cv2.cvtColor(resized, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)

        with self._hsv_lock:
            lower, upper = self._lower, self._upper

        mask = cv2.inRange(hsv, lower, upper)
        edges = cv2.Canny(mask, 50, 150)
        lines = cv2.HoughLinesP(edges, 1, np.pi / 180, 50,
                                minLineLength=40, maxLineGap=10)

        if lines is not None:
            lines = lines.reshape(-1, 4)  # OpenCV 4 und 5
            for x1, y1, x2, y2 in lines:
                cv2.line(resized, (int(x1), int(y1)), (int(x2), int(y2)), (0, 0, 255), 2)
            log.debug("%d Linien erkannt", len(lines))

        ok, jpeg = cv2.imencode(".jpg", resized,
                                [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, JPEG_QUALITY])
        if ok:
            with self._frame_cond:
                self._jpeg = jpeg.tobytes()
                self.lines = lines
                self._frame_cond.notify_all()

    def mjpeg_stream(self):
        """Liefert nur fertige Frames aus – beliebig viele Clients, eine Verarbeitung."""
        while True:
            with self._frame_cond:
                self._frame_cond.wait(timeout=1.0)
                jpeg = self._jpeg
            if jpeg is None:
                continue
            yield b"--frame\r\nContent-Type: image/jpeg\r\n\r\n" + jpeg + b"\r\n"


def create_app(processor: LaneProcessor) -> Dash:
    server = Flask(__name__)
    app = Dash(__name__, server=server, external_stylesheets=[dbc.themes.CERULEAN])

    @server.route("/test")
    def unterseite_test():
        return "Hier ist die Testseite"

    @server.route("/stream")
    def stream():
        return Response(processor.mjpeg_stream(),
                        mimetype="multipart/x-mixed-replace; boundary=frame")

    def slider(channel, max_value):
        return html.Div([
            html.Label(channel.upper()),
            dcc.RangeSlider(0, max_value, value=list(DEFAULT_HSV[channel]),
                            id=f"{channel}-slider",
                            tooltip={"placement": "bottom"}),
        ])

    app.layout = dbc.Container([
        dbc.Row(html.H1("Projektphase 2")),
        dbc.Row([
            dbc.Col(html.Img(src="/stream", style={"width": "100%"}), md=8),
            dbc.Col([
                slider("h", 179),
                slider("s", 255),
                slider("v", 255),
                html.Div(id="hsv-info", className="mt-3"),
            ], md=4),
        ]),
    ], fluid=True)

    @app.callback(
        Output("hsv-info", "children"),
        Input("h-slider", "value"),
        Input("s-slider", "value"),
        Input("v-slider", "value"),
    )
    def update_filter(h, s, v):
        processor.set_hsv(h, s, v)
        return f"H: {h}, S: {s}, V: {v}"

    return app


def main():
    car = CameraCar()
    sensor = CameraSensor(car)
    mode = Driving.CameraMode(car=car)
    processor = LaneProcessor(car)

    stop_event = threading.Event()
    threads = [
        threading.Thread(target=sensor.read_loop, args=(stop_event,),
                         daemon=True, name="sensor"),
        threading.Thread(target=processor.run, args=(stop_event,),
                         daemon=True, name="processor"),
    ]
    for t in threads:
        t.start()
    mode.start()

    try:
        app = create_app(processor)
        # Reloader aus, sonst öffnet ein zweiter Prozess die Kamera
        app.run(host="0.0.0.0", debug=False, use_reloader=False)
    finally:
        stop_event.set()
        if hasattr(mode, "stop"):  # an eure Driving-API anpassen
            mode.stop()
        for t in threads:
            t.join(timeout=2)
        log.info("Sauber beendet")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

END FILE: dash_live.py

"""Zeichnet das Fahrspur-Overlay ausschliesslich fuer das Dashboard."""
import cv2
import numpy as np


def render_overlay(camera, driving, processed=False):
    """Zeigt ROI, erkannte Geraden und Lenkwinkel auf einer Rohbild-Kopie.

    Die Linien sind im Koordinatensystem des zugeschnittenen Bildes definiert.
    Deshalb wird die y-Koordinate um den oberen ROI-Rand verschoben.
    """
    image = camera.process_image if processed else camera.raw_image
    if image is None or image.size == 0:
        return None
    if camera.frame_id != driving.frame_id:
        return None
    result = image.copy()
    if result.ndim == 2:
        result = cv2.cvtColor(result, cv2.COLOR_GRAY2BGR)
    h, w = result.shape[:2]
    if processed:
        # Das vorverarbeitete Bild ist bereits auf den ROI zugeschnitten.
        # Seine Linienkoordinaten brauchen daher keinen Versatz.
        top = 0
    else:
        crop = getattr(camera, 'crop', (0.1, 0.9))
        low, high = crop
        top = max(0, min(h, int(h * (1 - high))))
        bottom = max(top, min(h, int(h * (1 - low))))
        cv2.rectangle(result, (0, top), (w - 1, max(top, bottom - 1)), (255, 200, 0), 1)
    for lane in (driving.lanes if driving.lanes is not None else []):
        if lane is None or len(lane) != 2:
            continue
        m, b = lane
        if not np.isfinite(m) or not np.isfinite(b):
            continue
        # Die Liniengleichung lautet y = m*x + b (bezogen auf den ROI).
        y0 = int(np.clip(float(b) + top, -100000, 100000))
        y1 = int(np.clip(float(m) * (w - 1) + float(b) + top, -100000, 100000))
        cv2.line(result, (0, y0), (w - 1, y1), (0, 255, 0), 2)
    # Die Anzeige ist keine neue Fahrentscheidung.
    cv2.putText(result, f'Frame {camera.frame_id}  Winkel {driving.steering_angle:.0f}  '
                f'Speed {driving.speed:.0f}', (8, 22), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
                0.48, (255, 255, 255), 2, cv2.LINE_AA)
    return result


def render_calibration_overlay(camera, processed=False):
    """Zeigt erkannte Linien auch ohne gestarteten Fahrmodus an.

    Das Dashboard berechnet keine Linien selbst, sondern zeichnet nur
    die bereits im Kamerapaket enthaltenen Parameter.
    """
    if camera is None:
        return None
    source = camera.process_image if processed else camera.raw_image
    if source is None or source.size == 0:
        return None
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    preview = SimpleNamespace(
        frame_id=camera.frame_id,
        lanes=camera.lanes,
        steering_angle=90.0,
        speed=0.0,
    )
    image = render_overlay(camera, preview, processed=processed)
    if image is not None:
        cv2.putText(image, 'KALIBRIERUNG - FAHRMODUS AUS',
                    (8, max(45, image.shape[0] - 12)),
                    cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.45, (0, 255, 255), 2, cv2.LINE_AA)
    return image

"""Visualisiert den Lenkwinkel ohne Veränderung des Kameradatenpakets."""
import math
import cv2


def draw_steering_overlay(image, angle):
    """Zeichnet eine kleine Lenkrichtungsanzeige auf eine Bildkopie."""
    if image is None or getattr(image, 'size', 0) == 0:
        return image
    result = image.copy()
    h, w = result.shape[:2]
    radius = max(15, min(h, w) // 13)
    center = (max(radius + 12, w - radius - 14), radius + 16)
    cv2.circle(result, center, radius + 6, (20, 30, 45), -1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    cv2.circle(result, center, radius, (160, 180, 200), 2, cv2.LINE_AA)
    try:
        degrees = float(angle)
        if not math.isfinite(degrees):
            raise ValueError('Ungültiger Winkel')
        # 90 Grad entsprechen der Neutralstellung. Keine neue Fahrentscheidung.
        delta = math.radians(degrees - 90)
        end = (int(center[0] + radius * .78 * math.sin(delta)),
               int(center[1] - radius * .78 * math.cos(delta)))
        cv2.line(result, center, end, (0, 220, 255), 3, cv2.LINE_AA)
        label = f'{degrees:.0f} deg'
    except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
        label = 'n/a'
    cv2.putText(result, label, (max(5, center[0] - radius), center[1] + radius + 20),
                cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, .45, (255, 255, 255), 1, cv2.LINE_AA)
    return result

"""Fahrmodi des Modellautos.

Jede Implementierung liegt in einer eigenen Datei. Abhaengigkeiten

from __future__ import annotations

# Gemeinsame Bibliotheken aus der bisherigen Driving.py.
# Die Fahrlogik bleibt bei dieser reinen Modulaufteilung unveraendert.
import abc
import curses
import random
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, Tuple
import logging
import cv2
import numpy as np
from BaseCar import BaseCar
from CameraCar import CameraCar, CameraDataProcessed, CameraData
from CarLogger import Loggable
import ConfigReader
from SensorCar import SensorCar
from SonicCar import SonicCar
from LenkwinkelKalkulator import lenkwinkel_from_image
import select
from evdev import InputDevice, ecodes

from .sonic_car_mode import SonicCarMode
from .stop_reason import StopReason

class ApproachObstacle(SonicCarMode):
        
    def __init__(self, name="Fahrmodus 3 (ApproachObstacle)", car:Optional[SonicCar]=None):
        super().__init__(name=name, car=car)
        
        # Dein Flag für einmaliges Logging
        self._free_logged = False

    def _pre_run(self) -> bool:
        ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop = self._cfg.get_int("ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop", 30)
        actual_distance = self._soniccar.distance

        self._log.debug(f"Max-Distance to stop: {ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop}, actual_distance: {actual_distance}")        
        self._soniccar.speed = self._soniccar.v_min

        return True

    def _run_condition(self) -> bool:
        ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop = self._cfg.get_int("ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop", 30)
        d = self._soniccar.distance
        if (d < ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop):
            self._log.info(f"Reached obstacle (distance: {d}), stopping")
            return False
        return True

    def _run(self) -> bool:

        freie_fahrt = self._set_speed_based_on_distance()

        ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop = self._cfg.get_int("ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop", 30)
        d = self._soniccar.distance

        if freie_fahrt:
            self._car.drive(self._car.speed + 5, 90)
        elif (d < ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop):
            self.stop(StopReason(StopReason.OBSTACLE_AHEAD))
            return False

        return True

    def _set_speed_based_on_distance(self) -> bool:
        # False, wenn freie Fahrt, sonst true

        ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop = self._cfg.get_int("ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop", 30)
        slow_down_window = self._cfg.get_float("slow_down_window", 0.3)
        
        actual_distance = self._soniccar.distance
        self._log.debug(f"Sensor-Wert ausgelesen: {actual_distance}")

        old_speed = self._soniccar.speed
        new_speed = old_speed
            
        if (actual_distance <= ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop):
            self._log.info("Obstacle ahead, stopping...")
            self._soniccar.stop()
            self._free_logged = False
            return True
        elif (actual_distance <= ((1+slow_down_window)*ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop)):
            self._free_logged = False
            self._soniccar.speed =  int(self._soniccar.speed * (self._soniccar.distance/ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop))
            self._log.info(f"Fahrzeug nähert sich dem Hindernis (d={actual_distance}), verlangsame Fahrt ({old_speed} -> {self._soniccar.speed})")
            return True
        elif not self._free_logged:
            self._log.info("Freie Fahrt voraus, kein Hindernis in Sicht")
            self._free_logged = True
            return False
        
        return False


================================================================================
END FILE: driving/approach_obstacle.py
================================================================================

================================================================================
FILE: driving/base.py
================================================================================

from __future__ import annotations

# Gemeinsame Bibliotheken aus der bisherigen Driving.py.
# Die Fahrlogik bleibt bei dieser reinen Modulaufteilung unveraendert.
import abc
import curses
import random
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, Tuple
import logging
import cv2
import numpy as np
from BaseCar import BaseCar
from CameraCar import CameraCar, CameraDataProcessed, CameraData
from CarLogger import Loggable
import ConfigReader
from SensorCar import SensorCar
from SonicCar import SonicCar
from LenkwinkelKalkulator import lenkwinkel_from_image
import select
from evdev import InputDevice, ecodes

from .stop_reason import StopReason

class DrivingMode(abc.ABC):

    def __init__(self, name:str, car:BaseCar, logger:logging.Logger, config:ConfigReader.ConfigReader, update_cfg:bool=False, frequency:int=500) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self._stop_event = None
        self._name = name
        self._car = car
        self._log = logger
        self._cfg = config
        self._frequency = self._cfg.get_int("scan_frequency", frequency)
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._update_cfg = update_cfg
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._drive, daemon=True, args=[update_cfg])

    def start(self):
        """Startet höchstens einen Worker und setzt dessen Stop-Ereignis vorher."""
        with self._lock:
            if self._thread is not None and self._thread.is_alive():
                return
            self._stop_event = threading.Event()
            self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._drive, daemon=True,
                                            args=[self._update_cfg], name=self._name)
            self._thread.start()

    def _drive(self, update_cfg: bool = False):
        event = self._stop_event
        try:
            if update_cfg:
                self._log.warning("Konfiguration wird während der Fahrt neu geladen")
            if not self._pre_run():
                self._log.error("Vorbereitung des Fahrmodus fehlgeschlagen")
                return
            period = 1.0 / max(1, self._frequency)
            while not event.is_set() and self._run_condition():
                started = time.monotonic()
                if update_cfg:
                    self._cfg._load_config_file()
                    self._car._update_config()
                if not self._run():
                    break
                event.wait(max(0.0, period - (time.monotonic() - started)))
        except Exception:
            self._log.exception("Fehler im Fahrmodus %s", self._name)
        finally:
            event.set()
            try:
                self._post_run()
            except Exception:
                self._log.exception("Fehler beim Beenden des Fahrmodus")
            with self._lock:
                if self._stop_event is event:
                    self._stop_event = None

    @abc.abstractmethod
    def _run_condition(self) -> bool:
        pass

    def _pre_run(self) -> bool:
        self._car.drive(0,90)
        return True

    @abc.abstractmethod
    def _run(self) -> bool:
        pass

    def _post_run(self) -> bool:
        self._car.drive(0,90)
        return True

    def is_running(self) -> bool:
        with self._lock:
            return self._thread is not None and self._thread.is_alive() and self._stop_event is not None and not self._stop_event.is_set()

    def stop(self, reason: StopReason | int = 0) -> bool:
        with self._lock:
            event = self._stop_event
            thread = self._thread
            if event is None:
                return False
            event.set()
        self._stop_car(reason)
        if thread is not None and thread is not threading.current_thread():
            thread.join(timeout=2)
        return True

    def _stop_car(self, reason:StopReason|int=0):
        """_summary_

        Args:
            reason (StopReason | int, optional): _description_. Defaults to 0.
        """
        self._log.info(f"Car stopped for {reason}")
        self._car.stop()

END FILE: driving/base.py

================================================================================
FILE: driving/camera_mode.py

from __future__ import annotations
import abc
import curses
import random
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, Tuple
import logging
import cv2
import numpy as np
from BaseCar import BaseCar
from CameraCar import CameraCar
from models import CameraDataProcessed, CameraData, DrivingState
from CarLogger import Loggable
import ConfigReader
from SensorCar import SensorCar
from SonicCar import SonicCar
from LenkwinkelKalkulator import (
    put_steering_lines_into_image, cals_drive_parameter_from_lanes
)
import select
from evdev import InputDevice, ecodes
from .base import DrivingMode
class CameraMode(DrivingMode):

    def __init__(self, name='CameraModus', car:Optional[CameraCar]=None, cfg:Optional[ConfigReader.ConfigReader]=None):
        car = car if car is not None else CameraCar()
        self._cameracar = car
        cfg = cfg if cfg is not None else ConfigReader.ConfigReader("cameracar_controller")
        self._last_steering_angle = 90
        self._lost_since = None
        self.last_stop_reason = None
        self.last_diagnostic = None
        self._lost_timeout = cfg.get_float('lost_line_timeout', 1)
        logger = logging.getLogger(self.__class__.__name__)

        super().__init__(name, car, logger, cfg)

    # Werte aus dem vorherigen run
    def _pre_run(self) -> bool:
        self._last_steering_angle = 90
        self._lost_since = None
        self.last_stop_reason = None
        self.last_diagnostic = None
        self._car.drive(0, 90)
        return True

    def _run(self) -> bool:

        camera_data_transfer = self._cameracar.camera_data
        if camera_data_transfer is None:
            self.last_stop_reason = 'NO_CAMERA_DATA'
            self._log.error('CameraMode beendet: kein Kameradatenpaket')
            return False

        try:
            now = time.monotonic_ns()


            # Der LaneDetector hat die Linien bereits fuer diesen Frame ermittelt.
            # Hier wird nur noch der Lenkwinkel aus den Linien berechnet.
            lanes = list(camera_data_transfer.lanes)
            img_with_lines = put_steering_lines_into_image(
                camera_data_transfer.process_image, lanes
            )
            steering_angle_raw, processed_img_areas = cals_drive_parameter_from_lanes(
                img_with_lines, lanes
            )


            # Diagnose wird unabhängig von der Fahrentscheidung veröffentlicht.
            # Die beiden Linienwerte stammen ausschließlich aus CameraData.
            self.last_diagnostic = {
                'frame_id': camera_data_transfer.frame_id,
                'lanes': tuple(lanes),
                'raw_angle': steering_angle_raw,
                'held_previous_angle': steering_angle_raw is None,
                'previous_angle': self._last_steering_angle,
                'stop_reason': None,
            }
            if steering_angle_raw is None:
                if self._lost_since is None:
                    self._lost_since = now

                if now - self._lost_since > self._lost_timeout * 1e9:
                    self.last_stop_reason = 'LANE_TIMEOUT'
                    self.last_diagnostic['stop_reason'] = 'LANE_TIMEOUT'
                    self._log.warning('CameraMode stoppt: LANE_TIMEOUT, Frame %s, letzte Lenkung %s', camera_data_transfer.frame_id, self._last_steering_angle)
                    self._log.info(f'Seit {self._lost_timeout}s keine zwei Linien – {self._name} wird beendet')
                    return False

                steering_angle_raw = self._last_steering_angle

            else:
                self._lost_since = None
                self._last_steering_angle = steering_angle_raw

            driving_parameter = self.driving_parameter(steering_angle_raw)

            steering_gain = self._cfg.get_float('steering_gain', 2.0)
            steering_angle = int(max(45, min(135, 90 + (driving_parameter[0] - 90) * steering_gain)))
            speed = driving_parameter[1]


            self._cameracar.drive(speed, steering_angle)
            self.last_diagnostic.update(
                requested_angle=steering_angle,
                actual_angle=self._car.steering_angle,
                speed_control=self._car.speed,
            )


            timestamp = time.time_ns()

            latenz = timestamp - camera_data_transfer.timestamp

            camera_data_processed = CameraDataProcessed(
                frame_id=camera_data_transfer.frame_id,
                raw_image=camera_data_transfer.raw_image,
                timestamp=timestamp,
                process_image=camera_data_transfer.process_image,
                process_image_lines=processed_img_areas,
                steering_angle=steering_angle_raw,
                steering_angle_car=self._car.steering_angle,
                line_data=np.empty(0),
                speed=self._car.speed,
                lanes=lanes
            )


            # Die Fahrentscheidung referenziert genau das verarbeitete Bild.
            self._cameracar.driving_state = DrivingState(
                frame_id=camera_data_transfer.frame_id,
                timestamp_ns=timestamp,
                steering_angle=camera_data_processed.steering_angle,
                speed=camera_data_processed.speed,
                lanes=camera_data_processed.lanes,
                steering_angle_car=camera_data_processed.steering_angle_car,
            )
            # Alter Datenvertrag bleibt fuer Dashboard/Recorder vorerst aktiv.
            self._cameracar.camera_data_processed = camera_data_processed


        except Exception:
            self.last_stop_reason = 'CONTROLLER_EXCEPTION'
            self._log.exception('Fehler in CameraMode._run()')
            return False


        return True


    def _run_condition(self) -> bool:
        return True    
    # def: Linienwerte bekommen, Aus den Linien lenkwinkel bearbeiten


    def driving_parameter (self, steering_angle_raw: int ) -> tuple[int, int]:
        steering_angle = steering_angle_raw  # Replace with actual calculation
        speed = 30           # Replace with actual calculation

        return steering_angle, speed

from __future__ import annotations

# Gemeinsame Bibliotheken aus der bisherigen Driving.py.
# Die Fahrlogik bleibt bei dieser reinen Modulaufteilung unveraendert.
import abc
import curses
import random
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, Tuple
import logging
import cv2
import numpy as np
from BaseCar import BaseCar
from CameraCar import CameraCar, CameraDataProcessed, CameraData
from CarLogger import Loggable
import ConfigReader
from SensorCar import SensorCar
from SonicCar import SonicCar
from LenkwinkelKalkulator import lenkwinkel_from_image
import select
from evdev import InputDevice, ecodes

from .sensor_car_mode import SensorCarMode

class FollowLine(SensorCarMode):
    def __init__(self, name="Fahrmodus 5", car:Optional[SensorCar]=None):
        super().__init__(name=name, car=car)

        self._summe_integral = 0
        self._previous_error = 0
        self._last_direction = 0
        self._last_lenkwinkel = 90
        self._last_time = time.monotonic()
    
    def _run_condition(self) -> bool:
        return self._is_on_line()
    
    def _pre_run(self) -> bool:
        self._last_time = time.monotonic()
        self._summe_integral = 0.0
        self._previous_error = 0.0
        self._last_lenkwinkel = 90
        return super()._pre_run()

    def _run(self) -> bool:

        # Lenkwinkel berechnen und zeitpersistente Werte merken
        lw = self._calc_steering_angle_from_ir_sensors()
        v = self._calc_speed_from_steering_angle(lw)

        self._log.info(f"Folge Linie mit Lenkwinkel {lw} und Geschwindigkeit {v}")

        self._sensorcar.drive(v, lw)

        return True    
    
    def _post_run(self) -> bool:
        return super()._post_run()    
    

    def _is_on_line(self) -> bool:
        if (not self._sensorcar.is_on_line()):
            self._log.info("Lost line")
            return False
        return True
    
    def _calc_steering_angle_from_ir_sensors(self) -> int:
        """Berechnet den Lenkwinkel basierend auf IR-Messwerten und PID-Korrekturfaktoren.

        Die Funktion nutzt gewichtete Infrarotmesswerte, um einen Fehlerwert zu bestimmen
        und daraus mittels proportionaler und differenzieller Steuerung den Lenkwinkel
        zu berechnen. Der resultierende Winkel wird auf den Bereich von 45 bis 135 Grad
        begrenzt.

        Returns:
            float: Der berechnete Lenkwinkel im Bereich von 45 bis 135 Grad.
        """

        korrektur_proportional = self._cfg.get_float("korrektur_proportional", 5)
        korrektur_integral = self._cfg.get_float("korrektur_integral", 0)
        korrektur_integral_boundary = self._cfg.get_float("korrektur_integral_boundary", 30)
        korrektur_differential = self._cfg.get_float("korrektur_differential", 0)

        sensor_gewichte = self._cfg.get_list("ir_sensor_weights", [-2,-1,0,1,2])
        slew_rate = self._cfg.get_int("slew_rate", 3)

        messwerte = self._sensorcar.ir_sensor_values

        self._log.debug(f"Starte Lenkwinkel-Berechnung mit Eingangsdaten: messwerte:{messwerte}, sensor_gewichtung:{sensor_gewichte}, slew_rate:{slew_rate}, korrektur_proportional:{korrektur_proportional}, korrektur_integral:{korrektur_integral}, summe_integral:{self._summe_integral}, korrektur_integral_boundary:{korrektur_integral_boundary}, korrektur_differential:{korrektur_differential}, previous_error:{self._previous_error}, last_time:{self._last_time}, last_lenkwinkel:{self._last_lenkwinkel}")

        current_time_stamp = time.monotonic()

        sum_messwerte = sum(messwerte)
        
        # Div/0 -> Lenkwinkel geradeaus
        if (sum_messwerte == 0):
            self._last_time = current_time_stamp
            self._previous_error = 0.0
            self._last_lenkwinkel = 90
            return 90
        
        error = sum(np.multiply(messwerte, sensor_gewichte))/sum_messwerte
        self._log.debug(f"Aktueller Messwerte-Fehler: {error}")

        # P
        dKP = (korrektur_proportional * error)
        self._sensorcar.p_wert = dKP
        

        # I
        self._summe_integral += error * (current_time_stamp-self._last_time)
        self._summe_integral = max(min(self._summe_integral, korrektur_integral_boundary), -korrektur_integral_boundary)   # Anti-Windup
        dKI = korrektur_integral * self._summe_integral
        self._sensorcar.i_wert = dKI

        # D
        dKD = 0
        if (current_time_stamp> self._last_time):
            dKD = (korrektur_differential * ((error - self._previous_error))/(current_time_stamp - self._last_time))
        self._sensorcar.d_wert = dKD

        u = dKP + dKI + dKD

        self._log.info(f"Lenkwinkel berechnet u:{dKP:.2f}, P:{dKP:.2f}, I:{dKI:.2f}, D:{dKD:.2f}")

        # 1. Normierung
        lw = int(max(45, min(135, 90 + u)))
        self._log.debug
        (f"Errechneter Lenkwinkel (u): {u} --> normiert: {lw}")
        # 2. Anti-Slew
        delta = lw - self._last_lenkwinkel
        delta = max(-slew_rate, min(delta, slew_rate))
        lw = self._last_lenkwinkel + delta

        # 3. Finales Clamping
        lw = max(45, min(135, lw))
        self._log.debug(f"Lenkwinkel nach anti-slew (lw): {lw}")

        self._log.debug(f"Ergebnis der Lenkwinkel-Berechung: \ndKP: {dKP}, \ndKI: {dKI}, \ndKD: {dKD}, \nu: {u}, \nlw: {lw}, \ndelta t:{current_time_stamp-self._last_time}, \nsumme integral:{self._summe_integral}")
        self._log.debug(f"Ergebnis der dKI-Berechung: \nerror: {error}, \ndelta t:{current_time_stamp-self._last_time}, \nsumme integral:{self._summe_integral}, \nkorr integral:{korrektur_integral}")

        # # Hier Werte berechnen und für Logging in Methode get_logging_payload in Klasse speichern
        # # Diese Werte kommen dann in das JSON-DataLog
        # with self._lock:
        #     self._lw_debug_values["integral"] = 0
        #     self._lw_debug_values["sum_messwerte"] = 0
        #     self._lw_debug_values["previous_error"] = previous_error


        # last_time, summe_integral, last_error, lw
        self._previous_error = error
        self._last_time = current_time_stamp
        self._last_lenkwinkel = lw
        return lw

    def _calc_speed_from_steering_angle(self, lenkwinkel:float) -> int:
        v_min = self._sensorcar.v_min
        v_max = self._sensorcar.v_max
        self._log.info(f"Berechne Geschwindigkeit für Lenkwinkel: {lenkwinkel}°")
        if (45 > lenkwinkel or 135 < lenkwinkel):
            raise ValueError(f"Lenkwinkel muss zwischen 45 und 135° sein (war: {lenkwinkel})")
        
        self._speed = int(v_min + (v_max - v_min) * (1 - ((lenkwinkel-90)/45)**2))
        self._log.info(f"Kalkulierte Speed: {self._speed}")
        return self._speed

END FILE: driving/follow_line.py
