from __future__ import annotations

# Gemeinsame Bibliotheken aus der bisherigen Driving.py.
# Die Fahrlogik bleibt bei dieser reinen Modulaufteilung unveraendert.
import abc
import curses
import random
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, Tuple
import logging
import cv2
import numpy as np
from BaseCar import BaseCar
from CameraCar import CameraCar, CameraDataProcessed, CameraData
from CarLogger import Loggable
import ConfigReader
from SensorCar import SensorCar
from SonicCar import SonicCar
from LenkwinkelKalkulator import lenkwinkel_from_image
import select
from evdev import InputDevice, ecodes

from .base import DrivingMode

class KeyboardMode(DrivingMode):

    def __init__(self, 
                 car: Optional[BaseCar] = None, 
                 name: str = "Interaktiver Tastaturmodus", 
                 logger: Optional[logging.Logger] = None, 
                 cfg: Optional[ConfigReader.ConfigReader] = None, 
                 update_cfg: bool = False, 
                 frequency: int = 25) -> None:
        
        car = car if car is not None else SonicCar()
        logger = logging.getLogger(self.__class__.__name__)
        cfg = cfg if cfg is not None else ConfigReader.ConfigReader("soniccar_controller")
        
        super().__init__(name, car, logger, cfg, update_cfg, frequency)
        
        self.SPEED_FORWARD = 40
        self.SPEED_BACKWARD = -40
        self.ANGLE_STRAIGHT = 90
        self.ANGLE_LEFT = 60
        self.ANGLE_RIGHT = 120

    def start(self) -> None:
        self._log.info(f"Starte {self._name} direkt via start().")
        
        with self._lock:
            self._stop_event = threading.Event()
        
        try:
            curses.wrapper(self._interactive_loop)
        except Exception as e:
            self._log.exception(f"Fehler im curses-Wrapper von {self._name}: {e}")
        finally:
            self._car.stop()
            with self._lock:
                self._stop_event = None
            self._log.info(f"{self._name} beendet und Fahrzeug gestoppt.")

    def _run_condition(self) -> bool:
        return False
    
    def _run(self) -> bool:
        return False

    def _interactive_loop(self, stdscr) -> None:
        curses.cbreak()          
        stdscr.keypad(True)      
        stdscr.nodelay(True)     
        
        stdscr.clear()
        stdscr.addstr(1, 0, "Q = Vorwärts-Links | W = Vorwärts  | E = Vorwärts-Rechts")
        stdscr.addstr(2, 0, "A = Rückwärts-Links | S = Rückwärts | D = Rückwärts-Rechts")
        stdscr.addstr(3, 0, "Drücke 'Space' oder 'Esc' zum Beenden.\n")
        stdscr.refresh()
        
        last_speed = 0
        last_angle = self.ANGLE_STRAIGHT
        step_time = 1 / self._frequency

        if (self._stop_event is None):
            raise RuntimeError("Stop_event not available")
        
        while not self._stop_event.is_set():
            try:
                key = stdscr.getch()
                if key != -1:
                    speed = 0
                    angle = self.ANGLE_STRAIGHT
                    action_text = ""
                    valid_key = False

                    if key == ord('w') or key == curses.KEY_UP:
                        speed = self.SPEED_FORWARD
                        angle = self.ANGLE_STRAIGHT
                        action_text = "VORWÄRTS"
                        valid_key = True
                    elif key == ord('q'):
                        speed = self.SPEED_FORWARD
                        angle = self.ANGLE_LEFT
                        action_text = "VORWÄRTS LINKS"
                        valid_key = True
                    elif key == ord('e'):
                        speed = self.SPEED_FORWARD
                        angle = self.ANGLE_RIGHT
                        action_text = "VORWÄRTS RECHTS"
                        valid_key = True

                    elif key == ord('s') or key == curses.KEY_DOWN:
                        speed = self.SPEED_BACKWARD
                        angle = self.ANGLE_STRAIGHT
                        action_text = "RÜCKWÄRTS"
                        valid_key = True
                    elif key == ord('a') or key == curses.KEY_LEFT:
                        speed = self.SPEED_BACKWARD
                        angle = self.ANGLE_LEFT
                        action_text = "RÜCKWÄRTS LINKS"
                        valid_key = True
                    elif key == ord('d') or key == curses.KEY_RIGHT:
                        speed = self.SPEED_BACKWARD
                        angle = self.ANGLE_RIGHT
                        action_text = "RÜCKWÄRTS RECHTS"
                        valid_key = True

                    elif key == 27 or key == ord(' '):
                        self._stop_event.set()
                        break

                    if valid_key and (speed != last_speed or angle != last_angle):
                        self._car.drive(speed, angle)
                        
                        stdscr.move(5, 0)
                        stdscr.clrtoeol()
                        stdscr.addstr(5, 0, f"Aktion: {action_text} -> car.drive({speed}, {angle})")
                        stdscr.refresh()
                        
                        last_speed = speed
                        last_angle = angle

                else:
                    if last_speed != 0 or last_angle != self.ANGLE_STRAIGHT:
                        self._car.drive(0, self.ANGLE_STRAIGHT)
                        
                        stdscr.move(5, 0)
                        stdscr.clrtoeol()
                        stdscr.addstr(5, 0, "Aktion: STOPP (Keine Taste gedrückt)")
                        stdscr.refresh()
                        
                        last_speed = 0
                        last_angle = self.ANGLE_STRAIGHT

                time.sleep(step_time)

            except KeyboardInterrupt:
                self._stop_event.set()
                break


================================================================================
END FILE: driving/keyboard_mode.py

================================================================================
FILE: driving/mode_one.py

from __future__ import annotations

# Gemeinsame Bibliotheken aus der bisherigen Driving.py.
# Die Fahrlogik bleibt bei dieser reinen Modulaufteilung unveraendert.
import abc
import curses
import random
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, Tuple
import logging
import cv2
import numpy as np
from BaseCar import BaseCar
from CameraCar import CameraCar, CameraDataProcessed, CameraData
from CarLogger import Loggable
import ConfigReader
from SensorCar import SensorCar
from SonicCar import SonicCar
from LenkwinkelKalkulator import lenkwinkel_from_image
import select
from evdev import InputDevice, ecodes

from .base import DrivingMode

class ModeOne(DrivingMode):

    def __init__(self, car: Optional[BaseCar], name:str="Fahrmodus 1", logger: Optional[logging.Logger]=None, cfg: Optional[ConfigReader.ConfigReader]=None, update_cfg: bool = False, frequency: int = 500) -> None:
        car = car if car is not None else SonicCar()
        logger = logging.getLogger(self.__class__.__name__)
        cfg = cfg if cfg is not None else ConfigReader.ConfigReader("soniccar_controller")
        super().__init__(name, car, logger, cfg)

    def _run_condition(self) -> bool:
        return True
    
    def _run(self) -> bool:
        self._sleep_with_stop(1, stop=True)  
                     
        self._car.drive(30)
        self._log.debug(f"3 Sekunden vorwärts. Geschwindigkeit: {self._car.speed}, Lenkwinkel: {self._car.steering_angle}")
        self._sleep_with_stop(3, stop=False)

        self._log.debug(f"1 Sekunde Stopp. Geschwindigkeit: {self._car.speed}, Lenkwinkel: {self._car.steering_angle}")
        self._sleep_with_stop(1, stop=True)
            
        self._car.drive(-30)
        self._log.debug(f"3 Sekunden rückwärts. Geschwindigkeit: {self._car.speed}, Lenkwinkel: {self._car.steering_angle}")
        self._sleep_with_stop(3, stop = False)

        self._car.stop()
        return False


    def _sleep_with_stop(self, duration: float, stop:bool) -> bool:
        if (self._stop_event is None):
            raise RuntimeError("Stop_event not availabel")
        end_time = time.time() + duration
        step_time = 1/self._frequency 
        if (stop):
            self._car.stop()

        while time.time() < end_time and not self._stop_event.is_set():
            time.sleep(step_time)

        return False


================================================================================
END FILE: driving/mode_one.py

from __future__ import annotations

# Gemeinsame Bibliotheken aus der bisherigen Driving.py.
# Die Fahrlogik bleibt bei dieser reinen Modulaufteilung unveraendert.
import abc
import curses
import random
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, Tuple
import logging
import cv2
import numpy as np
from BaseCar import BaseCar
from CameraCar import CameraCar, CameraDataProcessed, CameraData
from CarLogger import Loggable
import ConfigReader
from SensorCar import SensorCar
from SonicCar import SonicCar
from LenkwinkelKalkulator import lenkwinkel_from_image
import select
from evdev import InputDevice, ecodes

from .mode_one import ModeOne

class ModeTwo(ModeOne):

    def __init__(self, car: Optional[BaseCar], name: str = "Fahrmodus 2", logger: Optional[logging.Logger] = None, cfg: Optional[ConfigReader.ConfigReader] = None, update_cfg: bool = False, frequency: int = 500) -> None:
        super().__init__(car=car, name=name, logger=logger, cfg=cfg, update_cfg=update_cfg, frequency=frequency)

    def _run_condition(self) -> bool:
        return True
    
    def _run(self) -> bool:
        self._circle((135, "rechts/Uhrzeigersinn"))
        self._circle((45, "links/gegen Uhrzeigersinn"))
        return False
    
    def _circle(self, direction: Tuple[int, str]) -> bool:
        self._log.info(f'Starte Fahrmodus 2 ({direction[1]})')
        
        self._sleep_with_stop(1, stop=True)

        self._car.drive(30)
        self._log.debug(f"1 Sekunde vorwärts. Geschwindigkeit: {self._car.speed}, Lenkwinkel: {self._car.steering_angle}")
        self._sleep_with_stop(1, stop=False)

        self._car.drive(30, direction[0])
        self._log.debug(f"8 Sekunden {direction[1]} vorwärts. Geschwindigkeit: {self._car.speed}, Lenkwinkel: {self._car.steering_angle}")
        self._sleep_with_stop(8, stop=False)

        self._log.debug(f"Kurzer Zwischenstopp. Geschwindigkeit: {self._car.speed}, Lenkwinkel: {self._car.steering_angle}")
        self._sleep_with_stop(2, stop=True)

        self._car.drive(-30, direction[0])
        self._log.debug(f"8 Sekunden {direction[1]} rückwärts. Geschwindigkeit: {self._car.speed}, Lenkwinkel: {self._car.steering_angle}")
        self._sleep_with_stop(8, stop=False)

        self._car.drive(-30, 90)
        self._log.debug(f"1 Sekunde rückwärts zum Startpunkt. Geschwindigkeit: {self._car.speed}, Lenkwinkel: {self._car.steering_angle}")
        self._sleep_with_stop(1, stop=False)

        self._car.stop()
        self._log.info(f"Fahrmodus 2 {direction[1]} beendet. Geschwindigkeit: {self._car.speed}, Lenkwinkel: {self._car.steering_angle}") 

        return False

from __future__ import annotations

# Gemeinsame Bibliotheken aus der bisherigen Driving.py.
# Die Fahrlogik bleibt bei dieser reinen Modulaufteilung unveraendert.
import abc
import curses
import random
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, Tuple
import logging
import cv2
import numpy as np
from BaseCar import BaseCar
from CameraCar import CameraCar, CameraDataProcessed, CameraData
from CarLogger import Loggable
import ConfigReader
from SensorCar import SensorCar
from SonicCar import SonicCar
from LenkwinkelKalkulator import lenkwinkel_from_image
import select
from evdev import InputDevice, ecodes

from .base import DrivingMode

class PSControllerMode(DrivingMode):

    def __init__(self, name='PS-Controller', car:Optional[BaseCar]=None, cfg:Optional[ConfigReader.ConfigReader]=None, device_path:str='/dev/input/event4'):
        car = car if car is not None else CameraCar()
        logger = logging.getLogger(self.__class__.__name__)
        cfg = cfg if cfg is not None else ConfigReader.ConfigReader('cameracar_controller')
        super().__init__(name, car, logger, cfg)
        self._device_path = device_path
        self._controller = None
        self._steer = 0.0
        self._speed = 0.0
        self._steer_min = 0.1
        self._speed_min = 15
        self._last_drive = None

    def _run_condition(self) -> bool:
        return True

    def _pre_run(self) -> bool:
        try:
            self._controller = InputDevice(self._device_path)
            self._controller.grab()
        except OSError as e:
            self._log.error(f'Controller {self._device_path} nicht verfügbar: {e}')
            self._controller = None
            return False
        self._log.info(f'Using device: {self._controller.path} - {self._controller.name}')
        self._steer, self._speed, self._last_drive = 0.0, 0.0, None
        return super()._pre_run()

    def _run(self) -> bool:
        if self._controller is None: return False

        try:
            ready, _, _ = select.select([self._controller.fd], [], [], 0.005)
            if ready:
                for event in self._controller.read():
                    if event.type != ecodes.EV_ABS: continue
                    if event.code == ecodes.ABS_RY: self._speed = (event.value - 127) / 128
                    elif event.code == ecodes.ABS_X: self._steer = (event.value - 127) / 128
        except BlockingIOError:
            pass
        except OSError as e:
            self._log.error(f'Controller getrennt: {e}')
            return False

        # Lenkung
        steer_angle = 90 if abs(self._steer) < self._steer_min else int(max(45, min(135, 90 + 45 * self._steer)))

        # Geschwindigkeit: Stick nach oben -> speed < 0 -> vorwärts (wie bw.forward() im Originalskript)
        speed_0_to_100 = min(100, int(abs(self._speed) * 100))
        v = 0 if speed_0_to_100 < self._speed_min else (speed_0_to_100 if self._speed < 0 else -speed_0_to_100)

        # Nur bei Änderung an die Hardware senden
        if (v, steer_angle) != self._last_drive:
            self._car.drive(v, steer_angle)
            self._last_drive = (v, steer_angle)

        return True

    def _post_run(self) -> bool:
        if self._controller is not None:
            try: self._controller.ungrab()
            except OSError: pass
            try: self._controller.close()
            except OSError: pass
            self._controller = None
        return super()._post_run()


================================================================================
END FILE: driving/ps_controller_mode.py
================================================================================

from __future__ import annotations

# Gemeinsame Bibliotheken aus der bisherigen Driving.py.
# Die Fahrlogik bleibt bei dieser reinen Modulaufteilung unveraendert.
import abc
import curses
import random
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, Tuple
import logging
import cv2
import numpy as np
from BaseCar import BaseCar
from CameraCar import CameraCar, CameraDataProcessed, CameraData
from CarLogger import Loggable
import ConfigReader
from SensorCar import SensorCar
from SonicCar import SonicCar
from LenkwinkelKalkulator import lenkwinkel_from_image
import select
from evdev import InputDevice, ecodes

from .approach_obstacle import ApproachObstacle

class RoomExplorer(ApproachObstacle):
    def __init__(self, name="Fahrmodus 4 (RoomExplorer)", car:Optional[SonicCar]=None):
        super().__init__(name=name, car=car)

        self._explore_logged = False

    def _pre_run(self):
        explorer_max_time = self._cfg.get_int("explorer_max_time", 30)

        self._t_end = time.time() + explorer_max_time
        self._log.debug(f"Erkunde den Raum für {explorer_max_time}s bis {datetime.fromtimestamp(self._t_end).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")}")

        actual_speed_drive_explore = self._cfg.get_int("start_speed", 60)
        steering_angle_drive_explore = self._cfg.get_int("start_angle", 90)
        self._car.drive(actual_speed_drive_explore, steering_angle_drive_explore)

        self._counter = 0
        self._log.debug(f"Parameter: speed_direction ({actual_speed_drive_explore}, steer_direction:{steering_angle_drive_explore})")

        return True

    def _run_condition(self) -> bool:
        if (self._t_end is None):
            raise RuntimeError("Start- und/oder Endzeit nicht gesetzt (t_start: {self._t_start}, t_end: {self._t_end})")
        return (time.time() < self._t_end)

    def _run(self) -> bool:
            
        if (not self._set_speed_based_on_distance()):
            # Freie Fahrt
            self._log.debug("Freie Fahrt")
            self._explore()
        else:
            # Ausweichmanöver einleiten
            self._explore_logged = False
            suchzeit_sekunden = self._overcome_obstacle()
            self._log.info(f"Fahre rückwärts für {suchzeit_sekunden}")
            start_suchzeit = time.time()
            ende_suchzeit = start_suchzeit + suchzeit_sekunden
            if (self._stop_event is None):
                raise RuntimeError("Stop_Event not available")
            while (not self._stop_event.is_set() and (time.time() < ende_suchzeit)):
                time.sleep(1/self._frequency)
            self._log.info(f"Rückwärtsfahrt beendet")

        return True

    def _post_run(self):
        self._t_end = None
        self._counter = 0

        return True

    def _explore(self):
        if not self._explore_logged:
            self._log.info(f"Fahre auf Erkundungsfahrt bis zum nächsten Hindernis...")
            self._explore_logged = True
        actual_speed = self._soniccar.speed
        steering_angle = self._soniccar.steering_angle
        steer_dir = -1 if steering_angle < 90 else 1
        speed_dir = self._soniccar.direction

        self._log.debug(f"speed: {actual_speed},, angle: {steering_angle}, speed_dir: {speed_dir}, steer_dir: {steer_dir}, counter: {self._counter}")
        self._counter += 1

        # Richtung nur alle x Zyklen ändern
        if self._counter >= 25:
            counter = 0
            speed_dir = random.choice([-1, 1])
            steer_dir = random.choice([-1, 1])

        # kleine Schritte -> smooth
        actual_speed += speed_dir * 1
        steering_angle += int(steer_dir * 1.5)

        # Grenzen
        actual_speed = max(30, min(actual_speed, 100))
        steering_angle = max(45, min(steering_angle, 135))

        self._log.debug(f"Exploriere den Raum..... (Geschwindigkeit: {actual_speed}, Lenkwinkel: {steering_angle})")
        self._soniccar.drive(actual_speed, steering_angle)

    def _overcome_obstacle(self) -> int:
        # Gibt die Rückwärtsfahrzeit in Sekunden aus
                
        self._soniccar.stop()

        # Lenkt links oder rechts ein
        ausweich_lenkung = random.choice(
            (
                (45, "links"), 
                (135, "rechts")
            )
        )

        self._log.info(f"Overcoming obstacle..... driving to the {ausweich_lenkung[1]}")

        self._soniccar.drive(-30, ausweich_lenkung[0])
        return random.randint(1, 4)

    def get_logging_payload(self, log_level:int=logging.INFO) -> dict:
        """_summary_

        Args:
            log_level (int, optional): _description_. Defaults to logging.INFO.

        Returns:
            dict: _description_
        """
        with self._lock:
            payload = self._car.get_logging_payload()
            payload["lenkwinkel"] = "yet to be implemented"
            #if (log_level == logging.DEBUG):
                #payload["lenkwinkel_calculus"] = self._lw_debug_values
            return payload

END FILE: driving/room_explorer.py

from __future__ import annotations

# Gemeinsame Bibliotheken aus der bisherigen Driving.py.
# Die Fahrlogik bleibt bei dieser reinen Modulaufteilung unveraendert.
import abc
import curses
import random
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, Tuple
import logging
import cv2
import numpy as np
from BaseCar import BaseCar
from CameraCar import CameraCar, CameraDataProcessed, CameraData
from CarLogger import Loggable
import ConfigReader
from SensorCar import SensorCar
from SonicCar import SonicCar
from LenkwinkelKalkulator import lenkwinkel_from_image
import select
from evdev import InputDevice, ecodes


class StopReason(int):

    NONE=int(0),
    LOST_LINE = int(10)
    OBSTACLE_AHEAD = int(20)
    PROGRAM_STOPPED_BY_USER = int(30)

    STR_REASONS = {
        NONE: "No reason",
        LOST_LINE: "Lost line",
        OBSTACLE_AHEAD: "Obstacle ahead",
        PROGRAM_STOPPED_BY_USER: "Stopped by user"
    }
    
    def __init__(self, reason:int) -> None:
        """_summary_

        Args:
            reason (int): _description_
        """
        self._reason = reason

    def __int__(self) -> int:
        """_summary_

        Returns:
            int: _description_
        """
        return self._reason

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        """_summary_

        Returns:
            str: _description_
        """
        return StopReason.STR_REASONS[self._reason] if self._reason in self.STR_REASONS else "Unknown reason"

    def __eq__(self, value: object) -> bool:
        """_summary_

        Args:
            value (object): _description_

        Returns:
            bool: _description_
        """
        if (isinstance(value, int)):
            return int(value) == self._reason
        return False

================================================================================
FILE: driving_health.py

"""Beobachtet Fahrzustände ohne die Fahrregelung zu verändern."""
from collections import deque
from dataclasses import dataclass
from threading import RLock
from time import monotonic_ns

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class HealthEvent:
    timestamp_ns: int
    frame_id: int | None
    code: str
    detail: str

class DrivingHealthMonitor:
    """Erfasst Zustandsübergänge und begrenzt den Speicherverbrauch."""
    def __init__(self, max_events=120):
        self._lock = RLock()
        self._events = deque(maxlen=max_events)
        self._lane_state = None
        self._was_running = False
        self._last_frame = None
        self._last_diagnostic_frame = None
        self._last_held = None
        self._last_reason = None

    def observe(self, camera, driving, running, stop_reason=None, diagnostic=None):
        with self._lock:
            frame_id = getattr(camera, 'frame_id', None)
            if camera is not None and frame_id != self._last_frame:
                self._last_frame = frame_id
                raw_lanes = getattr(camera, 'lanes', ())
                try:
                    lanes = tuple(raw_lanes) if raw_lanes is not None else ()
                except TypeError:
                    lanes = ()
                count = sum(lane is not None for lane in lanes)
                state = 'LANE_LOST' if count == 0 else ('LANE_PARTIAL' if count == 1 else 'LANES_DETECTED')
                if state != self._lane_state:
                    self._events.append(HealthEvent(monotonic_ns(), frame_id, state,
                        f'{count} erkannte Linien; geometrische Gültigkeit nicht geprüft'))
                    self._lane_state = state
            if diagnostic is not None:
                diag_frame = diagnostic.get('frame_id')
                if diag_frame != self._last_diagnostic_frame:
                    self._last_diagnostic_frame = diag_frame
                    held = bool(diagnostic.get('held_previous_angle'))
                    if held != self._last_held:
                        self._events.append(HealthEvent(monotonic_ns(), diag_frame,
                            'STEERING_HELD' if held else 'STEERING_COMPUTED',
                            f"Rohwinkel={diagnostic.get('raw_angle')}, "
                            f"vorheriger Winkel={diagnostic.get('previous_angle')}, "
                            f"Soll={diagnostic.get('requested_angle')}, "
                            f"Stellwert={diagnostic.get('actual_angle')}"))
                        self._last_held = held
            if stop_reason and stop_reason != self._last_reason:
                self._last_reason = stop_reason
                self._events.append(HealthEvent(monotonic_ns(), frame_id,
                    'STOP_REASON', stop_reason))
            if self._was_running and not running:
                self._events.append(HealthEvent(monotonic_ns(), frame_id,
                    'CONTROLLER_STOPPED', stop_reason or 'Ursache nicht veröffentlicht'))
            self._was_running = running

    def events(self):
        with self._lock:
            return tuple(self._events)

================================================================================
END FILE: driving_health.py

"""Erkennt Fahrspurlinien aus bereits aufbereiteten Sensordaten.

Die Erkennung hat keinen Bezug zur Fahrzeugansteuerung oder zum Dashboard.
"""
from LenkwinkelKalkulator import detect_lanes_from_areas

class LaneDetector:
    def detect(self, processed_image):
        """Gibt unveraenderliche (m, b)-Paare im ROI-Koordinatensystem zurueck."""
        lanes, _ = detect_lanes_from_areas(
            processed_image, n_lanes=2, min_area=150, fit_frac=1.0
        )
        return tuple((float(m), float(b)) for m, b in lanes)

================================================================================

import logging
import threading
import time

import os
import signal

import cv2 #Bildverarbeitung
import dash_bootstrap_components as dbc
import numpy as np
from dash import Dash, Input, Output, dcc, html, State
from flask import Flask, Response
import plotly.graph_objects as go

import LenkwinkelKalkulator
import logging_setup  # noqa: F401  – konfiguriert Logging beim Import

# Unsere Packge für CameraCar
import Driving
from Save_Data_AI import SaveData_4_AI

from CameraCar import CameraCar, CameraData, CameraDataProcessed
from CameraSensor import CameraSensor
from telemetry import FrequencyCounter
from dashboard_overlay import render_calibration_overlay


# Config
DEFAULT_HSV = {'h': (100, 130), 's': (80, 255), 'v': (50, 255), 'crop': (0.2, 0.8), 'blur': 3, 'morph_kernel': 5}

SCALE = 0.5
TARGET_FPS = 15
JPEG_QUALITY = 70

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
_shutdown_lock = threading.Lock()


class ImageStream:
    '''Importiert Kamerabilder in einem eigenen Thread und hält das letzte Ergebnis bereit.'''

    def __init__(self, car, sensor, driving, use_raw_data:bool=True):
        self.car = car
        self._hsv_lock = threading.Lock()
        self._lower = np.array([DEFAULT_HSV[k][0] for k in 'hsv'], dtype=np.uint8)
        self._upper = np.array([DEFAULT_HSV[k][1] for k in 'hsv'], dtype=np.uint8)
        self._frame_cond = threading.Condition()
        self.use_raw_data = use_raw_data
        self._jpeg = None
        self.lines = None  # Koordinaten im skalierten Bild (Faktor SCALE)!
        self.sensor = sensor
        self.driving = driving
        self.metrics = FrequencyCounter()

    def set_hsv(self, hue, saturation, value, crop, blur, morph_kernel):
        self.sensor.processing_parameters = (hue, saturation, value, crop, blur, morph_kernel)

    def run(self, stop_event):
        period = 1.0 / TARGET_FPS
        while not stop_event.is_set():
            t0 = time.monotonic()
            try:
                self._process_once()
            except Exception:
                log.exception('Fehler in der Bildverarbeitung')
                stop_event.wait(1.0)  # im Fehlerfall nicht im Vollgas loopen
            stop_event.wait(max(0.0, period - (time.monotonic() - t0)))

    def _process_once(self):
        """Ein Bild erzeugen, ohne das Original im CameraCar zu veraendern."""
        from dashboard_overlay import render_overlay

        # Beide Streams zeichnen dasselbe Overlay, aber auf verschiedenen
        # Originalbildern: links Rohbild, rechts vorverarbeitetes ROI-Bild.
        # Die Linienerkennung selbst wird NICHT erneut ausgefuehrt.
        processed = not self.use_raw_data
        mode_running = self.driving is not None and self.driving.is_running()
        if not mode_running:
            camera = self.car.camera_data
            frame = render_calibration_overlay(camera, processed=processed)
        else:
            state = self.car.driving_state
            if state is None:
                return
            record = next((r for r in reversed(self.car.frame_history.snapshot())
                           if r.camera.frame_id == state.frame_id and r.driving is not None), None)
            if record is None:
                return
            frame = render_overlay(record.camera, record.driving, processed=processed)

        if frame is None or frame.size == 0:
            return

        ok, jpeg = cv2.imencode('.jpg', frame, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, JPEG_QUALITY])
        if ok:
            self.metrics.tick()
            with self._frame_cond:
                self._jpeg = jpeg.tobytes()
                self._frame_cond.notify_all()

    def mjpeg_stream(self):
        '''Liefert nur fertige Frames aus – beliebig viele Clients, eine Verarbeitung.'''
        while True:
            with self._frame_cond:
                self._frame_cond.wait(timeout=1.0)
                jpeg = self._jpeg

            if jpeg is None:
                continue

            yield b'--frame\r\nContent-Type: image/jpeg\r\n\r\n' + jpeg + b'\r\n'


def create_app(modes, car, stream_pre_processing: ImageStream, stream_post_processing: ImageStream, sensor: CameraSensor, save_data: SaveData_4_AI, on_stop) -> Dash:

    server = Flask(__name__)
    app = Dash(__name__, server=server, external_stylesheets=[dbc.themes.CERULEAN])

    @server.route('/test')
    def unterseite_test():
        return 'Hier ist die Testseite'

    @server.route('/stream')
    def stream():
        return Response(stream_pre_processing.mjpeg_stream(), mimetype='multipart/x-mixed-replace; boundary=frame')

    @server.route('/stream2')
    def stream2():
        return Response(stream_post_processing.mjpeg_stream(), mimetype='multipart/x-mixed-replace; boundary=frame')

    def slider(channel, max_value, increment=1.0):
        default = DEFAULT_HSV[channel]

        if isinstance(default, (tuple, list)):
            component = dcc.RangeSlider(0, max_value, value=list(default), step=increment, id=f'{channel}-slider', tooltip={'placement': 'bottom'})
        else:
            component = dcc.Slider(0, max_value, value=default, step=increment, id=f'{channel}-slider', tooltip={'placement': 'bottom'})

        return html.Div([html.Label(channel.upper()), component])

    app.layout = dbc.Container([

        dbc.Row(html.H1('Projektphase 2')),
        dbc.Row([
            dbc.Col([
                html.H5('Thread-Frequenzen: Soll / Ist'),
                html.Div(id='thread-metrics', className='mb-2'),
                dcc.Graph(id='frequency-plot', config={'displayModeBar': False},
                          style={'height': '310px'}),
            ]),
            dcc.Interval(id='metrics-interval', interval=1000, n_intervals=0),
        ]),

        dbc.Row([
            dbc.Col([html.H6('Rohbild + Overlay'), html.Img(src='/stream', style={'width': '100%'})], md=4),
            dbc.Col([html.H6('Vorverarbeitetes Bild + Overlay'), html.Img(src='/stream2', style={'width': '100%'})], md=4),
            dbc.Col([
                slider('h', 179),
                slider('s', 255),
                slider('v', 255),
                slider('crop', 1, 0.05),
                slider('blur', 20, 1),
                slider('morph_kernel', 20, 1),
                html.Div(id='hsv-info', className='mt-3')
            ], md=4),
        ]),

        dbc.Row([
            dbc.Col([
                html.Label('Fahrmodus wählen'),
                dcc.Dropdown(
                    id='dropdown_fahrmodus',
                    options=[
                        {'label': 'Fahrmodus 1 - Bilderkennung', 'value': 'fahrmodus_1'},
                        {'label': 'Fahrmodus 2 - AI', 'value': 'fahrmodus_2'},
                        {'label': 'Fahrmodus 3 - PS Controller', 'value': 'fahrmodus_ps'}
                    ],
                    value='fahrmodus_1',
                    clearable=False,
                    placeholder='Bitte Fahrmodus wählen'
                ),
                dbc.Button('Fahrmodus starten', id='button_fahrmodi', n_clicks=0, color='success', className='mt-4'),
                dcc.Store(id='fahrmodus_state', data=False),
                dbc.Button('Datenaufnahme starten', id='button_save_data', n_clicks=0, color='primary', className='mt-4'),
                dcc.Store(id='save_data_state', data=False)
            ], md=4)
        ]),

        dbc.Row([
            dbc.Col([
                dbc.Button('Close App', id='stop-button', color='danger', className='mt-4', n_clicks=0),
                html.Div(id='stop-info', className='mt-3')
            ], md=4),
        ]),

    ], fluid=True)

    @app.callback(
        Output('thread-metrics', 'children'),
        Output('frequency-plot', 'figure'),
        Input('metrics-interval', 'n_intervals'),
    )
    def show_thread_metrics(_):
        """Soll-/Ist-Frequenzen anzeigen und in einem eigenen Plot vergleichen.

        Bei einem inaktiven Thread zeigen wir 0 Hz statt einer alten Messung.
        Die Sollwerte stammen aus der jeweiligen Konfiguration.
        """
        camera_mode = modes.get('fahrmodus_1')
        controller_target = float(camera_mode._frequency) if camera_mode else 0.0
        entries = [
            ('Kamera', float(sensor.scan_frequency), car.thread_metrics['camera']),
            ('Controller', controller_target, car.thread_metrics['controller']),
            ('Dashboard Rohbild', float(TARGET_FPS), stream_pre_processing.metrics),
            ('Dashboard verarbeitet', float(TARGET_FPS), stream_post_processing.metrics),
        ]
        names, targets, actuals, parts = [], [], [], []
        for name, target, metric in entries:
            age = metric.age_seconds
            active = age is not None and age <= 2.0
            actual = metric.hz if active else 0.0
            status = 'aktiv' if active else 'inaktiv / keine Daten'
            names.append(name)
            targets.append(target)
            actuals.append(actual)
            parts.append(html.Span(
                f'{name}: {target:.1f} / {actual:.1f} Hz ({status})',
                className='badge bg-secondary me-2 mb-2',
            ))

        figure = go.Figure()
        figure.add_bar(name='Soll', x=names, y=targets, marker_color='#7b8ca3')
        figure.add_bar(name='Ist', x=names, y=actuals, marker_color='#20a080')
        figure.update_layout(
            barmode='group',
            margin=dict(l=30, r=15, t=15, b=65),
            yaxis_title='Frequenz (Hz)',
            yaxis=dict(rangemode='tozero'),
            legend=dict(orientation='h', y=1.15),
            uirevision='frequenzen',
        )
        return parts, figure

    @app.callback(
        Output('hsv-info', 'children'),
        Input('h-slider', 'value'),
        Input('s-slider', 'value'),
        Input('v-slider', 'value'),
        Input('crop-slider', 'value'),
        Input('blur-slider', 'value'),
        Input('morph_kernel-slider', 'value')
    )
    def update_filter(h, s, v, crop, blur, morph_kernel):
        stream_pre_processing.set_hsv(h, s, v, crop, blur, morph_kernel)
        return f'H: {h}, S: {s}, V: {v}, crop: {crop}, blur: {blur}, morph: {morph_kernel}'

    @app.callback(
        Output('stop-info', 'children'),
        Input('stop-button', 'n_clicks'),
        prevent_initial_call=True
    )
    def stop_app(n_clicks):
        on_stop()
        threading.Timer(0.5, os.kill, args=(os.getpid(), signal.SIGINT)).start()
        return 'App beendet'

    @app.callback(
        Output('fahrmodus_state', 'data'),
        Output('button_fahrmodi', 'children'),
        Output('button_fahrmodi', 'color'),
        Output('dropdown_fahrmodus', 'disabled'),
        Input('button_fahrmodi', 'n_clicks'),
        State('fahrmodus_state', 'data'),
        State('dropdown_fahrmodus', 'value'),
        prevent_initial_call=True
    )
    def toggle_fahrmodus(n_clicks, running, selected):
        if running:
            for m in modes.values():
                m.stop()

            return False, 'Fahrmodus starten', 'success', False

        mode = modes.get(selected)

        if mode is None:
            log.warning(f'{selected} ist nicht implementiert')
            return False, 'Fahrmodus starten', 'success', False

        mode.start()

        return True, 'Fahrmodus stoppen', 'danger', True

    @app.callback(
        Output('save_data_state', 'data'),
        Output('button_save_data', 'children'),
        Output('button_save_data', 'color'),
        Input('button_save_data', 'n_clicks'),
        State('save_data_state', 'data'),
        prevent_initial_call=True
    )
    def toggle_save_data(n_clicks, running):
        if running:
            save_data.stop()
            return False, 'Datenaufnahme starten', 'primary'

        save_data.start()

        return True, 'Datenaufnahme stoppen', 'danger'

    return app


def shutdown(modes, car, sensor, save_data, threads, stop_event):
    # Reihenfolge:
    # SaveData_4_AI -> Driving -> CameraCar -> CameraSensor -> eigene Threads

    # Speichere Sliderdaten in config.json

    if not _shutdown_lock.acquire(blocking=False):
        return

    try:
        save_data.stop()
    except Exception:
        log.exception('Fehler beim Stoppen von SaveData_4_AI')

    for name, obj in [*modes.items(), ('CameraCar', car), ('CameraSensor', sensor)]:
        try:
            if hasattr(obj, 'stop'):
                obj.stop()
        except Exception:
            log.exception('Fehler beim Stoppen von %s', name)

    stop_event.set()

    for t in threads:
        t.join(timeout=2)

        if t.is_alive():
            log.warning('Thread %s läuft noch', t.name)

    log.info('Sauber beendet')


def main():

    car = CameraCar()
    sensor = CameraSensor(car)
    save_data = SaveData_4_AI(car=car, frequency=5.0)

    modes = {
        'fahrmodus_1': Driving.CameraMode(car=car),
        'fahrmodus_ps': Driving.PSControllerMode(car=car)
    }

    stream_pre_processing = ImageStream(car, sensor, modes.get('fahrmodus_1'), use_raw_data=True)
    stream_post_processing = ImageStream(car, sensor, modes.get('fahrmodus_1'), use_raw_data=False)

    stop_event = threading.Event()

    threads = [
        threading.Thread(target=sensor.read_loop, args=(stop_event,), daemon=True, name='sensor'),
        threading.Thread(target=stream_pre_processing.run, args=(stop_event,), daemon=True, name='pre_processes_stream'),
        threading.Thread(target=stream_post_processing.run, args=(stop_event,), daemon=True, name='post_processed_stream'),
    ]

    for t in threads:
        t.start()

    try:
        app = create_app(
            modes,
            car,
            stream_pre_processing,
            stream_post_processing,
            sensor,
            save_data,
            on_stop=lambda: shutdown(modes, car, sensor, save_data, threads, stop_event)
        )

        # Reloader aus, sonst öffnet ein zweiter Prozess die Kamera
        app.run(host='0.0.0.0', debug=False, use_reloader=False)

    finally:
        shutdown(modes, car, sensor, save_data, threads, stop_event)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()


    # 1: [X] Sensor-Funktion bauen (Thread-sicher, vgl. InfraredSensor:43) zum Aufnehmen der Slider-Einstellungen
    # 2: [X] Sensor an App übergeben ('update_filter') zur Übergabe der Filterwerte
    # 3: [HOLD] inkl. Switch RGBBHRGREYHSV...
    # 4: [X] Im Sensor die Bildbearbeitung durchführen und img_processed setzen in datapacket
    # 5: inkl. Linienerkennung einmalen in img_processed
    #    --- Wenn nur eine Linie zu sehen ist - Linie rechts im Bild -> Lenke rechts und fahre langsam bis wieder zwei linien zu sehen sind - Analog ander seite
    # 6: [X] Start Drive/Stop Drive Button (Analog Dashboard_Live_Data.py) Zeile: 460 - 475, 331ff
    # 7: [X] Start Button: Fange an Bilder zu speichern
    #    --- Unterordner (fig) erstellen, dann Timestamp (Ordnername), in einem eigenen Thread laufen lassen, Speicherfrequenz, time.sleep(frequenz - Delta t) Delta t ist speicherdauer
    #    --- Bild braucht eindeutigen Namen - Parameter in Tabelle (Spalte 1 dann Bildname (eindeutig), Parameter)
    #    --- Bild nur speicher, wenn bedingung erfüllt ist zB 2 Fahrbahnlinien sind da
    # 8: [X] Button: Manuel Fahren, Driving Mode umschalten

"""Driving Cockpit: neue Oberflaeche fuer die bestehende Fahrzeugarchitektur.

Startet die Kamera, aber keinen Fahrmodus automatisch. Alle Fahrbefehle
laufen weiterhin ausschliesslich ueber die vorhandenen DrivingMode-Klassen.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import atexit
import base64
import logging
import threading
import time
from collections import deque
from datetime import datetime
import cv2
import numpy as np
import plotly.graph_objects as go
import dash_bootstrap_components as dbc
from dash import Dash, Input, Output, State, dcc, html, ctx, no_update
from CameraCar import CameraCar
from CameraSensor import CameraSensor
from Save_Data_AI import SaveData_4_AI
import Driving
from dashboard_overlay import render_calibration_overlay, render_overlay

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class CockpitHistory:
    """Kleine Historie fuer Plots. Speichert keine Bildkopien."""
    def __init__(self, maxlen=180):
        self._rows = deque(maxlen=maxlen)
        self._events = deque(maxlen=30)
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._last_frame = None
        self._missing = False

    def sample(self, car):
        # Nur neue Fahrentscheidungen zaehlen. Bei ausgeschaltetem Controller
        # entstehen keine erfundenen Messwerte.
        state = car.driving_state
        if state is None:
            return
        with self._lock:
            if state.frame_id == self._last_frame:
                return
            self._last_frame = state.frame_id
            count = len(state.lanes) if state.lanes is not None else 0
            missing = count == 0
            if missing != self._missing:
                self._events.appendleft((datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S'),
                                        'Keine Linien erkannt' if missing else 'Linien wieder erkannt',
                                        state.frame_id))
                self._missing = missing
            self._rows.append(dict(t=time.monotonic(), frame=state.frame_id,
                                   steering=float(state.steering_angle), speed=float(state.speed),
                                   line_missing=int(missing), lines=count))

    def snapshot(self):
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._rows), list(self._events)

class Cockpit:
    def __init__(self):
        self.car = CameraCar()
        self.sensor = CameraSensor(self.car)
        self.modes = {
            'camera': Driving.CameraMode(car=self.car),
            'gamepad': Driving.PSControllerMode(car=self.car),
        }
        self.recorder = SaveData_4_AI(car=self.car, frequency=5.0)
        self.history = CockpitHistory()
        self.stop_event = threading.Event()
        self.lock = threading.RLock()
        self.active_mode = None
        self.recording = False
        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.sensor.read_loop,
                                       args=(self.stop_event,), daemon=True, name='sensor')
        self.thread.start()

    def running(self):
        return any(mode.is_running() for mode in self.modes.values())

    def start_mode(self, key):
        with self.lock:
            if self.running():
                return 'Ein Fahrmodus laeuft bereits. Zuerst stoppen.'
            if key not in self.modes:
                return 'Unbekannter Fahrmodus.'
            self.modes[key].start()
            self.active_mode = key
            return f'{key} gestartet'

    def stop_modes(self):
        with self.lock:
            # Hardware zuerst anhalten, auch falls der Thread blockiert.
            self.car.drive(0, 90)
            for mode in self.modes.values():
                if mode.is_running():
                    mode.stop()
            self.active_mode = None
            return 'Fahrzeug gestoppt'

    def switch_processor(self, name):
        with self.lock:
            if self.running():
                return 'Prozessorwechsel nur bei gestopptem Fahrmodus.'
            self.sensor.set_processor(name)
            return f'Bildprozessor: {name}'

    def toggle_recording(self):
        with self.lock:
            if self.recording:
                self.recorder.stop()
                self.recording = False
                return 'Aufzeichnung gestoppt'
            self.recorder.start()
            self.recording = True
            return 'Aufzeichnung gestartet'

    def shutdown(self):
        try:
            self.stop_modes()
        except Exception:
            log.exception('Fehler beim Fahrzeugstopp')
        try:
            self.recorder.stop()
        except Exception:
            log.exception('Fehler beim Recorder-Stopp')
        self.stop_event.set()
        try:
            self.sensor.stop()
        except Exception:
            log.exception('Fehler beim Kamera-Stopp')
        self.thread.join(timeout=2)


def jpeg_uri(image):
    if image is None or image.size == 0:
        return None
    ok, data = cv2.imencode('.jpg', image, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 68])
    return 'data:image/jpeg;base64,' + base64.b64encode(data).decode('ascii') if ok else None


def line_chart(rows, chosen):
    """Nur wirklich vorhandene Daten anzeigen; Offset wird nicht erfunden."""
    fig = go.Figure()
    if rows:
        base = rows[-1]['t']
        x = [row['t'] - base for row in rows]
        series = {'steering': ('Lenkwinkel (Grad)', '#45c8f5'),
                  'speed': ('Speed (Steuerwert)', '#f0ad4e'),
                  'line_missing': ('Keine Linie (0/1)', '#f66d84')}
        for key in chosen or []:
            if key in series:
                label, color = series[key]
                fig.add_scatter(x=x, y=[row[key] for row in rows],
                                name=label, mode='lines', line={'color':color})
    fig.update_layout(template='plotly_dark', paper_bgcolor='#17243a',
                      plot_bgcolor='#17243a', height=300,
                      margin=dict(l=35,r=15,t=15,b=35),
                      xaxis_title='Sekunden bis jetzt', uirevision='history')
    return fig


def frequency_chart(car, sensor, camera_mode):
    labels=['Kamera','Controller']
    targets=[float(sensor.scan_frequency), float(camera_mode._frequency)]
    metrics=[car.thread_metrics['camera'],car.thread_metrics['controller']]
    actual=[m.hz if m.age_seconds is not None and m.age_seconds < 2 else 0.0
            for m in metrics]
    fig=go.Figure()
    fig.add_bar(x=labels,y=targets,name='Soll',marker_color='#73859f')
    fig.add_bar(x=labels,y=actual,name='Ist',marker_color='#43cfa4')
    fig.update_layout(barmode='group',template='plotly_dark',height=260,
                      paper_bgcolor='#17243a',plot_bgcolor='#17243a',
                      margin=dict(l=35,r=15,t=15,b=25),yaxis_title='Hz',uirevision='frequency')
    return fig


def create_app(cockpit):
    app=Dash(__name__,external_stylesheets=[dbc.themes.DARKLY])
    app.title='Camp2Code | Driving Cockpit'
    panel={'background':'#17243a','border':'1px solid #34465e','borderRadius':'14px','padding':'16px'}
    camera_style={'width':'100%','aspectRatio':'4/3','objectFit':'contain',
                  'background':'#0a1322','borderRadius':'8px'}
    app.layout=dbc.Container([
        dbc.Row([dbc.Col([html.H2('◉ Driving Cockpit',className='fw-bold'),
                 html.P('Camp2Code · Fahrbetrieb und Live-Diagnose',className='text-secondary')],md=8),
                 dbc.Col(html.Div(id='run-status',className='text-end fw-semibold'),md=4)],className='pt-4'),
        dbc.Row([dbc.Col(html.Div([
            html.H5('Fahrzeugsteuerung'),
            dbc.Row([dbc.Col([html.Label('Fahrmodus'),dcc.Dropdown(id='mode',
                options=[{'label':'CameraMode','value':'camera'},
                         {'label':'PSControllerMode','value':'gamepad'}],
                value='camera',clearable=False,style={'color':'#111'})],md=5),
                dbc.Col([html.Label('Bildprozessor'),dcc.Dropdown(id='processor',
                options=[{'label':'LaneProcessor (Legacy)','value':'legacy'},
                         {'label':'CopilotLaneProcessor (Diagnose)','value':'copilot'}],
                value='copilot',clearable=False,style={'color':'#111'})],md=7)]),
            html.Div([dbc.Button('Fahrmodus starten',id='start',color='success',className='me-2'),
                      dbc.Button('STOP',id='stop',color='danger',className='me-2'),
                      dbc.Button('Aufzeichnung an/aus',id='record',color='info',outline=True)],
                     className='mt-3'),
            html.Div('Bereit',id='action-message',className='small text-info mt-2')
        ],style=panel),md=12)],className='mb-3'),
        dbc.Row([dbc.Col(html.Div([html.H5('Live · Rohbild'),html.Img(id='raw',style=camera_style)],style=panel),md=6),
                 dbc.Col(html.Div([html.H5('Live · Fahrspur-Overlay'),html.Img(id='overlay',style=camera_style),
                    dbc.Checklist(id='layers',options=[{'label':'Linien / ROI','value':'overlay'}],
                                  value=['overlay'],switch=True)],style=panel),md=6)],className='g-3 mb-3'),
        dbc.Row([dbc.Col(html.Div([html.H5('Fahrdaten'),html.Div(id='kpis',className='fs-5'),
                  html.P('Offset: nicht verfügbar (noch keine fachlich definierte Messgröße)',
                         className='small text-secondary')],style=panel),md=12)],className='mb-3'),
        dbc.Row([dbc.Col(html.Div([html.H5('Telemetrie · letzte 180 Messpunkte'),
                   dbc.Checklist(id='series',inline=True,value=['steering','speed'],
                      options=[{'label':'Lenkwinkel','value':'steering'},
                               {'label':'Speed','value':'speed'},
                               {'label':'Linie fehlt','value':'line_missing'}]),
                   dcc.Graph(id='telemetry',config={'displayModeBar':True})],style=panel),md=8),
                 dbc.Col(html.Div([html.H5('Ereignisse'),html.Div(id='events'),
                       html.P('„Linie fehlt“ ist eine Beobachtung, kein bestätigtes LOST_LINE-Stoppevent.',
                              className='small text-secondary')],style=panel),md=4)],className='g-3 mb-3'),
        dbc.Row([dbc.Col(html.Div([dbc.Checklist(id='show-frequency',
                  options=[{'label':'Performance / Frequenzen anzeigen','value':'show'}],value=['show'],switch=True),
                  html.Div(dcc.Graph(id='frequencies'),id='frequency-panel')],style=panel),md=12)],className='mb-4'),
        dcc.Interval(id='camera-timer',interval=350,n_intervals=0),
        dcc.Interval(id='telemetry-timer',interval=500,n_intervals=0),
    ],fluid=True,style={'maxWidth':'1600px','background':'#0f192b','minHeight':'100vh'})

    @app.callback(Output('action-message','children'),Input('start','n_clicks'),
                  Input('stop','n_clicks'),Input('record','n_clicks'),
                  Input('processor','value'),State('mode','value'),prevent_initial_call=True)
    def actions(_start,_stop,_record,processor,mode):
        try:
            trigger=ctx.triggered_id
            if trigger=='start':return cockpit.start_mode(mode)
            if trigger=='stop':return cockpit.stop_modes()
            if trigger=='record':return cockpit.toggle_recording()
            if trigger=='processor':return cockpit.switch_processor(processor)
        except Exception as error:
            log.exception('Cockpit-Aktion fehlgeschlagen')
            return f'Aktion fehlgeschlagen: {type(error).__name__}'
        return no_update

    @app.callback(Output('raw','src'),Output('overlay','src'),
                  Input('camera-timer','n_intervals'),Input('layers','value'))
    def images(_tick,layers):
        camera=cockpit.car.camera_data
        if camera is None:return None,None
        raw=jpeg_uri(camera.raw_image)
        if 'overlay' not in (layers or []):return raw,raw
        # Im Fahrbetrieb nur ein Paar mit identischer Frame-ID anzeigen.
        state=cockpit.car.driving_state
        if cockpit.running() and state is not None:
            record=next((r for r in reversed(cockpit.car.frame_history.snapshot())
                         if r.camera.frame_id==state.frame_id and r.driving is not None),None)
            overlay=render_overlay(record.camera,record.driving) if record else None
        else:
            overlay=render_calibration_overlay(camera)
        return raw,jpeg_uri(overlay)

    @app.callback(Output('run-status','children'),Output('kpis','children'),
                  Output('telemetry','figure'),Output('events','children'),
                  Output('frequencies','figure'),Output('frequency-panel','style'),
                  Input('telemetry-timer','n_intervals'),Input('series','value'),
                  Input('show-frequency','value'))
    def metrics(_tick,selected,frequency_enabled):
        cockpit.history.sample(cockpit.car)
        rows,events=cockpit.history.snapshot()
        state=cockpit.car.driving_state
        running=cockpit.running()
        status=('● FAHRT AKTIV' if running else '● GESTOPPT') + (' · REC' if cockpit.recording else '')
        kpis=('Noch keine Fahrentscheidung' if state is None else
              f'Lenkwinkel: {state.steering_angle:.0f}°   |   Speed: {state.speed:.0f}   |   '
              f'Linien: {len(state.lanes) if state.lanes is not None else 0}   |   Frame: {state.frame_id}')
        event_nodes=[html.Div(f'{t} · {text} · Frame {frame}',className='small border-bottom py-2')
                     for t,text,frame in events[:8]] or [html.P('Keine Ereignisse',className='text-secondary')]
        return (status,kpis,line_chart(rows,selected),event_nodes,
                frequency_chart(cockpit.car,cockpit.sensor,cockpit.modes['camera']),
                {} if 'show' in (frequency_enabled or []) else {'display':'none'})
    return app


def main():
    cockpit=Cockpit()
    atexit.register(cockpit.shutdown)
    create_app(cockpit).run(host='0.0.0.0',port=8050,debug=False,use_reloader=False)

if __name__=='__main__':
    main()

"""Eigenstaendiges Kalibrierungs-Dashboard, ohne Fahrbefehle."""
import atexit
import base64
import logging
import threading
import cv2
import dash_bootstrap_components as dbc
import plotly.graph_objects as go
from dash import Dash, Input, Output, State, dcc, html
from CameraCar import CameraCar
from CameraSensor import CameraSensor
from pipeline_config import load as load_pipeline_config, save as save_pipeline_config
CFG = load_pipeline_config()
from dashboard_overlay import render_calibration_overlay

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

STAGES = [
    ('raw', '01 · Rohbild', 'Kameraaufnahme', None),
    ('roi', '02 · Bildausschnitt', 'Nur der relevante Bildbereich', 'crop'),
    ('hsv', '03 · HSV-Farbraum', 'Farbwerte zur Filterung', None),
    ('mask', '04 · Farbmaske', 'Hue, Saturation und Value', 'hsv'),
    ('blur', '05 · Glättung', 'Störpixel reduzieren', 'blur'),
    ('morph', '06 · Morphologie', 'Lücken schließen', 'morph'),
    ('edges', '07 · Kanten', 'Canny-Kantenerkennung', 'canny'),
    ('overlay', '08 · Erkannte Linien', 'Linien im Rohbild, ohne Fahrbetrieb', 'lanes'),
]

def image_uri(image):
    if image is None or image.size == 0:
        return None
    ok, encoded = cv2.imencode('.jpg', image, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 68])
    return 'data:image/jpeg;base64,' + base64.b64encode(encoded).decode('ascii') if ok else None

def slider(label, name, min_v, max_v, value, step=1, range_slider=False):
    value = CFG.get(name, value)
    control = dcc.RangeSlider if range_slider else dcc.Slider
    return html.Div([html.Label(label, className='small fw-semibold'),
                     control(id=name, min=min_v, max=max_v, step=step, value=value,
                             tooltip={'placement':'bottom','always_visible':False})], className='mb-3')

def controls(kind):
    if kind == 'crop':
        return slider('ROI: oberer / unterer Anteil', 'crop', 0, 1, [0.1,0.9], 0.01, True)
    if kind == 'hsv':
        return html.Div([slider('Hue (0–179)', 'hue', 0,179,[110,179],1,True),
                         slider('Saturation (0–255)', 'sat', 0,255,[0,255],1,True),
                         slider('Value (0–255)', 'val', 0,255,[0,255],1,True)])
    if kind == 'blur':
        return slider('Blur-Kernel', 'blur', 1,15,3,1)
    if kind == 'morph':
        return slider('Morph-Kernel', 'morph', 1,15,5,1)
    if kind == 'canny':
        return slider('Canny: untere / obere Schwelle', 'canny', 0,255,[50,100],1,True)
    return html.Div('Keine eigenen Parameter', className='text-muted small')

def card(key,title,desc,kind):
    return dbc.Col(dbc.Card([
        dbc.CardHeader([html.Strong(title), html.Div(desc,className='small text-muted')]),
        dbc.CardBody([html.Div(html.Img(id=f'image-{key}',style={'width':'100%','aspectRatio':'4/3',
                  'objectFit':'contain','background':'#101827','borderRadius':'8px'})),
                  html.Div([dcc.Checklist(id=f'enabled-{key}',
                         options=[{'label':' Verarbeitung aktiv', 'value':'on'}],
                         value=['on'] if CFG['enabled'].get('lanes' if key == 'overlay' else 'canny' if key == 'edges' else key, True) else [], inline=True)], className='pt-2')
                  if key in ('roi','mask','blur','morph','edges','overlay') else html.Div(),
                  html.Div(controls(kind),className='pt-3')])
    ],className='h-100 shadow-sm'),xs=12,md=6,xl=4,className='mb-3')

def make_app(car,sensor):
    app=Dash(__name__,external_stylesheets=[dbc.themes.BOOTSTRAP])
    app.title='Camp2Code | Pipeline Studio'
    app.layout=dbc.Container([
        dbc.Row([dbc.Col([html.H2('Pipeline Studio',className='fw-bold mb-1'),
              html.P('Vom Rohbild zur erkannten Fahrspur · Live-Kalibrierung ohne Fahrbetrieb',
                     className='text-muted')],md=9),
              dbc.Col([html.Label('Bildverarbeitung',className='small fw-semibold'),
                       dcc.Dropdown(id='processor',options=[
                           {'label':'CopilotLaneProcessor · Diagnose','value':'copilot'},
                           {'label':'LaneProcessor · Legacy','value':'legacy'}],
                           value=CFG['processor'],clearable=False)],md=3)],className='pt-4 mb-3'),
        dbc.Alert('Kalibrierungsmodus: Dieses Dashboard startet keinen Fahrmodus und sendet keine Fahrbefehle.',
                  color='info'),
        dbc.Row([card(*stage) for stage in STAGES]),
        dbc.Row([dbc.Col([dbc.Button('Einstellungen speichern', id='save-config', color='success'),
                          html.Span(id='save-feedback', className='ms-3')], className='mb-3')]),
        dbc.Card([dbc.CardHeader('Performance · Soll / Ist'),dbc.CardBody([
            html.Div(id='status',className='mb-2'),
            dcc.Graph(id='frequency-chart',config={'displayModeBar':False})])],className='mb-4'),
        dcc.Interval(id='refresh',interval=600,n_intervals=0),
        html.Div(id='settings-status',className='visually-hidden')
    ],fluid=True,style={'maxWidth':'1560px'})

    @app.callback(Output('settings-status','children'),
                  Input('processor','value'),Input('crop','value'),Input('hue','value'),
                  Input('sat','value'),Input('val','value'),Input('blur','value'),Input('morph','value'),Input('canny','value'),
                  *[Input(f'enabled-{name}','value') for name in ('roi','mask','blur','morph','edges','overlay')])
    def update_settings(processor,crop,hue,sat,val,blur,morph,canny,*switches):
        # Einmalige atomare Parameteruebergabe; keine Fahrentscheidung.
        if crop[0] >= crop[1]:
            return 'ROI ungueltig'
        names=('roi','mask','blur','morph','canny','lanes')
        enabled={name: 'on' in values for name, values in zip(names,switches)}
        sensor.processing_parameters=(tuple(hue),tuple(sat),tuple(val),tuple(crop),
                                      int(blur),int(morph),tuple(canny),enabled)
        sensor.set_processor(processor)
        return 'Parameter uebernommen'

    @app.callback(Output('save-feedback', 'children'),
                  Input('save-config', 'n_clicks'),
                  State('processor', 'value'), State('crop', 'value'),
                  State('hue', 'value'), State('sat', 'value'), State('val', 'value'),
                  State('blur', 'value'), State('morph', 'value'), State('canny', 'value'),
                  *[State(f'enabled-{name}', 'value') for name in
                    ('roi', 'mask', 'blur', 'morph', 'edges', 'overlay')],
                  prevent_initial_call=True)
    def persist(_, processor, crop, hue, sat, val, blur, morph, canny, *switches):
        """Speichert Slider und Aktivierungsschalter fuer den naechsten Start."""
        names = ('roi', 'mask', 'blur', 'morph', 'canny', 'lanes')
        enabled = {name: 'on' in values for name, values in zip(names, switches)}
        config = dict(processor=processor, crop=crop, hue=hue, sat=sat, val=val,
                      blur=blur, morph=morph, canny=canny, enabled=enabled)
        try:
            save_pipeline_config(config)
            return 'Gespeichert in pipeline_studio.json'
        except OSError as exc:
            log.exception('Kalibrierung konnte nicht gespeichert werden')
            return f'Speichern fehlgeschlagen: {exc}'

    outputs=[Output(f'image-{key}','src') for key,_,_,_ in STAGES]
    @app.callback(outputs+[Output('status','children'),Output('frequency-chart','figure')],Input('refresh','n_intervals'))
    def refresh(_):
        data=car.camera_data
        images={}
        if data is not None:
            images={'raw':data.raw_image,**getattr(data,'stages',{})}
            if data.raw_image is not None and data.raw_image.size:
                images['overlay']=render_calibration_overlay(data)
        encoded=[image_uri(images.get(key)) for key,_,_,_ in STAGES]
        age=car.thread_metrics['camera'].age_seconds
        actual=car.thread_metrics['camera'].hz if age is not None and age<2 else 0.0
        target=float(sensor.scan_frequency)
        fig=go.Figure()
        fig.add_bar(name='Soll',x=['Kamera + Pipeline'],y=[target],marker_color='#7b8ca3')
        fig.add_bar(name='Ist',x=['Kamera + Pipeline'],y=[actual],marker_color='#14a78c')
        fig.update_layout(barmode='group',height=230,margin=dict(l=25,r=15,t=20,b=30),
                          yaxis_title='Hz',uirevision='freq')
        status=f'Frame {getattr(data,"frame_id",-1)} · {len(getattr(data,"lanes",()))} Linien · Soll {target:.1f} Hz / Ist {actual:.1f} Hz'
        return encoded+[status,fig]
    return app

def main():
    car=CameraCar()
    sensor=CameraSensor(car)
    stop=threading.Event()
    thread=threading.Thread(target=sensor.read_loop,args=(stop,),name='camera-pipeline',daemon=True)
    thread.start()
    def shutdown():
        stop.set()
        thread.join(timeout=2)
        # Kameraressourcen werden ueber die bestehende Camera-Klasse verwaltet.
        if hasattr(sensor,'close'):
            sensor.close()
    atexit.register(shutdown)
    make_app(car,sensor).run(host='0.0.0.0',port=8050,debug=False,use_reloader=False)

if __name__=='__main__':
    main()

================================================================================
FILE: logging_setup.py

import logging
from logging import handlers
import os


def setup_project_logging(default_level=logging.INFO):
    """
    Konfiguriert das Logging zentral. Erstellt für die Hauptkomponenten
    eigene Log-Dateien und setzt das initiale Log-Level.
    """
    # Verzeichnis für Logs erstellen, falls nicht vorhanden
    os.makedirs("logs", exist_ok=True)
    
    # Gemeinsames Format für alle Logs
    log_format = logging.Formatter('[%(filename)s:%(lineno)d]: %(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s')

    # Definition der Module und ihrer Log-Dateien
    log_mapping = {
        "BaseCar": "logs/car.log",
        "SonicCar": "logs/car.log",
        "SensorCar": "logs/car.log",
        "InfraredSensor": "logs/sensors.log",
        "UltrasonicSensor": "logs/sensors.log",
        "MockSensorCar": "logs/car.log",
        "RoomExplorer": "logs/controller.log",
        "ApproachObstacle": "logs/controller.log",
        "FollowLine": "logs/controller.log",
        "ModeOne": "logs/controller.log",
        "ModeTwo": "logs/controller.log",
        "KeyboardMode": "logs/controller.log"
    }

    for logger_name, log_file in log_mapping.items():
        logger = logging.getLogger(logger_name)
        logger.setLevel(default_level)
        
        # Handler hinzufügen, falls noch keiner existiert (verhindert doppelte Logs)
        if not logger.handlers:
            file_handler = handlers.RotatingFileHandler(log_file, encoding="utf-8", maxBytes=1024*1024, backupCount=3)
            file_handler.setFormatter(log_format)
            logger.addHandler(file_handler)
            
    # Optional: Ein globaler Konsolen-Logger für wichtige Ausgaben
    console_handler = logging.StreamHandler()
    console_handler.setFormatter(log_format)
    console_handler.setLevel(logging.WARNING) # Nur Warnungen/Fehler auf die Konsole
    logging.getLogger().addHandler(console_handler)

"""Gemeinsame Kameradaten ohne Abhaengigkeit von der Fahrzeugklasse."""
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import numpy as np

@dataclass
class CameraData:
    # Vorhandene Felder bleiben fuer die alten Verbraucher erhalten.
    process_image: np.ndarray
    raw_image: np.ndarray
    timestamp: int = 0
    frame_id: int = -1
    # Der Zeitstempel fuer Laufzeitmessungen ist unabhaengig von der Systemuhr.
    monotonic_ns: int = 0
    crop: tuple[float, float] = (0.1, 0.9)
    # Bereits erkannte Linien, einmal pro Frame ermittelt.
    lanes: tuple[tuple[float, float], ...] = ()
    # Optionale Diagnosebilder, nur im CopilotLaneProcessor vorhanden.
    stages: dict[str, np.ndarray] = field(default_factory=dict)
    processing_parameters: tuple | None = None
    processor_name: str | None = None

@dataclass
class CameraDataProcessed:
    # Uebergangsmodell: Dashboard und Recorder lesen diese Felder noch.
    process_image: np.ndarray
    process_image_lines: np.ndarray
    raw_image: np.ndarray
    line_data: np.ndarray
    lanes: list
    timestamp: int = 0
    steering_angle: int = 90
    steering_angle_car: int | None = None
    speed: int = 0
    frame_id: int = -1

"""Fahrentscheidung mit Referenz auf das zugehoerige Kamerabild."""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DrivingState:
    frame_id: int
    timestamp_ns: int
    steering_angle: float
    speed: float
    # Originale Linienrepraesentation vorerst beibehalten; kein Algorithmus-Umbau.
    lanes: Any = None
    steering_angle_car: float | None = None
    driving_mode: str | None = None

"""Zeitlich zuordenbare Messwerte ohne Hardware-Abhängigkeiten."""
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SensorSnapshot:
    infrared: tuple[float | None, ...] = (None, None, None, None, None)
    ultrasonic_cm: float | None = None
    timestamp_ns: int | None = None
    monotonic_ns: int | None = None
    infrared_timestamp_ns: int | None = None
    infrared_monotonic_ns: int | None = None
    ultrasonic_timestamp_ns: int | None = None
    ultrasonic_monotonic_ns: int | None = None

    def infrared_age_ns(self, now_ns: int) -> int | None:
        """Alter der letzten Infrarotmessung auf der monotonen Uhr."""
        return None if self.infrared_monotonic_ns is None else max(0, now_ns - self.infrared_monotonic_ns)

    def ultrasonic_age_ns(self, now_ns: int) -> int | None:
        """Alter der letzten Ultraschallmessung auf der monotonen Uhr."""
        return None if self.ultrasonic_monotonic_ns is None else max(0, now_ns - self.ultrasonic_monotonic_ns)

================================================================================
FILE: pipeline_ab.py

"""A/B-Kalibrierung auf einem eingefrorenen Rohbild, ohne Hardwarezugriff."""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from threading import RLock
import numpy as np
from pipeline_processors import CopilotLaneProcessor, LaneProcessor

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Comparison:
    frame_id: int
    image_a: np.ndarray
    image_b: np.ndarray
    lanes_a: tuple
    lanes_b: tuple

class PipelineAB:
    """Hält höchstens ein Rohbild und eine Referenzkonfiguration im RAM."""
    def __init__(self):
        self._lock = RLock()
        self._raw = None
        self._frame_id = None
        self._reference = None
        self._processor = 'copilot'

    def freeze(self, camera, parameters, processor='copilot'):
        """Kopiert nur das Rohbild; weitere Zwischenbilder werden nicht behalten."""
        if camera is None or camera.raw_image is None or camera.raw_image.size == 0:
            raise ValueError('Kein gültiges Kamerabild verfügbar')
        with self._lock:
            self._raw = camera.raw_image.copy()
            self._frame_id = camera.frame_id
            self._reference = tuple(parameters)
            self._processor = processor
            return self._frame_id

    def clear(self):
        with self._lock:
            self._raw = None
            self._reference = None
            self._frame_id = None

    def compare(self, candidate, processor=None):
        """Berechnet A und B auf demselben Rohbild, ohne Fahrentscheidung."""
        with self._lock:
            if self._raw is None:
                raise ValueError('Zuerst einen Frame für A/B einfrieren')
            raw = self._raw.copy()
            reference = self._reference
            frame_id = self._frame_id
            name_a = self._processor
        def run(name, params):
            instance = CopilotLaneProcessor() if name == 'copilot' else LaneProcessor()
            return instance.process(raw.copy(), params)
        a = run(name_a, reference)
        b = run(processor or name_a, tuple(candidate))
        return Comparison(frame_id, a.processed_image, b.processed_image,
                          tuple(a.lanes), tuple(b.lanes))

================================================================================

"""Persistente Einstellungen fuer das experimentelle Pipeline Studio."""
import json
from pathlib import Path

PATH = Path(__file__).with_name('pipeline_studio.json')
DEFAULT = {
    'processor': 'copilot', 'crop': [0.1, 0.9],
    'hue': [110, 179], 'sat': [0, 255], 'val': [0, 255],
    'blur': 3, 'morph': 5, 'canny': [50, 100],
    'enabled': {key: True for key in ('roi', 'mask', 'blur', 'morph', 'canny', 'lanes')},
}

def load():
    """Fehlende Felder werden mit Standardwerten ergaenzt."""
    if not PATH.exists():
        return {**DEFAULT, 'enabled': dict(DEFAULT['enabled'])}
    data = json.loads(PATH.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    result = {**DEFAULT, **data}
    result['enabled'] = {**DEFAULT['enabled'], **data.get('enabled', {})}
    return result

def save(data):
    """Schreibt erst eine temporaere Datei und ersetzt dann atomar."""
    temp = PATH.with_suffix('.json.tmp')
    temp.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
    temp.replace(PATH)

================================================================================
FILE: pipeline_processors.py
================================================================================

"""Austauschbare Bildverarbeitung, ohne Eingriff in die Fahrregelung."""
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import cv2
import numpy as np
from lane_detector import LaneDetector

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PipelineResult:
    processed_image: np.ndarray
    lanes: tuple
    stages: dict[str, np.ndarray] = field(default_factory=dict)

class LaneProcessor:
    """Legacy: bisherige Operationen und Reihenfolge, ohne Debug-Zwischenbilder."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.detector = LaneDetector()

    def process(self, raw: np.ndarray, params: tuple) -> PipelineResult:
        result = self._process(raw, params, capture=False)
        return result

    def _process(self, raw, params, capture):
        if raw is None or raw.size == 0:
            raise ValueError('Kamerabild ist leer')
        hue, saturation, value, crop, blur, morph = params[:6]
        canny = params[6] if len(params) > 6 else (50, 100)
        # Optionale Stufen sind reine Diagnose-/Experiment-Einstellungen.
        # Ohne Schalter bleibt die bisherige Verarbeitung unveraendert.
        enabled = params[7] if len(params) > 7 else {}
        def on(name):
            return bool(enabled.get(name, True))

        h = raw.shape[0]
        top = int(h * (1 - crop[1])) if on('roi') else 0
        bottom = int(h * (1 - crop[0])) if on('roi') else h
        if not (0 <= top < bottom <= h):
            raise ValueError('ROI ist ungueltig')
        roi = raw[top:bottom, :]
        hsv = cv2.cvtColor(roi, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV)
        # Ohne HSV-Filter verwenden wir ein Graustufenbild als Eingabe.
        mask = (cv2.inRange(hsv, np.array([hue[0], saturation[0], value[0]]),
                            np.array([hue[1], saturation[1], value[1]]))
                if on('mask') else cv2.cvtColor(roi, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY))
        blurred = (cv2.blur(mask, (max(1, int(blur)), max(1, int(blur))))
                   if on('blur') else mask)
        # Beim Weichzeichnen entstehen schwache Grauwerte am Maskenrand.
        # > 0 würde jeden Randpixel wieder vollweiß machen und die Linie
        # künstlich verbreitern. Die Hälfte der Maskenintensität trennt
        # Vordergrund und Hintergrund bei einer binären Eingangsmaske.
        # Ohne Blur bleibt die bisherige Binärisierung unverändert.
        threshold = 127 if on('blur') and on('mask') else 0
        binary = (blurred > threshold).astype(np.uint8) * 255
        if on('morph'):
            kernel = np.ones((max(1, int(morph)), max(1, int(morph))), dtype=np.uint8)
            closed = cv2.morphologyEx(binary, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, kernel)
        else:
            closed = binary
        edges = (cv2.Canny(closed, int(canny[0]), int(canny[1]))
                 if on('canny') else closed)
        lanes = self.detector.detect(edges) if on('lanes') else ()
        stages = {}
        if capture:
            # Kopien sind nur fuer die Diagnose vorgesehen, nicht fuer den Fahrregler.
            stages = {'roi': roi.copy(), 'hsv': cv2.cvtColor(hsv, cv2.COLOR_HSV2BGR),
                      'mask': mask.copy(), 'blur': blurred.copy(),
                      'morph': closed.copy(), 'edges': edges.copy()}
        return PipelineResult(edges, lanes, stages)

class CopilotLaneProcessor(LaneProcessor):
    """Drop-in: gleiche Erkennung, zusaetzlich sichtbare Pipeline-Stufen."""
    def process(self, raw: np.ndarray, params: tuple) -> PipelineResult:
        return self._process(raw, params, capture=True)

================================================================================
FILE: recovery_control.py

"""Zeitlich begrenzte manuelle Bergungssteuerung mit serverseitigem Watchdog."""
import threading
import time

class RecoveryControl:
    """Bewegt nur bei aktivem Deadman und stoppt bei ausbleibenden Befehlen."""
    def __init__(self, car, autonomous_running, timeout=0.35, speed=30):
        self._car = car
        self._autonomous_running = autonomous_running
        self._timeout = min(max(float(timeout), 0.15), 0.5)
        self._speed = min(max(int(speed), 1), 40)
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._enabled = False
        self._deadline = 0.0
        self._stop = threading.Event()
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._watchdog, daemon=True, name='recovery-watchdog')
        self._thread.start()

    def enable(self):
        with self._lock:
            if self._autonomous_running():
                raise RuntimeError('Autonomen Fahrmodus zuerst stoppen')
            self._car.stop()
            self._enabled = True
            self._deadline = 0

    def command(self, forward=False, reverse=False, left=False, right=False, deadman=False):
        with self._lock:
            if not self._enabled or self._autonomous_running():
                self._car.stop()
                self._enabled = False
                raise RuntimeError('Bergungsmodus nicht aktiv oder Fahrmodus läuft')
            if not deadman or forward == reverse:
                self._deadline = 0
                self._car.stop()
                return
            speed = self._speed if forward else -self._speed
            angle = 75 if left and not right else 105 if right and not left else 90
            self._deadline = time.monotonic() + self._timeout
            self._car.drive(speed, angle)

    def disable(self):
        with self._lock:
            self._enabled = False
            self._deadline = 0
            self._car.stop()

    @property
    def enabled(self):
        with self._lock:
            return self._enabled

    def _watchdog(self):
        while not self._stop.wait(0.05):
            with self._lock:
                if self._enabled and self._deadline and time.monotonic() > self._deadline:
                    self._deadline = 0
                    self._car.stop()

    def shutdown(self):
        self.disable()
        self._stop.set()
        self._thread.join(timeout=1)

================================================================================
END FILE: recovery_control.py
================================================================================

FILE: replay_hud.py
