"""HUD für gespeicherte Rohbilder ohne erneute Fahrspurerkennung."""
import json
import math
import cv2


def encode_lanes(lanes):
    """Übernimmt ausschließlich numerische Linienkoeffizienten aus dem Datenpaket."""
    result=[]
    for lane in lanes or ():
        if lane is None:
            result.append(None)
            continue
        try:
            values=list(lane)
            if len(values)!=2 or not all(math.isfinite(float(x)) for x in values):
                result.append(None)
            else:
                result.append([float(x) for x in values])
        except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
            result.append(None)
    return json.dumps(result)


def draw_hud(image, row):
    """Zeigt gespeicherte Linien und Winkel; keine neuen Messwerte werden berechnet."""
    if image is None or getattr(image,'size',0)==0:
        return image
    result=image.copy()
    height,width=result.shape[:2]
    def number(key):
        try:
            v=float(row.get(key,''))
            return v if math.isfinite(v) else None
        except (ValueError,TypeError):return None
    try:lanes=json.loads(row.get('lanes_json') or '[]')
    except (ValueError,TypeError):lanes=[]
    colors=[(50,230,100),(20,200,255)]
    endpoints=[]
    upper_points=[]
    for i,lane in enumerate(lanes[:2]):
        if not isinstance(lane,list) or len(lane)!=2:continue
        try:
            m,b=map(float,lane)
            if not all(map(math.isfinite,(m,b))):continue
            # Vorhandenes Linienmodell: x = m*y + b.
            y0,y1=int(height*.48),height-1
            x0=int(max(-width*2,min(width*3,m*y0+b)))
            x1=int(max(-width*2,min(width*3,m*y1+b)))
            cv2.line(result,(x0,y0),(x1,y1),colors[i],3,cv2.LINE_AA)
            endpoints.append(x1)
            upper_points.append(x0)
        except (ValueError,TypeError,OverflowError):continue
    angle=number('steering_angle_car')
    if angle is None:angle=number('steering_angle')
    # Lenkrichtung ist eine Visualisierung des Stellwerts, keine Vorhersage.
    if angle is not None:
        origin=(int(sum(endpoints)/2),height-1) if len(endpoints)==2 else (width//2,height-1)
        theta=math.radians(max(-70,min(70,angle-90)))
        length=int(height*.42)
        target=(int(origin[0]+length*math.sin(theta)),int(origin[1]-length*math.cos(theta)))
        cv2.arrowedLine(result,origin,target,(255,210,40),3,cv2.LINE_AA,tipLength=.13)
    shade=result.copy()
    cv2.rectangle(shade,(0,0),(width,min(height,76)),(12,20,32),-1)
    cv2.addWeighted(shade,.68,result,.32,0,result)
    def text(value,x,y,scale=.5):
        cv2.putText(result,str(value),(x,y),cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,scale,(245,245,245),1,cv2.LINE_AA)
    text('FRAME '+str(row.get('frame_id','?')),12,23,.65)
    text('STEER '+(f'{angle:.0f} deg' if angle is not None else 'N/A'),12,46)
    text('SPEED '+str(row.get('speed') or 'N/A'),min(width//2,210),46)
    text(str(row.get('data_status') or 'unknown').upper(),12,67,.4)
    if row.get('event_codes'):
        cv2.rectangle(result,(0,height-32),(width,height),(15,30,100),-1)
        text('EVENT: '+str(row['event_codes'])[:55],10,height-10,.45)
    return result

================================================================================
END FILE: replay_hud.py

"""Frame-ID-basierte Bereichsauswahl für den flüchtigen Replay-Puffer."""

def selected_bounds(relayout):
    """Liest eine Plotly-X-Achsenauswahl ohne Annahmen über andere Achsen."""
    if not isinstance(relayout, dict):
        return None
    if relayout.get('xaxis.autorange'):
        return None
    values = relayout.get('xaxis.range')
    if values is None:
        values = (relayout.get('xaxis.range[0]'), relayout.get('xaxis.range[1]'))
    if not isinstance(values, (list, tuple)) or len(values) != 2:
        return None
    try:
        a, b = float(values[0]), float(values[1])
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    import math
    if not math.isfinite(a) or not math.isfinite(b):
        return None
    return min(a, b), max(a, b)


def select_records(records, bounds):
    """Filtert ausschließlich vorhandene Frames und behält ihre Reihenfolge."""
    if bounds is None:
        return tuple(records)
    lo, hi = bounds
    return tuple(record for record in records if lo <= record.camera.frame_id <= hi)

================================================================================
END FILE: replay_selection.py

import logging
import threading
import time
import os
import sys

import AdvancedKeyboardMode
import Driving
import InfraredSensor
from SensorCar import SensorCar  # MockSensorCar entfernt
import UltrasonicSensor
import logging_setup
from ConfigReader import ConfigReader

def clear_screen():
    sys.stdout.write("\033[2J\033[H")
    sys.stdout.flush()

def hole_modus_parameter(wahl: str) -> str:
    """Liest die spezifischen Parameter für den gewählten Modus aus der Config."""
    cfg_sonic = ConfigReader("soniccar_controller")
    cfg_pid = ConfigReader("sensorcar_controller")
    
    if wahl in ["1", "2"]:  # Basisfahrt / Kreise
        return f"Start-Tempo: {cfg_sonic.get_int('start_speed', 60)}%"
    elif wahl in ["3", "4"]:  # Hindernis / Explorer
        return f"Stopp-Abstand: {cfg_sonic.get_int('ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop', 5)}cm | Start-Tempo: {cfg_sonic.get_int('start_speed', 60)}%"
    elif wahl == "5":  # Follow Line
        return (f"P: {cfg_pid.get_int('korrektur_proportional', 25)} | "
                f"D: {cfg_pid.get_int('korrektur_differential', 10)} | "
                f"Kontrast-Limit: {cfg_pid.get_float('minimum_line_contrast', 0.5)}")
    return "Keine spezifischen Parameter"

def zeige_live_dashboard(sc, wahl: str):
    """Baut das erweiterte Dashboard im Speicher und gibt es flackerfrei aus."""
    speed = getattr(sc, "speed", 0)
    distance = getattr(sc, "distance", 300)
    steering_angle = getattr(sc, "steering_angle", 90)
    
    p_val = getattr(sc, "_p_wert", 0.0)
    i_val = getattr(sc, "_i_wert", 0.0)
    d_val = getattr(sc, "_d_wert", 0.0)
    
    ir_values = getattr(sc, "ir_sensor_values", [[1.,1.,1.,1.,1.]])
    if isinstance(ir_values, list) and len(ir_values) > 0 and isinstance(ir_values[0], list):
        ir_display = ir_values[-1]
    else:
        ir_display = ir_values

    # Sensor-Statistiken berechnen
    s_min = min(ir_display) if ir_display else 0.0
    s_max = max(ir_display) if ir_display else 0.0
    s_delta = s_max - s_min

    try:
        is_on_line_state = "JA 🛣️ " if sc.is_on_line() else "NEIN"
    except Exception:
        is_on_line_state = "UNKNOWN"

    ir_str = " ".join([f"S{i+1}:[{v:.2f}]" for i, v in enumerate(ir_display)])
    aktuelle_params = hole_modus_parameter(wahl)

    buffer = (
        "\033[H"  # Cursor nach oben links
        f"┌" + "─"*74 + "┐\033[K\n"
        f"│ 📊 LIVE-DASHBOARD (Fahrt aktiv)                                         │\033[K\n"
        f"├" + "─"*74 + "┤\033[K\n"
        f"│ 🏎️  Tempo: {speed:4d} %   | 📐 Lenkwinkel: {steering_angle:3d}°   | 🛣️  Auf Linie?: {is_on_line_state:<10s} │\033[K\n"
        f"│ 📏 US-Distanz: {distance:3d} cm                                                 │\033[K\n"
        f"│ 📷 IR-Sensoren: {ir_str:<53s} │\033[K\n"
        f"│ 📈 IR-Stats:    Min: [{s_min:.2f}]  |  Max: [{s_max:.2f}]  |  Delta: [{s_delta:.2f}]           │\033[K\n"
        f"│ 🎛️  PID-Status:  P: {p_val:5.2f}  |  I: {i_val:5.2f}  |  D: {d_val:5.2f}                       │\033[K\n"
        f"├" + "─"*74 + "┤\033[K\n"
        f"│ ⚙️  Aktive Config-Werte: {aktuelle_params:<47s} │\033[K\n"
        f"└" + "─"*74 + "┘\033[K\n"
        f"\n  🛑 HINWEIS: Drücke [STRG + C] um die Fahrt zu BEENDEN.\033[J"
    )
    
    sys.stdout.write(buffer)
    sys.stdout.flush()

def parameter_menue():
    cfg_controller = ConfigReader("soniccar_controller")
    cfg_pid = ConfigReader("sensorcar_controller")
    
    while True:
        clear_screen()
        print("="*65)
        print(" 🛠️  FAHRPARAMETER ANPASSEN (Temporär für diese Session)")
        print("="*65)
        print(f"  [1] Start-Geschwindigkeit (Sonic) : {cfg_controller.get_int('start_speed', 60)} %")
        print(f"  [2] Mindest-Stopp-Abstand (Sonic) : {cfg_controller.get_int('ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop', 5)} cm")
        print(f"  [3] PID: Proportional (P-Wert)    : {cfg_pid.get_int('korrektur_proportional', 25)}")
        print(f"  [4] PID: Differential (D-Wert)    : {cfg_pid.get_int('korrektur_differential', 10)}")
        print("-"*65)
        print("  [0] Zurück zum Hauptmenü")
        print("="*65)
        
        wahl = input("\n  Welchen Parameter ändern? -> ").strip()
        if wahl == "0":
            break
        elif wahl == "1":
            val = input("  Neuer Wert für Start-Geschwindigkeit (20-100): ").strip()
            if val.isdigit(): cfg_controller._json_config["soniccar_controller"]["start_speed"] = int(val)
        elif wahl == "2":
            val = input("  Neuer Wert für Stopp-Abstand: ").strip()
            if val.isdigit(): cfg_controller._json_config["soniccar_controller"]["ultrasonic_max_distance_to_stop"] = int(val)
        elif wahl == "3":
            val = input("  Neuer P-Wert für Linienfolger: ").strip()
            if val.isdigit(): cfg_pid._json_config["sensorcar_controller"]["korrektur_proportional"] = int(val)
        elif wahl == "4":
            val = input("  Neuer D-Wert für Linienfolger: ").strip()
            if val.isdigit(): cfg_pid._json_config["sensorcar_controller"]["korrektur_differential"] = int(val)

def main():
    logging_setup.setup_project_logging(logging.INFO)
    root_logger = logging.getLogger()

    # Nutzt jetzt regulär das echte SensorCar()
    sc = SensorCar()
    sc.stop()

    us = UltrasonicSensor.UltrasonicSensor(sc)
    ir = InfraredSensor.InfraredSensor(sc)
    
    stop_event = threading.Event()
    us_sensor_thread = threading.Thread(target=us.read_loop, args=[stop_event], daemon=True)
    ir_sensor_thread = threading.Thread(target=ir.read_loop, args=[stop_event], daemon=True)
    
    ir_sensor_thread.start()
    us_sensor_thread.start()

    modus_klassen = {
        "1": {"name": "Forward / Backward", "class": Driving.ModeOne, "interactive": False},
        "2": {"name": "Circles", "class": Driving.ModeTwo, "interactive": False},
        "3": {"name": "Approach Obstacle", "class": Driving.ApproachObstacle, "interactive": False},
        "4": {"name": "Room Explorer", "class": Driving.RoomExplorer, "interactive": False},
        "5": {"name": "Follow Line", "class": Driving.FollowLine, "interactive": False},
        "6": {"name": "Keyboard Control (Interactive)", "class": AdvancedKeyboardMode.KeyboardMode, "interactive": True}
    }

    try:
        while True:
            clear_screen()
            print("="*60)
            print("  🏎️   SENSOR CAR - CONTROL INTERFACE")
            print("="*60)
            print("  Verfügbare Fahrmodi:")
            print("-"*60)
            
            for key, info in modus_klassen.items():
                print(f"  [{key}] {info['name']}")
                
            print("-"*60)
            print("  [P] 🛠️  FAHRPARAMETER KONFIGURIEREN (Config)")
            print("  [0] ❌ PROGRAMM BEENDEN")
            print("="*60)

            wahl = input("\n  Deine Wahl -> ").strip()

            if wahl == "0":
                break
            
            elif wahl.upper() == "P":
                parameter_menue()

            elif wahl in modus_klassen:
                mode_info = modus_klassen[wahl]
                mode_instance = mode_info["class"](car=sc)
                
                if mode_info["interactive"]:
                    clear_screen()
                    mode_instance.start()
                    sc.stop()
                else:
                    root_logger.disabled = True
                    clear_screen()
                    mode_instance.start()
                    
                    try:
                        while True:
                            zeige_live_dashboard(sc, wahl)
                            time.sleep(0.1)
                    except KeyboardInterrupt:
                        sys.stdout.write("\n\n")
                        sys.stdout.flush()
                        print("  Abbruch-Signal registriert...")
                    
                    mode_instance.stop()
                    sc.stop()
                    
                    root_logger.disabled = False
                    print("\n  Modus erfolgreich beendet. Zurück zum Menü mit <ENTER>.")
                    input()
            else:
                print("\n  ❌ Ungültige Auswahl!")
                time.sleep(1)

    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\n\n  👋 Programm per Haupt-STRG+C abgebrochen.")
    finally:
        print("\n  Räume Hardware auf...")
        stop_event.set()
        sc.stop()
        us_sensor_thread.join()
        ir_sensor_thread.join()
        print("  [Done] Ready to pack!")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

================================================================================
END FILE: run.py

================================================================================
FILE: session_recorder.py
================================================================================

"""Persistente, begrenzte Frame-Aufzeichnung unabhängig vom Browser."""
import csv
import json
import logging
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
import cv2
from replay_hud import encode_lanes

FIELDS = ('frame_id','camera_timestamp_ns','camera_monotonic_ns','driving_timestamp_ns','image_filename','steering_angle','steering_angle_car','speed','lane_count','lanes_json','event_codes','data_status')

class SessionRecorder:
    """Schreibt nur neue Frames aus dem vorhandenen Ringbuffer auf die SD-Karte."""
    def __init__(self, car, root='Data_AI/sessions', interval=0.12, event_source=None):
        self.car=car
        self.event_source=event_source
        self._event_keys=set()
        self.root=Path(root)
        self.interval=interval
        self._lock=threading.RLock()
        self._stop=threading.Event()
        self._thread=None
        self._last_id=-1
        self.count=0
        self.path=None
        self.error=None
        self._log=logging.getLogger(__name__)

    def is_running(self):
        return self._thread is not None and self._thread.is_alive() and not self._stop.is_set()

    def start(self):
        with self._lock:
            if self._thread is not None and self._thread.is_alive():
                if not self._stop.is_set(): return
                raise RuntimeError('Recorder beendet noch den vorherigen Thread')
            self.root.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
            self.path=self.root/('session_'+datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f'))
            (self.path/'frames').mkdir(parents=True)
            with (self.path/'events.csv').open('w',newline='',encoding='utf-8') as event_file:
                csv.DictWriter(event_file,fieldnames=('timestamp_ns','frame_id','code','detail')).writeheader()
            self._event_keys=set()
            with (self.path/'frames.csv').open('w',newline='',encoding='utf-8') as f:
                csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=FIELDS).writeheader()
            (self.path/'manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps({'schema_version':1,'created_at':datetime.now().isoformat(),'status':'recording'},indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
            self._last_id=-1;self.count=0;self.error=None;self._stop.clear()
            self._thread=threading.Thread(target=self._loop,name='session-recorder',daemon=True)
            self._thread.start()

    def stop(self):
        self._stop.set()
        thread=self._thread
        if thread and thread is not threading.current_thread():
            thread.join(timeout=5)
            if thread.is_alive():
                raise RuntimeError('Recorder-Thread konnte nicht beendet werden')
        if self.path:
            manifest=self.path/'manifest.json'
            manifest.write_text(json.dumps({'schema_version':1,'status':'error' if self.error else 'stopped','frames':self.count,'error':self.error},indent=2),encoding='utf-8')

    def _loop(self):
        while not self._stop.is_set():
            try: self.capture_once()
            except Exception as exc:
                self.error=str(exc)
                self._log.exception('Session-Aufzeichnung fehlgeschlagen')
                self._stop.set()
            self._stop.wait(self.interval)

    def _new_events(self):
        if self.event_source is None:return []
        events=[]
        for event in self.event_source():
            key=(event.timestamp_ns,event.frame_id,event.code,event.detail)
            if key not in self._event_keys:
                events.append((key,event))
        return events

    def capture_once(self):
        pending=self._new_events()
        if pending:
            with (self.path/'events.csv').open('a',newline='',encoding='utf-8') as f:
                writer=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=('timestamp_ns','frame_id','code','detail'))
                for key,event in pending:
                    writer.writerow(dict(timestamp_ns=event.timestamp_ns,frame_id=event.frame_id,
                                         code=event.code,detail=event.detail))
                    self._event_keys.add(key)
        records=self.car.frame_history.snapshot()
        for record in records:
            cam=record.camera
            if cam.frame_id<=self._last_id:continue
            image=cam.raw_image
            if image is None or not getattr(image,'size',0):
                self._last_id=cam.frame_id;continue
            drive=record.driving if record.driving is not None and record.driving.frame_id==cam.frame_id else None
            filename=f'frames/frame_{cam.frame_id:010d}.jpg'
            target=self.path/filename
            if not cv2.imwrite(str(target),image):
                raise OSError(f'JPEG konnte nicht geschrieben werden: {target}')
            row=dict(frame_id=cam.frame_id,camera_timestamp_ns=cam.timestamp,
                camera_monotonic_ns=cam.monotonic_ns,driving_timestamp_ns=drive.timestamp_ns if drive else '',
                image_filename=filename,steering_angle=drive.steering_angle if drive else '',
                steering_angle_car=drive.steering_angle_car if drive else '',
                speed=drive.speed if drive else '',lane_count=len(cam.lanes) if cam.lanes is not None else 0,
                lanes_json=encode_lanes(cam.lanes),
                event_codes='|'.join(event.code for _,event in pending if event.frame_id==cam.frame_id),
                data_status='complete' if drive else 'no_driving_state')
            try:
                with (self.path/'frames.csv').open('a',newline='',encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=FIELDS).writerow(row)
            except Exception:
                target.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                raise
            self._last_id=cam.frame_id
            self.count+=1


def list_sessions(root='Data_AI/sessions'):
    base=Path(root)
    if not base.is_dir():return []
    return sorted((p.name for p in base.iterdir() if p.is_dir() and (p/'frames.csv').is_file()),reverse=True)


def load_session(name,root='Data_AI/sessions'):
    base=Path(root).resolve()
    path=(base/name).resolve()
    if path.parent!=base or not (path/'frames.csv').is_file():
        raise ValueError('Ungültige Session')
    with (path/'frames.csv').open(newline='',encoding='utf-8') as f:
        rows=list(csv.DictReader(f))
    return path,rows

================================================================================
FILE: stadia_controller.py
================================================================================

import time
import select
from evdev import InputDevice, list_devices, ecodes
from basisklassen import *

print("Verfügbare Geräte:", list_devices())
controller = None

PREFER = "stadia"

for path in list_devices():
    dev = InputDevice(path)
    name = (dev.name or "").lower()
    if PREFER in name:
        controller = dev
        controller.grab()
        break
        
if controller is None:
    raise RuntimeError("Kein Stadia Controller gefunden! Ist er eingeschaltet und verbunden?")

print(f"Benutze Gerät: {controller.path} - {controller.name}")

# Feste Werte für den Stadia Controller über Bluetooth
center_value = 127

steer = 0
speed = 0

steer_min = 0.1
speed_min = 15

# Initialisierung deiner Basisklassen
bw = BackWheels()
fw = FrontWheels()

print("Fahrprogramm gestartet. Drücke STRG+C zum Beenden.")

try:
    while True:
        # Abfrage des Controllers mit einem kleinen Timeout (0.005s)
        select_event, _, _ = select.select([controller.fd], [], [], 0.005)
        
        if select_event:
            for event in controller.read():
                if event.type == ecodes.EV_ABS:
                    
                    # RECHTER STICK: Hoch/Runter (Achse 2) -> BACK WHEELS (Antrieb)
                    if event.code == 2:
                        speed = (event.value - center_value) / 128

                    # LINKER STICK: Links/Rechts (Achse 0) -> FRONT WHEELS (Lenkung)
                    if event.code == 0:
                        steer = (event.value - center_value) / 128

        # =========================================================
        # INTERNE LOGIK 1: FRONT WHEELS (Lenkung via Linker Stick)
        # =========================================================
        if (steer < steer_min) and (steer > -steer_min):
            fw.turn(90)  # Geradeaus, wenn Stick in der Mitte ist
        else:
            steer_angle = int(90 + 45 * steer)
            # Sicherheitsbegrenzung, damit die Servos nicht überdrehen
            steer_angle = max(45, min(135, steer_angle)) 
            fw.turn(steer_angle)

        # =========================================================
        # INTERNE LOGIK 2: BACK WHEELS (Antrieb via Rechter Stick)
        # =========================================================
        speed_0_to_100 = int(abs(speed) * 100)

        # Stick nach unten -> Rückwärts fahren
        if (speed_0_to_100 >= speed_min) and (speed >= 0):
            bw.backward()
            bw.speed = speed_0_to_100
            
        # Stick nach oben -> Vorwärts fahren (da Werte negativ werden beim Drücken nach oben)
        if (speed_0_to_100 >= speed_min) and (speed < 0):
            bw.forward()
            bw.speed = speed_0_to_100
            
        # Stopp-Bedingungen (Deadzone), wenn der Stick nahe der Mitte ist
        if (speed_0_to_100 <= speed_min):
            bw.speed = 0

except KeyboardInterrupt:
    # Sicherheits-Netz: Wenn du das Skript abbrichst, stoppt das Auto sofort
    print("\nProgramm beendet. Stoppe Motoren...")
    bw.speed = 0
    fw.turn(90)

================================================================================
FILE: telemetry/frequency_counter.py
================================================================================

"""Misst die tatsaechliche Zyklusfrequenz in einem gleitenden Fenster."""
from collections import deque
from threading import Lock
from time import monotonic

class FrequencyCounter:
    def __init__(self, window: int = 30):
        if window < 2:
            raise ValueError("window muss mindestens 2 sein")
        self._times = deque(maxlen=window)
        self._lock = Lock()

    def tick(self) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._times.append(monotonic())

    @property
    def hz(self) -> float:
        with self._lock:
            if len(self._times) < 2:
                return 0.0
            delta = self._times[-1] - self._times[0]
            return (len(self._times) - 1) / delta if delta > 0 else 0.0

    @property
    def age_seconds(self) -> float | None:
        with self._lock:
            return monotonic() - self._times[-1] if self._times else None

END FILE: telemetry/frequency_counter.py

================================================================================
FILE: telemetry/ring_buffer.py
================================================================================

"""Begrenzte Historie der zuletzt veroeffentlichten Kameraframes."""
from collections import deque
from dataclasses import dataclass
from threading import RLock
from models import CameraData, DrivingState

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FrameRecord:
    camera: CameraData
    driving: DrivingState | None = None

class FrameRingBuffer:
    def __init__(self, maxlen: int = 60):
        if maxlen < 1:
            raise ValueError("maxlen muss positiv sein")
        self._items = deque(maxlen=maxlen)
        self._lock = RLock()

    def add_camera(self, data: CameraData) -> None:
        # Nur Referenzen speichern. Bereits publizierte Bilder nicht veraendern!
        with self._lock:
            self._items.append(FrameRecord(camera=data))

    def add_driving(self, state: DrivingState) -> bool:
        with self._lock:
            for index in range(len(self._items) - 1, -1, -1):
                record = self._items[index]
                if record.camera.frame_id == state.frame_id:
                    self._items[index] = FrameRecord(record.camera, state)
                    return True
        return False

    def snapshot(self) -> tuple[FrameRecord, ...]:
        # Kopie der Referenzliste, damit Leser den Lock nicht lange halten.
        with self._lock:
            return tuple(self._items)

END FILE: telemetry/ring_buffer.py

"""Regressionstest für das Binärisieren nach aktiviertem Blur."""
import sys
import types
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch
import cv2
import numpy as np

# Für den Test der Pipeline ist kein echter Linien-Detektor nötig.
module = types.ModuleType('lane_detector')
class Detector:
    def detect(self, image):
        return ()
module.LaneDetector = Detector
sys.modules['lane_detector'] = module
from pipeline_processors import CopilotLaneProcessor


class TestBlurMaske(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_blur_verbreitert_binäre_linie_nicht_durch_geringste_grauwerte(self):
        image = np.zeros((90, 120, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
        image[20:70, 56:64] = (255, 255, 255)
        base = ([0, 179], [0, 255], [200, 255], [0, 1], 15, 1, [50, 100])
        enabled = dict(roi=False, mask=True, blur=True, morph=False,
                       canny=False, lanes=False)
        processor = CopilotLaneProcessor()
        on = processor.process(image, (*base, enabled))
        off = processor.process(image, (*base, {**enabled, 'blur': False}))
        self.assertLessEqual(np.count_nonzero(on.stages['morph']),
                             np.count_nonzero(off.stages['morph']) * 1.2)
        self.assertTrue(np.any((on.stages['blur'] > 0) & (on.stages['blur'] < 127)))

    def test_blur_off_bleibt_unveraendert(self):
        image = np.zeros((32, 32, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
        image[:, 12:20] = 255
        enabled = dict(roi=False, mask=True, blur=False, morph=False,
                       canny=False, lanes=False)
        result = CopilotLaneProcessor().process(image,
            ([0,179], [0,255], [200,255], [0,1], 15, 1, [50,100], enabled))
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(result.stages['mask'], result.stages['blur'])
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(result.stages['mask'], result.stages['morph'])

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

"""Hardwarefreie Tests für die zentrale Anwendungsverwaltung."""
import threading
import sys
from unittest.mock import MagicMock
for name in ("CameraCar", "CameraSensor", "Save_Data_AI", "pipeline_config", "Driving"):
    sys.modules.setdefault(name, MagicMock())
import unittest
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import Mock
from car_application import CarApplication

class TestCarApplication(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.car = Mock()
        self.car.driving_state = None
        self.car.camera_data = None
        self.car.thread_metrics = {}
        self.sensor = Mock()
        self.sensor.read_loop = Mock(side_effect=lambda event: event.wait())
        self.mode = Mock()
        self.mode.is_running.return_value = False
        self.recorder = Mock()
        self.recorder.is_running.return_value = False
        self.app = CarApplication(self.car, self.sensor, {'camera': self.mode}, self.recorder, start_camera=False)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.app.shutdown()

    def test_keine_zweite_kamera(self):
        self.app.start_camera()
        thread = self.app._camera_thread
        self.app.start_camera()
        self.assertIs(thread, self.app._camera_thread)

    def test_doppelstart_abgewiesen(self):
        self.mode.is_running.return_value = True
        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
            self.app.start_mode('camera')

    def test_kalibrierung_nicht_waehrend_fahrt(self):
        self.mode.is_running.return_value = True
        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
            self.app.set_pipeline({'crop': (0.1,0.9)})

    def test_shutdown_idempotent(self):
        self.app.shutdown()
        self.app.shutdown()
        self.assertTrue(self.app.stop_event.is_set())

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

"""Hardwarefreie Tests der Lenkwinkelanzeige."""
import unittest
import numpy as np
from dashboard_steering import draw_steering_overlay

class TestSteeringOverlay(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_kein_bild(self):
        self.assertIsNone(draw_steering_overlay(None, 90))

    def test_original_bleibt_unveraendert(self):
        original = np.zeros((180, 320, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
        result = draw_steering_overlay(original, 105)
        self.assertTrue(np.all(original == 0))
        self.assertTrue(np.any(result != 0))

    def test_fehlender_winkel(self):
        result = draw_steering_overlay(np.zeros((180, 320, 3), dtype=np.uint8), None)
        self.assertEqual(result.shape, (180, 320, 3))

"""Prüft die Zustandsübergänge der Fahrdiagnose ohne Hardware."""
import unittest
from types import SimpleNamespace
from driving_health import DrivingHealthMonitor

class DiagnosticsTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_held_and_stop_reason(self):
        monitor = DrivingHealthMonitor()
        camera = SimpleNamespace(frame_id=8, lanes=((1, 2),))
        monitor.observe(camera, None, True, diagnostic={
            'frame_id': 8, 'raw_angle': None, 'held_previous_angle': True,
            'previous_angle': 102, 'requested_angle': 102, 'actual_angle': 101})
        monitor.observe(camera, None, False, stop_reason='LANE_TIMEOUT')
        codes = [event.code for event in monitor.events()]
        self.assertIn('STEERING_HELD', codes)
        self.assertIn('STOP_REASON', codes)
        self.assertIn('CONTROLLER_STOPPED', codes)
        self.assertEqual(len([x for x in monitor.events() if x.code == 'STOP_REASON']), 1)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

"""Hardwarefreie Kernprüfungen der Datenmodelle und des Ringbuffers."""
import unittest
import numpy as np
from models import CameraData, DrivingState
from models.sensor_snapshot import SensorSnapshot
from telemetry.ring_buffer import FrameRingBuffer

class TestPaketAB(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_ringbuffer_zuordnung(self):
        ring = FrameRingBuffer(maxlen=2)
        for i in (1, 2):
            ring.add_camera(CameraData(np.zeros((2,2)), np.zeros((2,2)), frame_id=i))
        self.assertTrue(ring.add_driving(DrivingState(1, 1, 90, 30)))
        self.assertFalse(ring.add_driving(DrivingState(3, 1, 90, 30)))
        self.assertEqual(ring.snapshot()[0].driving.frame_id, 1)
        ring.add_camera(CameraData(np.zeros((2,2)), np.zeros((2,2)), frame_id=3))
        self.assertEqual([r.camera.frame_id for r in ring.snapshot()], [2,3])
    def test_sensor_alter(self):
        s=SensorSnapshot(infrared_monotonic_ns=10,ultrasonic_monotonic_ns=20)
        self.assertEqual(s.infrared_age_ns(30),20)
        self.assertEqual(s.ultrasonic_age_ns(30),10)
    def test_fehlende_entscheidung(self):
        r=FrameRingBuffer();r.add_camera(CameraData(np.zeros((1,1)),np.zeros((1,1)),frame_id=1))
        self.assertIsNone(r.snapshot()[0].driving)

if __name__ == '__main__': unittest.main()

END FILE: tests/test_paket_ab.py

"""Hardwarefreie A/B-Tests mit deterministischem Mock-Prozessor."""
import sys
import types
import unittest
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import patch
import numpy as np

# Der Test verwendet nur die veröffentlichte Prozessorschnittstelle.
module = types.ModuleType('pipeline_processors')
class FakeProcessor:
    def process(self, raw, params):
        return SimpleNamespace(processed_image=raw.copy() + int(params[0]), lanes=((1,2),))
module.CopilotLaneProcessor = FakeProcessor
module.LaneProcessor = FakeProcessor
with patch.dict(sys.modules, {'pipeline_processors': module}):
    from pipeline_ab import PipelineAB

class TestPipelineAB(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_identischer_frame_unterschiedliche_parameter(self):
        lab = PipelineAB()
        raw = np.zeros((5, 5), dtype=np.uint8)
        lab.freeze(SimpleNamespace(raw_image=raw, frame_id=42), (1,))
        raw[:] = 99
        comparison = lab.compare((2,))
        self.assertEqual(comparison.frame_id, 42)
        self.assertTrue(np.all(comparison.image_a == 1))
        self.assertTrue(np.all(comparison.image_b == 2))

    def test_reset(self):
        lab = PipelineAB()
        lab.clear()
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            lab.compare((1,))

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

END FILE: tests/test_pipeline_ab.py

"""Prüft das Replay-HUD und das Ereignisprotokoll ohne Hardware."""
import csv
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
import numpy as np
from replay_hud import draw_hud, encode_lanes
from session_recorder import SessionRecorder

class ReplayHUDTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_hud_uses_copy(self):
        raw=np.zeros((240,320,3),dtype=np.uint8)
        result=draw_hud(raw,{'frame_id':'4','steering_angle_car':'110','speed':'30',
            'lanes_json':'[[0.1,10],[-0.1,290]]','event_codes':'LANE_PARTIAL'})
        self.assertFalse(np.array_equal(raw,result))
        self.assertFalse(raw.any())

    def test_events_are_separate(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            event=SimpleNamespace(timestamp_ns=200,frame_id=4,code='LANE_LOST',detail='Keine Linien')
            cam=SimpleNamespace(frame_id=4,raw_image=np.zeros((12,16,3),dtype=np.uint8),
                timestamp=100,monotonic_ns=80,lanes=((.1,2),None))
            car=SimpleNamespace(frame_history=SimpleNamespace(snapshot=lambda:(SimpleNamespace(camera=cam,driving=None),)))
            recorder=SessionRecorder(car,folder,event_source=lambda:(event,))
            recorder.start();recorder.stop();recorder.capture_once();recorder.capture_once()
            with (recorder.path/'events.csv').open(newline='') as f:events=list(csv.DictReader(f))
            with (recorder.path/'frames.csv').open(newline='') as f:frames=list(csv.DictReader(f))
            self.assertEqual(len(events),1)
            self.assertEqual(events[0]['code'],'LANE_LOST')
            self.assertEqual(frames[0]['event_codes'],'LANE_LOST')
            self.assertIn('null',frames[0]['lanes_json'])

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

"""Hardwarefreie Tests für Framebereich und Plotly-Selektion."""
import unittest
from types import SimpleNamespace
from replay_selection import selected_bounds, select_records

class ReplaySelectionTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_bereich(self):
        self.assertEqual(selected_bounds({'xaxis.range[0]': 30, 'xaxis.range[1]': 10}), (10, 30))
        self.assertIsNone(selected_bounds({'xaxis.autorange': True}))
    def test_frames_ohne_erfindung(self):
        records = [SimpleNamespace(camera=SimpleNamespace(frame_id=i)) for i in (3, 7, 11, 20)]
        self.assertEqual([r.camera.frame_id for r in select_records(records, (5, 15))], [7, 11])
        self.assertEqual(select_records(records, (30, 40)), ())

"""Hardwarefreie Tests der Session-Aufzeichnung und des Ladevorgangs."""
import csv
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import patch
import numpy as np
from session_recorder import SessionRecorder, load_session, list_sessions

class SessionTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_recording_and_replay(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            camera=SimpleNamespace(frame_id=7,raw_image=np.zeros((12,16,3),dtype=np.uint8),timestamp=100,monotonic_ns=80,lanes=())
            car=SimpleNamespace(frame_history=SimpleNamespace(snapshot=lambda:(SimpleNamespace(camera=camera,driving=None),)))
            recorder=SessionRecorder(car,folder)
            recorder.start()
            recorder.stop()
            recorder.capture_once()
            recorder.capture_once()
            self.assertEqual(recorder.count,1)
            path,rows=load_session(recorder.path.name,folder)
            self.assertEqual(len(rows),1)
            self.assertEqual(rows[0]['data_status'],'no_driving_state')
            self.assertTrue((path/rows[0]['image_filename']).is_file())
            self.assertEqual(len(list_sessions(folder)),1)
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):load_session('../other',folder)

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

"""Tests für Zustandsbeobachtung und sichere Bergung ohne Hardware."""
import time
import unittest
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import Mock
from driving_health import DrivingHealthMonitor
from recovery_control import RecoveryControl

class HealthTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_transitionen(self):
        monitor = DrivingHealthMonitor()
        monitor.observe(SimpleNamespace(frame_id=1, lanes=()), None, True)
        monitor.observe(SimpleNamespace(frame_id=2, lanes=((1,2),)), None, True)
        monitor.observe(SimpleNamespace(frame_id=3, lanes=((1,2),(3,4))), None, False)
        self.assertEqual([e.code for e in monitor.events()],
            ['LANE_LOST', 'LANE_PARTIAL', 'LANES_DETECTED', 'CONTROLLER_STOPPED'])

class RecoveryTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.car = Mock()
        self.running = False
        self.recovery = RecoveryControl(self.car, lambda: self.running, timeout=0.15)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.recovery.shutdown()

    def test_keine_autonome_ueberlagerung(self):
        self.running = True
        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
            self.recovery.enable()
        self.car.drive.assert_not_called()

    def test_deadman_und_watchdog(self):
        self.recovery.enable()
        self.recovery.command(forward=True, deadman=False)
        self.car.drive.assert_not_called()
        self.recovery.command(forward=True, deadman=True)
        self.car.drive.assert_called_once_with(30,90)
        time.sleep(0.24)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(self.car.stop.call_count, 2)

    def test_rueckwaerts_und_stop(self):
        self.recovery.enable()
        self.recovery.command(reverse=True, left=True, deadman=True)
        self.car.drive.assert_called_once_with(-30,75)
        self.recovery.disable()
        self.assertFalse(self.recovery.enabled)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

================================================================================
END FILE: tests/test_v5.py

================================================================================
FILE: utils/ir_sensor_utils.py
================================================================================

import logging
from logging.handlers import TimedRotatingFileHandler
import os
import sys
import threading
import time
from typing import Tuple

import numpy as np
from InfraredSensor import InfraredSensor
from SensorCar import SensorCar

import time

def starte_poti_tuning(ir_sensors:InfraredSensor) -> Tuple[list[float], list[float], list[list[float]]]:
    print("\n" + "="*60)
    print(" 🔧 HARDWARE-TUNING: INFRAROT-SENSOREN (POTI)")
    print("="*60)
    print(" ANLEITUNG:")
    print(" 1. Stelle das Auto so auf die Strecken-Kante, dass")
    print("    z. B. Sensor 1 auf WEISS und Sensor 5 auf SCHWARZ steht.")
    print(" 2. Nimm einen kleinen Schraubendreher und drehe")
    print("    MILLIMETERWEISE am Potentiometer des Sensor-Boards.")
    print(" 3. Dein Ziel: Maximiere das 'DELTA' (Differenz).")
    print("    -> Weißer Boden = Hoher Wert")
    print("    -> Schwarze Linie = Niedriger Wert")
    print(" -> Beenden mit STRG+C")
    print("="*60 + "\n")

    values = ir_sensors.sensor_values
    normalized_values =ir_sensors._normalize(values)
    min_values = values.copy()
    max_values = values.copy()
    min_normalized_values = normalized_values.copy()
    max_normalized_values = normalized_values.copy()
            
    all_values = [values]

    try:
        while True:
            ir_sensors._cfg._load_config_file()
            # 1. Aktuelle Werte holen (Passe den Methoden-Namen an euren Code an)
            values = ir_sensors.sensor_values
            normalized_values =ir_sensors._normalize(values)
            all_values.append(values)

            assign_smaller = lambda x,y: x if x < y else y
            assign_larger = lambda x,y: x if x > y else y
            for i in range(len(values)):
                min_values[i] = assign_smaller(values[i], min_values[i])
                max_values[i] = assign_larger(values[i], max_values[i])
                min_normalized_values[i] = assign_smaller(normalized_values[i], min_normalized_values[i])
                max_normalized_values[i] = assign_larger(normalized_values[i], max_normalized_values[i])

            # 2. Das Delta berechnen (Höchster Wert minus niedrigster Wert in diesem Moment)
            min_val = min(values)
            max_val = max(values)
            min_normalized_val = min(normalized_values)
            max_normalized_val = max(normalized_values)
            delta = max_val - min_val
            delta_normalized = min_normalized_val - max_normalized_val
            
            # 3. Den String schön formatieren (Feste Breite mit :4d sorgt dafür, 
            #    dass die Zahlen nicht wackeln)
            ausgabe = (
                f"  Live: "
                f"S1:[{values[0]:4d}]  S2:[{values[1]:4d}]  S3:[{values[2]:4d}]  "
                f"S4:[{values[3]:4d}]  S5:[{values[4]:4d}] "
                f" |  DELTA: {delta:4d} "
            )

            # 4. Visuelles Ampel-Feedback für das Team hinzufügen
            if delta < 50:
                ausgabe += "🔴 (Zu schwach - weiter drehen!)"
            elif delta < 150:
                ausgabe += "🟡 (Besser, aber geht da noch was?)"
            else:
                ausgabe += "🟢 (PERFEKT! Finger weg vom Poti!)"
                
            # \r springt an den Zeilenanfang, \033[K löscht den Rest der Zeile
            print(f"\r{ausgabe}", end="\033[K", flush=True)

            ausgabe2 = (
                f"  Norm: "
                f"S1:[{normalized_values[0]:4.2f}]  S2:[{normalized_values[1]:4.2f}]  S3:[{normalized_values[2]:4.2f}]  "
                f"S4:[{normalized_values[3]:4.2f}]  S5:[{normalized_values[4]:4.2f}] "
                f" |  DELTA: {delta_normalized:4.2f} "
            )
            # \033[F springt eine Zeile hoch. Da wir 2 Zeilen ausgeben, springen wir 
            # am Anfang JEDES Durchlaufs erst mal eine Zeile hoch, um die alten Daten sauber zu überschreiben.
            print(f"\033[F\r{ausgabe}\033[K\n\r{ausgabe2}\033[K", end="", flush=True)            
            # Kurze Pause, damit man die Zahlen noch lesen kann
            time.sleep(0.1)
            
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        # Fängt STRG+C ab, damit das Skript nicht mit einem roten Fehler abstürzt
        print("\n\n ✅ Tuning beendet. Ihr könnt jetzt die Kalibrierung starten!")    
    except Exception as e:
        print("\n\n")
        raise e
    
    return min_values, max_values, all_values

def do_funny_stats(min_values:list[float], max_values:list[float], all_values:list[list[float]]):
        print(f"\nAusgelesene min values {min_values}")
        print(f"Ausgelesene max values {max_values}")
        print(f"\nsensor_min_values (dynamisch kalibrierte Werte): {ir.sensor_min_values}")
        print(f"sensor_max_values (dynamisch kalibrierte Werte): {ir.sensor_max_values}")

        for i in range(2):
            print(f"\nMathematics on axis {i}: mean: {np.mean(all_values, axis=i)}, min: {np.min(all_values, axis=i)}, max: {np.max(all_values, axis=i)}")    



if __name__ == '__main__':


    
    log_format = logging.Formatter('%(filename)s:%(lineno)d]: %(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s')

    logger = logging.getLogger("run_ir_sensors")
    logger.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)
        
    # Handler hinzufügen, falls noch keiner existiert (verhindert doppelte Logs)
    file_handler = TimedRotatingFileHandler(
        filename="run_ir_sensors.log",
        when = "m",  # LogFile vergisst Einträge älter als eine Minute 
        interval=5, 
        encoding="utf-8")
    file_handler.setFormatter(log_format)
    logger.addHandler(file_handler)
            
    stop_event = threading.Event()

    sc = SensorCar()
    ir = InfraredSensor(car=sc, logger=logger)


    while True:     
        min_values, max_values, all_values = (None, None, None)
        try:
            mode = int(input("Welchen Modus ausführen? <1> = Poti-Tuning-Tools, <2> = Test dynamische Kalibierung, <3> zuletzt ausgelesene Werte in cfg speichern, <andere Taste> = beenden"))

            if (mode == 1):
                os.system('clear')
                min_values, max_values, all_values = starte_poti_tuning(ir)
                do_funny_stats(min_values, max_values, all_values)
            elif (mode == 2):
                os.system('clear')
            elif (mode == 3):
                if (min_values is None or max_values is None or all_values is None):
                    print("Bitte zuerst eine Funktion ausführen, um Werte auszulesen")
                else:
                    ir._cfg.set_config("sensor_min_values", min_values)
                    ir._cfg.set_config("sensor_max_values", max_values)
                    ir._cfg._save_config()
                    print("Config-Datei aktualisiert")

            input("<ENTER>")
            os.system('clear')

        except ValueError:
            print("IR-Sensor-Utils beendet")
            break



    # ir_sensor_thread = threading.Thread(target=ir.read_loop, args=[stop_event])
    
    # min_values = []
    # max_values = []
    # all_values = []

    # try:
    #     ir_sensor_thread.start()
    #     values = ir.sensor_values
    #     min_values = values
    #     max_values = values
    #     all_values = [values]
    #     normalized_values = [ir._normalize(values)]

    #     while not stop_event.is_set():
    #         values = ir.sensor_values
    #         all_values.append(values)
    #         normalized_values.append(ir._normalize(values))

    #         time.sleep(0.1)

    #         print(f"Ausgelesene Sensor-Werte: {values}", end="\r", flush=True)
    #         for i in range(len(values)):
    #             min_values[i] = values[i] if values[i] < min_values[i] else min_values[i]
    #             max_values[i] = values[i] if values[i] > max_values[i] else max_values[i]

    # except KeyboardInterrupt: 
    #     stop_event.set()
    #     print("Stopp!")

    #     print(f"Ausgelesene min values {min_values}")
    #     print(f"Ausgelesene max values {max_values}")
    #     print(f"sensor_min_values (dynamisch kalibrierte Werte): {ir.sensor_min_values}")
